### [1] [Cooperative adsorption and diffusion trapping induced by AlF3 intercalation in graphite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05305)
*H. Betancourt-Infante,G. Ruano,F. Bonetto,S. J. Rodríguez-Sotelo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本论文通过密度泛函理论（DFT-D3）第一性原理计算，揭示了AlF3在石墨中的插层与表面吸附之间的微观耦合机制，解释了实验中观察到的两步自限性吸附现象，并建立了插层诱导变形与石墨结构-性能关系之间的定量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨中分子插层对其作为碳基电极材料的性能至关重要，但亚表面插层与表面吸附之间的微观耦合机制尚不清楚；该研究旨在从微观层面解释近期实验中观察到的两步自限性吸附（self-limiting sorption）现象的起因。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT-D3）进行第一性原理计算，对比纯净石墨与含有亚表面插层分子的石墨表面，分析覆盖度相关的吸附能量学、扩散势垒计算、电荷密度差分（charge-density difference）分析以及Mulliken布居分析（Mulliken population analysis）。

Result: 单个插层AlF3分子可通过泡状表面变形（blister-like surface deformation）局部改变石墨的结构、电子性质和扩散行为；吸附能量从排斥性侧向相互作用转变为泡上方的合作结合，源于局部曲率和插层诱导的电荷重分布；泡同时作为动力学陷阱，提高扩散势垒，将表面迁移从准无势垒转变为热激活机制；插层物作为稳定的电子储库，加深表面势景观并使吸附物种动力学固定化。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了插层诱导变形下石墨的结构-性能关系（structure-property relationship），为控制碳基储能和转换系统中的插层效率提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Graphite's structural and electronic response to molecular intercalation is central to its performance as a carbon-based electrode material, yet the microscopic coupling between subsurface intercalation and surface adsorption remains poorly understood. We present a first-principles investigation of AlF3 adsorption and intercalation in graphite to explain the microscopic origin of a recently observed two-step self-limiting sorption mechanism. Using density functional theory (DFT-D3), we show that a single intercalated AlF3 molecule locally transforms the structure, electronic properties, and diffusion behavior of graphite through a blister-like surface deformation. Comparing pristine graphite with a graphite surface containing a subsurface intercalated molecule, coverage-dependent adsorption energetics reveal a crossover from repulsive lateral interactions to cooperative binding above the blister, driven by local curvature and intercalation-induced charge redistribution. Diffusion-barrier calculations show that the blister simultaneously acts as a kinetic trap, raising diffusion barriers and transitioning surface mobility from a quasi-barrierless to a thermally activated regime. Charge-density difference and Mulliken population analyses identify the intercalant as a stable electronic reservoir that deepens the surface potential landscape, kinetically immobilizing adsorbed species. Together, these results establish a structure-property relationship for intercalation-induced deformation in graphite, offering a quantitative framework for controlling intercalation efficiency in carbon-based energy storage and conversion systems.

</details>


### [2] [Nitrogen Vacancy Centers in Hexagonal Diamond Exhibit Long Coherence Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05320)
*Gabriel Kumar,Siyuan Chen,Victor Wen-zhe Yu,Giulia Galli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究表明，六方金刚石多晶型六方碳（lonsdaleite）中带负电的氮空位（NV）中心（negatively charged nitrogen-vacancy centers）可作为自旋量子比特（spin qubits）的候选体系，其相干性优于立方金刚石中的对应体系。通过第一性原理计算，研究了两类缺陷构型（AA与AB），发现AB构型在零磁场下具有有限的横向零场劈裂（transverse zero-field splitting），使得Hahn-echo相干时间$T_2$约增强四倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方金刚石中的NV中心是量子传感与量子信息领域的重要体系，但其相干时间受环境噪声限制。寻找具有增强相干性的替代宿主材料，是推动量子技术发展的关键方向。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），考察六方碳中NV中心的两种不同缺陷构型：AA构型（与立方金刚石中对称性相同）和AB构型（对称性降低）。系统表征了两种构型的多体电子结构（many-body electronic structure）、垂直激发能（vertical excitation energies）以及光致发光谱（photoluminescence spectra）。

Result: AB构型NV中心展现出有限的横向零场劈裂，在零磁场下其Hahn-echo相干时间$T_2$约为立方金刚石NV中心的四倍；而AA构型的电子结构与相干性质则与立方NV中心高度一致。两种构型提供了可区分的谱学指纹特征。

Conclusion: 对称性破缺的六方碳NV中心是量子传感与量子信息科学的潜在候选材料，为设计具有增强相干性的自旋量子比特提供了新途径。

Abstract: We show that negatively charged nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in the hexagonal diamond polymorph lonsdaleite offer a route to spin qubits with enhanced coherence relative to their cubic-diamond counterparts. Using first-principles calculations, we examine two distinct defect configurations, AA, with the same symmetry as in cubic diamond and AB, with reduced symmetry. We find that the AB configuration of the NV center exhibits a finite transverse zero-field splitting, giving rise to an approximate fourfold enhancement of the Hahn-echo coherence time $T_2$ at zero magnetic field. The AA configuration, by contrast, closely reproduces the electronic structure and coherence properties of the cubic NV center. We further characterize the many-body electronic structure, vertical excitation energies, and photoluminescence spectra of both configurations, providing spectral fingerprints for their experimental identification. Our results establish symmetry-broken NV centers in lonsdaleite as promising candidates for quantum sensing and information science applications.

</details>


### [3] [Fine-Tuning Small Language Models for Reliable VASP INCAR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05387)
*Xinyue Zhang,Jixiang Li,Bin Shao,Baishun Yang,Zhiyang Liu,Weichao Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 INCAR-SLM，一个基于 Qwen3-4B 微调的小语言模型（SLM），并搭配确定性后处理器 VASPGuard，用于从自然语言请求生成 VASP INCAR 文件。在 INCARBench 基准测试中，INCAR-SLM 在 100 分制的 INCAR Score 上超越所有通用大语言模型（LLM），比 GPT-5.4 高出 15.55 分。研究发现模型规模的影响小于预期，性能在数十亿参数量级即趋饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型专有云模型虽然能够处理 VASP INCAR 文件的生成，但其依赖云服务的方式与本地、高通量材料工作流（high-throughput materials workflows）中对隐私、成本和离线部署的需求不匹配，因此需要探索小语言模型能否弥合这一差距。

Method: 采用参考 VASP 计算数据对小型语言模型 Qwen3-4B 进行微调（fine-tune），并设计确定性后处理器 VASPGuard 检查语法、工作流及材料相关约束；微调模型与 VASPGuard 的组合被命名为 INCAR-SLM，并在 INCARBench 基准上进行评估。

Result: INCAR-SLM 在 INCARBench 上以 100 分制的 INCAR Score 超越所有被评估的通用 LLM，比 GPT-5.4 高出 15.55 分。性能增益主要来自微调，VASPGuard 则负责纠正剩余的误差；Qwen3-4B 还优于同系列中更大的模型，表明性能在数十亿参数规模即趋饱和。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型经过微调和确定性后处理，能够在 VASP INCAR 生成任务上超过大型专有云模型，且模型规模的影响小于预期，为本地化、离线、注重隐私的高通量材料工作流提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Language models can prepare VASP INCAR files from natural-language requests, but so far only large proprietary cloud models come close to handling the tightly coupled, physics-sensitive settings reliably, a dependence that fits poorly with local, high-throughput materials workflows where privacy, cost, and offline deployment matter. We show that a small language model (SLM) can close this gap. The SLM is fine-tuned on reference VASP calculations and paired with VASPGuard, a deterministic post-processor that checks syntax, workflow, and material-dependent constraints; we call the combined model INCAR-SLM. On INCARBench, a benchmark for VASP INCAR generation, INCAR-SLM built on Qwen3-4B outperforms every general-purpose LLM evaluated, exceeding GPT-5.4 by 15.55 points on the 100-point INCAR Score. Most of this gain comes from fine-tuning, with VASPGuard correcting the errors that remain. We further find that model size matters less than expected: once fine-tuning and post-processing are applied, performance saturates at a few billion parameters, and Qwen3-4B outperforms larger models in the same family.

</details>


### [4] [Low-resistivity nitrogen-doped p-type Cu2O thin films enabled by millisecond flash lamp annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05452)
*Jan Koloros,Pavel Baroch,Thomas Preußner,Matthias Fahland,Michaela Červená,Jiří Rezek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究探究了闪光灯退火（flash lamp annealing, FLA）对反应溅射沉积的p型Cu2O:N薄膜的影响，发现低能量密度（4.9 J cm-2）的FLA能显著降低电阻率至0.045 Ωcm，而高能量密度则会提高电阻率、拓宽光学带隙但削弱电导性能，界定了对电学性能有利的窄低能量加工窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闪光灯退火（FLA）能提供毫秒级热加工，但其对p型Cu2O及氮相关缺陷的影响尚不清楚，需要系统评估。

Method: 采用反应高功率脉冲磁控溅射（reactive high-power impulse magnetron sputtering）沉积不同氮含量的Cu2O:N薄膜，随后施加4.9-11.7 J cm-2的单次1.9 ms FLA脉冲，并通过组分、形貌、结构、振动、电学及光学等手段进行表征。

Result: WDS显示总元素组成无显著变化，XRD确认立方Cu2O保留；含氮薄膜出现表面粗化、Cu2O衍射峰位移及N2拉曼峰的非单调变化。氮掺入大幅降低沉积态电阻率，4.9 J cm-2 FLA后获得0.045 Ωcm的最低值，而更高能量密度显著增加电阻率。霍尔测量显示迁移率上升但空穴浓度下降，高能量密度下富氮薄膜光学带隙变宽。

Conclusion: 低能量FLA存在一个窄窗口可改善电学性能，而高能量FLA虽改变结构与光学吸收边，但会劣化电导。

Abstract: Flash lamp annealing (FLA) provides millisecond-scale thermal processing, but its effects on p-type Cu2O and nitrogen-related defects remain poorly understood. Reactively sputtered Cu2O:N films with different nitrogen content were deposited using reactive high-power impulse magnetron sputtering and exposed to a single 1.9 ms FLA pulse at 4.9 - 11.7 J cm-2. Their compositional,morphological, structural, vibrational, electrical, and optical responses were evaluated. WDS showed no statistically significant change in total elemental composition, and XRD confirmed retention of cubic Cu2O. Nitrogen-containing films exhibited surface coarsening, shifts of the Cu2O reflections, and non-monotonic changes in the Raman band assigned to molecular N2. Nitrogen incorporation substantially reduced the as-deposited resistivity. The very low value of 0.045 Ωcm was obtained after FLA at 4.9 J cm-2, whereas higher energy densities markedly increased resistivity. Hall measurements showed increasing mobility but decreasing hole concentration. At high energy densities, the optical band gap of nitrogen-rich films widened. The results define a narrow low-energy processing window with a positive effect on electrical properties, whereas high-energy FLA modifies the structure and optical absorption edge but degrades electrical conductivity.

</details>


### [5] [Antiferromagnetic Phases in Zr-Fe-Ge Kagome Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05469)
*Peter Minch,Shiya Chen,Weiyi Xia,Wei-Shen Tee,Yang Sun,Cai-Zhuang Wang,Vladimir Antropov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理（first-principles）计算研究了铁磁性Kagome化合物ZrFe6Ge6、ZrFe6Ge4和ZrFe6Ge5的电子结构与磁有序。结果显示ZrFe6Ge6基态为A型反铁磁（A-type antiferromagnetic，AFM），与实验一致；ZrFe6Ge4和ZrFe6Ge5的基态为具有长周期有序的共线A型双层反铁磁结构，且此类长程磁结构是普遍特征而非特定替代所特有。磁性矩具有高度局域性且对磁构型依赖弱，与实验估计吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁性Kagome体系中的化学替代可产生多样的磁相，其电子结构适用于拓扑或量子材料研究。本文旨在通过第一性原理计算系统研究三种Kagome化合物的电子结构和磁有序，以预测其基态磁结构并揭示长程磁有序形成的普遍规律。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）方法，对ZrFe6Ge6、ZrFe6Ge4和ZrFe6Ge5三种磁性Kagome化合物进行电子结构和磁有序性计算，比较不同磁构型下的能量确定基态结构。

Result: ZrFe6Ge6的基态为A型反铁磁结构，与实验一致；ZrFe6Ge4和ZrFe6Ge5的基态被预测为共线A型双层反铁磁结构，具有包含铁磁（FM）与反铁磁（AFM）层间取向混合的长周期有序。长程磁结构的形成是普遍特征，与特定化学替代无关。磁性矩高度局域化且对磁构型依赖性弱，与实验估计的幅值一致。

Conclusion: 长周期磁有序是Kagome体系中的普遍现象，不依赖于特定化学替代。由于计算预测与实验估计吻合良好，中子散射（neutron scattering）实验对于直接验证这些预测尤为重要。

Abstract: A wide variety of chemical substitutions in ferromagnetic Kagome systems can lead to diverse magnetic phases with electronic structures suitable for topological or quantum material properties. Here, we study the electronic structure and magnetic orderings using first-principles calculations for the magnetic Kagome compounds ZrFe6Ge6, ZrFe6Ge4, and ZrFe6Ge5. For ZrFe6Ge6, the obtained ground-state magnetic structure is A-type antiferromagnetic (AFM), in agreement with existing experiments. We predicted that the magnetic ground states of ZrFe6Ge4 and ZrFe6Ge5 are collinear A-type bilayer AFM structures with long-period ordering that involves a mix of FM and AFM interlayer orientations. The formation of such long-range magnetic structures appears to be a general feature and is not tied to specific substitutions. The magnetic moments in these systems are largely local and only weakly dependent on the magnetic configuration, with magnitudes in good agreement with available experimental estimates. Neutron scattering experiments, which could provide direct verification of these predictions, are therefore of particular importance.

</details>


### [6] [Intra-unit-cell resolved intertwining of multi-$Q$ charge and spin textures in an itinerant skyrmion magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05528)
*Christopher J. Butler,Katsuki Nihongi,Haruto Yoshimochi,Nguyen Duy Khanh,Rina Takagi,Tetsuo Hanaguri,Shinichiro Seki*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope, STM）对GdRu₂Ge₂五个连续磁相（含两个纳米尺度斯格明子晶体相）的原子级观测，揭示了Gd 4f自旋的多-Q（multi-Q）磁性排列与Ru 4d巡游电子轨道多-Q电子织构之间的对应关系，并推断出最近邻Gd自旋排列与Ru 4d轨道局域态密度之间的紧密关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称晶体中非共线磁性的稳定机制尚不清楚，可能涉及巡游电子介导的自旋-自旋相互作用（如RKKY相互作用），且有限波矢Q的磁有序可能伴随可通过STM观测的电子调制，因此需要从原子尺度阐明电荷与自旋自由度之间的交织关系。

Method: 在GdRu₂Ge₂的五个连续磁相中，利用原子分辨的扫描隧道显微镜成像获取每种电子织构的基元图像；同时构建基于底层自旋结构的简单数值建模方案，用于推断Gd 4f自旋排列与Ru 4d轨道电子态之间的定量关系。

Result: 研究发现Gd 4f自旋的多-Q磁性必然对应Ru 4d巡游电子带的多-Q电子织构，且最近邻Gd自旋之间的相对排列与Gd方格子两个键中心亚晶格上Ru 4d轨道的局域态密度紧密相关。

Conclusion: 这些分析为理解非共线巡游磁体中电荷与自旋自由度在原子尺度上的交织提供了微观视角，有助于阐明中心对称晶体中非共线磁性（包括纳米尺度斯格明子晶体）的稳定机制。

Abstract: The mechanisms stabilizing non-collinear magnetism in centrosymmetric crystals remain unclear, but likely involve spin-spin interactions mediated by itinerant electrons, such as the RKKY interaction. Finite-$Q$ magnetic order may then be accompanied by electronic modulations that are observable using a scanning tunneling microscope. In five successive magnetic phases of GdRu$_{2}$Ge$_{2}$, including two nano-scale skyrmion crystal phases, we show that multi-$Q$ magnetism among Gd 4$f$ spins entails a corresponding multi-$Q$ texture among the Ru 4$d$ orbitals that contribute itinerant electron bands. With atomically-resolved images of each electronic texture's motif, and a simple numerical modeling scheme drawing on the underlying spin structures, we infer their key relationship: The alignment between nearest-neighbor Gd spins tightly correlates with the local density-of-states of the Ru 4$d$ orbitals on the two bond-centered sublattices of the Gd square net. These analyses offer a microscopic view of the atomic-scale intertwining of charge and spin degrees-of-freedom in non-collinear itinerant magnets.

</details>


### [7] [Emergent Surface Altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05529)
*Yuzhong Hu,Pan Zhou,Baoru Pan,Songmin Liu,Binchang Zhou,Lizhong Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出表面交替磁（surface altermagnetism, SAM）概念，建立从体态自旋群到表面自旋群的对称性理论框架，识别出35种PT对称反铁磁和61种体态交替磁的对称性破缺表面，并通过紧束缚模型和第一性原理计算在NaMnP、LiMnAs和CrSb等材料中验证了该现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对交替磁（altermagnetism）的研究主要集中在磁性材料的自旋极化体态电子态上，表面层面的交替磁现象尚未被探索。作者旨在将交替磁现象拓展至材料表面，为无外场的自旋操控提供新的物理基础。

Method: 作者构建了系统的对称性理论框架，将体态自旋群与表面自旋群系统地联系起来，适用于共线反铁磁（collinear AFM）和交替磁两类体系。通过对称性分析识别所有能支持SAM的对称性破缺表面，并利用紧束缚模型和第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）对框架进行验证。

Result: 对称性分析识别出35种PT对称反铁磁和61种体态交替磁的SAM支持表面；此外发现203个共线自旋空间群（collinear spin space groups）——其中100个不含[C₂‖P]操作、103个含该操作——允许在打破分数平移对称性的tT对称反铁磁表面上出现SAM。该框架在NaMnP、LiMnAs和CrSb等代表性化合物中得到实际材料验证。

Conclusion: 研究确立了SAM作为一种稳健的、受对称性保护的磁性态，将交替磁现象扩展至材料表面，为下一代自旋电子学（spintronics）技术中先进的无场自旋操控铺平了道路。

Abstract: Research on altermagnetism has thus far primarily focused on spin-polarized bulk electronic states in magnetic materials. In this work, we advance the field by introducing the concept of surface altermagnetism (SAM), wherein altermagnetic spin polarization emerges at the surfaces of collinear antiferromagnets (AFMs) or altermagnets (AMs). To lay the theoretical groundwork for this phenomenon, we construct a thorough symmetry-based framework that systematically connects bulk spin groups to surface spin groups for both types of systems.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机金属层状材料（organometallic layered materials）虽被视为有前景的氧还原与氧析出反应（oxygen reduction and evolution reactions）单原子催化剂，但其电化学稳定性不足严重限制了实际应用，亟需澄清活性与稳定性之间的内在权衡关系。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算，对比石墨烯嵌入的MN₄结构、酞菁类（phthalocyanine-like）框架以及MO₄配位框架（包括M₄(OHPTP)₂和M₃(HHTP)₂，M = Mn, Fe, Co, Ni, Cu, Zn）。稳定性通过表面Pourbaix分析评估，活性采用计算氢电极（computational hydrogen electrode）方法评价。

Result: MN₄体系虽表现出具有竞争力的过电位（overpotential），但存在强烈的pH依赖性不稳定性；相反，MO₄框架在宽pH范围内表现出增强的稳健性，同时保持良好催化性能。提出的稳定性描述符可实现跨体系直接比较，确定M₄(OHPTP)₂（M = Zn, Co）为活性与稳定性平衡的最优候选。

Conclusion: MO₄配位结构，特别是M₄(OHPTP)₂（M = Zn, Co），有望作为有机金属单原子催化剂中兼顾催化活性与电化学稳定性、适用于实际电催化的最优构型。

Abstract: Organometallic layered materials have emerged as promising single-atom catalysts for oxygen reduction and evolution reactions, but their practical use has been limited by insufficient electrochemical stability. Here, we present a density functional theory study clarifying the relationship between catalytic activity and stability in organometallic single-atom catalysts with metal-N$_4$ (MN$_4$) and metal-O$_4$ (MO$_4$) coordination. We compare graphene-embedded MN$_4$ motif and phthalocyanine-like frameworks with MO$_4$-coordination frameworks, including M$_4$(OHPTP)$_2$ and M$_3$(HHTP)$_2$ (M = Mn, Fe, Co, Ni, Cu, Zn). Stability is assessed by surface Pourbaix analysis, while activity is evaluated using the computational hydrogen electrode method. MN$_4$ systems show competitive overpotentials but suffer strong pH-dependent instability. In contrast, MO$_4$ frameworks exhibit enhanced robustness across wide pH ranges while maintaining good catalytic performance. A proposed stability descriptor enables direct comparison across systems, identifying MO$_4$ coordination structures, particularly M$_4$(OHPTP)$_2$ (M = Zn, Co) as optimal for balancing activity and stability in practical electrocatalysis.

</details>

### [16] [Search for resonant production of lepton-enriched semivisible jets in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05323)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究利用CMS探测器在CERN LHC收集的138 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据（√s=13 TeV），搜索由强耦合暗区（dark sector）产生的富含轻子的半可见喷注（semivisible jets, SVJs）的共振产生。研究涵盖两种信号场景（全部轻子味SVJℓ与τ轻子富集SVJτ），采用图神经网络（graph neural network）与全连接神经网络的双机器学习策略提升信号灵敏度。结果显示在SVJℓ场景中介子质量最高4.7 TeV被95%置信度水平排除，SVJτ场景排除1.8至3.5 TeV质量区间，这是对富含轻子SVJs的首个实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强耦合暗区可能产生的半可见喷注共振信号，检验包含不稳定暗强子（dark hadrons）与Z'介子的理论模型。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在LHC收集的138 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据（√s=13 TeV），针对缺失横动量（missing transverse momentum）与非隔离轻子喷注重合的末态进行分析。采用双重机器学习策略：图神经网络用于喷注识别，全连接神经网络结合喷注级与事件级信息以增强信号灵敏度并改进背景估计。信号模型假设重型Z'介子以0.25的基准耦合（benchmark coupling）耦合到标准模型夸克，并伴有不稳定暗强子的瞬发衰变。

Result: 在SVJℓ场景中，介子质量最高4.7 TeV在95%置信水平下被排除；在SVJτ场景中，1.8至3.5 TeV间的质量区间被排除。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了对富含轻子的半可见喷注的首个实验约束，为强耦合暗区的探测设定了新的质量限制。

Abstract: This search targets the resonant production of lepton-enriched semivisible jets (SVJs) from a strongly coupled dark sector, using 138 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data collected with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV. Two scenarios are investigated: jets enriched in all lepton flavors (SVJ$\ell$ signature) and jets predominantly enriched in tau leptons (SVJ$τ$ signature). The analysis focuses on final states in which the missing transverse momentum is aligned with jets containing nonisolated leptons. A dual machine-learning strategy is employed, using a graph neural network for jet identification and a fully connected neural network that combines jet- and event-level information to enhance signal sensitivity and background estimation. The signal models assume a heavy Z' mediator with a benchmark coupling of 0.25 to standard model quarks, together with prompt decays of unstable dark hadrons. In the SVJ$\ell$ scenario, mediator masses up to 4.7 TeV are excluded at 95% confidence level, while masses between 1.8 and 3.5 TeV are excluded in the SVJ$τ$ scenario. These results provide the first experimental constraints on lepton-enriched semivisible jets.

</details>


### [17] [Spin Physics at NICA SPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05772)
*Igor Denisenko*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了位于俄罗斯杜布纳联合核子研究所（JINR）NICA对撞机上的自旋物理探测器（SPD）——一种通用探测器，旨在利用最高27 GeV碰撞能量和10^32 cm^-2 s^-1亮度的极化质子与氘核束流，研究质子与氘核的自旋结构及相关自旋现象，特别是通过粲偶素（charmonia）、开粲（open charm）和瞬发光子（prompt photon）产生过程中的单双自旋不对称测量，获取胶子横动量依赖部分子分布函数（TMD PDFs）、胶子横向性分布（gluon transversity）及氘核张量PDF等信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究质子与氘核的自旋结构和相关自旋现象，特别是获取质子与氘核中的胶子TMD PDF、胶子横向性分布以及氘核张量PDF，这些是目前量子色动力学（QCD）中尚未被充分测量的关键信息。

Method: 利用NICA对撞机上SPD设备独有的极化质子与氘核束流能力，通过测量相关单自旋和双自旋不对称性（single and double spin asymmetries），使用粲偶素、开粲和瞬发光子产生过程等多种互补探针来实现目标；同时在NICA初期阶段（低亮度、低碰撞能量）开展其他偏振与未偏振物理研究。

Result: 本文呈现了SPD的物理研究计划和SPD装置设计，该实验能够在最高27 GeV碰撞能量和10^32 cm^-2 s^-1亮度下运行。

Conclusion: SPD作为NICA对撞机上的通用探测器，其物理计划涵盖从胶子TMD PDF等核心目标到初期阶段的其他偏振与未偏振物理研究，具有广泛的科学潜力。

Abstract: The Spin Physics Detector (SPD) is a universal detector at the NICA collider under commissioning at JINR, Dubna. The SPD is intended to study the spin structure of the proton and deuteron and other spin-related phenomena using a unique possibility to operate with polarized proton and deuteron beams at a collision energies up to 27~GeV and a luminosity up to 10$^{32}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$. As the primary goal, the experiment aims to provide access to the gluon TMD PDFs in the proton and deuteron, as well as the gluon transversity distribution and tensor PDFs in the deuteron, via the measurements of relevant single and double spin asymmetries using different complementary probes such as charmonia, open charm, and prompt photon production processes. Other polarized and unpolarized physics studies are possible, especially at the first stage of NICA operation with reduced luminosity and collision energy of the proton and ion beams. The physics program of the SPD and the design of the SPD setup will be presented.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器收集的约899.8万个J/ψ事件，对η'→eμ这一带电轻子味破坏（charged lepton flavour violation）衰变进行了搜索，未观察到显著信号，并在90%置信水平下将其分支比上限设定为6.3×10⁻⁷，比此前最优结果改进了近三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电轻子味破坏（charged lepton flavour violation）是超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的新物理的重要探针，η'→eμ衰变过程的研究可为检验轻子味守恒提供严格约束。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在质心能量√s=3.097 GeV的e⁺e⁻对撞中采集到的(8998±40)×10⁶个J/ψ事件，通过J/ψ→γη'的反应道搜索η'→eμ衰变信号。

Result: 未观察到显著信号，在90%置信水平下将该衰变的分支比上限设定为6.3×10⁻⁷，将此前最佳结果改善了近三个数量级。

Conclusion: 该搜索对η'→eμ带电轻子味破坏衰变给出了更为严格的分支比上限，显著提升了对此类新物理过程的实验约束能力。

Abstract: Based on $(8998\pm40)\times10^6$ $J/ψ$ events collected in $e^+e^-$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 3.097$ GeV with the BESIII detector, we present a search for the charged lepton flavour violating decay $η'\to eμ$ with $J/ψ\toγη'$. No significant signal is observed, and an upper limit on its decay branching fraction is set to be $6.3\times10^{-7}$ at the 90% confidence level, improving the previous best result by nearly three orders of magnitude.

</details>

Abstract: Lorentz symmetry and gauge symmetry constitute the mathematical cornerstones of modern physics, yet their ultimate physical origins remain elusive. From the standpoint of a constructive interpretation, this paper demonstrates that both symmetry structures can emerge isomorphically from a unified classical source: dynamical constraints on wave-packet excitations in a continuous elastic substrate medium (SM). Neither symmetry is posited as fundamental, nor does their emergence rely on quantization. Three core results are established. First, taking the transverse wave speed of a homogeneous, isotropic SM as the benchmark under a conventionalist synchronization scheme, Minkowski-type spacetime arises isomorphically, with Lorentz transformations as effective coordinate transformations between inertial frames. This reinterprets the Michelson--Morley null result: observers are composite wave-packet excitations, and medium-induced kinematic corrections are encoded in their measurement frameworks. We show that a deformation $β$ of order the meson mass or larger is sufficient to tame this divergence, although it nonetheless results in an excitation probability which is enhanced by a $β$-dependent fractional power of the inverse coupling.

Result: 导出了三层系统的哈密顿形式和以DN算子表达的运动方程，得到双三次色散关系，其六个实数解（三个正、对应右行波，三个负、对应左行波）的幅值大小不同阶，并给出了这些根的上下界及传播速度的近似公式。刚性盖模型下也获得了类似的DN算子表述、线性方程和Boussinesq近似的弱非线性方程，并能够回到两层自由表面模型的极限情形。

Conclusion: 基于DN算子的哈密顿表述为三层分层流体系统提供了一套统一而系统的理论工具，能够覆盖从线性色散关系到弱非线性传播的完整层次。所得结果适用于湖泊和海洋中的内波研究，以及三层叠加流体层的实验室实验。

Abstract: The object of investigation in this paper are the nonlinear equations of motion for two-dimensional inviscid water flows with piecewise constant density stratification in a three-layer fluid with a flat bottom, a free surface and two interfaces. We establish a Hamiltonian formulation for the nonlinear governing equations in this setup. The Hamiltonian of the system and the equations of motion of the surface and of the interfaces are expressed with the help of the Dirichlet-Neumann (DN) operators, which are introduced for each of the layers. Then, the linear equations for small amplitudes of the elevation of the surface and of the interfaces in the leading order are derived from which a bi-cubic equation for the dispersion relation is obtained, whose solutions are analysed. The six real solutions for the possible propagation speeds (three positive, related to right-moving waves and three negative, related to left-moving waves) have magnitudes of different order. Upper and lower bounds for the previously mentioned roots are also given in terms of the coefficients of the equation. Subsequently, approximate formulae for the propagation speeds are derived. The importance of the DN operators is further illustrated in a separate analysis of the three-layer model with flat surface (rigid lid). The full nonlinear evolution equations are expressed again in terms of the DN operators, and the equations in the linear regime and the weakly nonlinear propagation regime (the Boussinesq approximation) are derived by a proper expansion of the DN operators. Limits to the two-layer free surface model are obtained as well. The obtained results are applicable to internal waves in lakes and in the ocean as well as to laboratory experiments with three superimposed fluid layers.

</details>

### [62] [The Structure of Spreading on Temporal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06175)
*Omar Henderson,Mikko Kivelä,Márton Karsai*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明时序网络（temporal networks）上的传播动力学可类比静态网络中的渗流（percolation）映射，将其对应到时序事件图（temporal event graphs）中的可达性问题（reachability），从而无需显式模拟即可通过事件图的连通分量分析（component analysis）获得流行病流行率和流行阈值（epidemic thresholds），且计算效率显著优于显式模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态网络中的传播物理机制已通过映射到渗流得到充分理解，但时序网络上的传播动力学尚缺乏与之对应的理论分析框架，现有方法往往依赖大量显式模拟，计算成本高昂。

Method: 作者将时序网络上的传播动力学映射为时序事件图中的可达性问题，建立了一套面向易感-感染-易感（susceptible-infected-susceptible, SIS）模型变体等一类过程的普适理论与计算框架；通过对事件图进行连通分量分析，可在无需显式模拟的情况下提取流行病流行率并推导流行阈值，适用于任意度分布（degree distribution）和事件间隔时间分布（inter-event time distribution）的时序网络。

Result: 该方法能够在不进行显式模拟的前提下，通过对事件图的连通分量分析直接获得流行病流行率并推导出时序网络的流行病阈值，同时在不同度分布和事件间隔时间分布条件下均适用，并展现出相比显式模拟显著的（significant）计算优势。

Conclusion: 时序网络上的传播动力学可被精确映射为事件图中的可达性问题，这一框架在理论和计算两个层面为传播过程分析提供了统一且高效的工具，大幅降低了计算开销。

Abstract: The physics of spreading in static networks is well understood through mappings to percolation. We show that spreading dynamics on temporal networks can analogously be mapped to reachability in temporal event graphs. This provides a theoretical and computational framework for a class of processes, such as variants of the susceptible-infected-susceptible model. Without explicit simulations, through the component analysis of event graphs, we obtain epidemic prevalence and derive epidemic thresholds for temporal networks with arbitrary degree and inter-event time distributions, with significant computational advantages as compared to explicit simulations.

</details>

### [63] [Multiphysics tritium transport modelling of the ARC breeding blanket with FESTIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05398)
*James Dark,Arpan Sircar,Jin Whan Bae,Jørgen S. Dokken,Rémi Delaporte-Mathurin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全开源的、组件尺度的多物理场框架，用于模拟ARC级聚变反应堆液态浸没增殖包层中的氚输运行为，耦合了中子输运、热工水力和氢同位素输运三个物理过程，稳态模拟预测总氚库存约243 mg，稳态氚通量约30分钟即可达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熔盐增殖包层中氚行为的精确预测对ARC级聚变反应堆的设计和安全运行至关重要，需要开发高保真的多物理场耦合模拟工具。

Method: 采用OpenMC、OpenFOAM和FESTIM分别求解中子输运、热工水力与氢同位素输运问题，并通过专用工具实现求解器之间空间分辨场的直接传递，实现三物理场耦合的组件尺度模拟。

Result: 在零入口浓度假设下，稳态模拟预测总氚库存约为243 mg，包层在约30分钟内达到稳态氚通量，与先前的系统级估计处于同一量级。结果表明氚输运主要受湍流增强扩散（turbulence-enhanced diffusion）主导，在流动停滞区高度局域化，在高湍流区域累积减少。敏感性分析显示库存预测主要受数值稳定化方案影响，对湍流施密特数（turbulent Schmidt number）依赖较弱。

Conclusion: 所提出的工作流程为ARC级增殖包层中氚输运的高保真分析提供了透明且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of tritium behaviour in molten salt breeding blankets is essential for the design and safe operation of ARC-class fusion reactors. This work presents a fully open-source, component-scale multiphysics framework for modelling tritium transport in an ARC liquid immersion blanket. Neutron transport, thermal hydraulics, and hydrogen isotope transport are coupled using OpenMC, OpenFOAM, and FESTIM, leveraging dedicated tools enabling direct transfer of spatially resolved fields between solvers. Assuming a zero inlet concentration, steady-state simulations predict a total tritium inventory of approximately 243 mg, with the blanket reaching steady-state tritium throughput within approximately 30 min, which is of a similar order to previous system-level estimates. The results show that tritium transport is dominated by turbulence-enhanced diffusion, with strong localisation in flow stagnation regions and reduced accumulation in highly turbulent zones. Sensitivity analyses indicate that predicted inventories are governed primarily by the numerical stabilisation scheme, with only a modest dependence on the turbulent Schmidt number. The proposed workflow provides a transparent and extensible basis for high-fidelity analysis of tritium transport in ARC-class breeding blankets.

</details>

### [64] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基准测试（benchmark）与轨迹日志（trace-logging）框架，系统评估了多智能体架构、大语言模型选择、检索增强生成（RAG）参数等设计选择对显微镜控制智能体性能的影响。通过53项显微镜基准测试、105种智能体配置和1,949次独立测试运行，发现配置间在延迟、令牌使用、成本和故障模式上存在显著差异，但代理模型无法可靠预测智能体在未见任务上的表现，说明当前基准适用于资格认证、回归测试和诊断，但不足以支撑任务无关的全局配置优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正被广泛用于控制科学表征工具（如显微镜和同步辐射光束线），但对物理基础设施的智能体化控制研究尚处于萌芽阶段，缺乏成熟的工程范式和系统化的设计评估方法。研究者需要了解不同智能体架构选择如何影响任务性能，以及现有基准能否预测智能体在未见过的新任务上的泛化能力。

Method: 研究构建了一个基准测试与轨迹日志框架，用于评估单智能体、双智能体和三智能体的图拓扑结构，测试了五种大语言模型（LLM）、检索增强生成（RAG）与上下文参数以及操作约束，覆盖53项显微镜基准测试。共计记录了105种智能体配置、1,949次独立测试运行和49,109次RAG检索，并通过基于智能体架构和测试结果训练的代理模型（surrogate model）检验对新任务的预测能力。

Result: 直接对比显示不同配置在延迟、令牌使用、成本和故障模式上存在清晰差异。然而，基于智能体架构与测试结果训练的代理模型无法可靠预测智能体在全新未见任务（unseen tasks）上的表现，表明当前异构测试套件不支持任务无关的全局配置模型。

Conclusion: 基准测试对于智能体显微镜控制器的资格认证（qualification）、回归测试（regression testing）、诊断和直接对比具有实用价值，但现有基准的设计不足以支撑跨任务的全局配置优化。未来的研究需要重新考虑基准构建策略，以提升对智能体泛化性能的预测能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>

### [65] [Search for an all-sky and a Galactic Ridge diffuse neutrino emission with the first 2 years of KM3NeT/ARCA data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06163)

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用KM3NeT/ARCA探测器前四个配置阶段（6、8、19和21个探测单元）共640天有效观测时间的数据，搜索弥漫性天体物理中微子通量（diffuse astrophysical neutrino flux）的结果。全天分析在单幂律假设下给出了最佳拟合参数，而银河脊（Galactic Ridge）区域的拟合在当前灵敏度下无法约束通量参数。两种分析均获得了上限并与最新测量进行了比较，尽管未取得统计显著结果，但所发展的方法为未来KM3NeT探测器的测量奠定了坚实基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测全天弥漫性天体物理中微子通量以及银河平面特定区域（银河脊）的中微子信号，以深入理解宇宙射线起源与天体物理加速机制。通过搜索中微子信号，为高能天体物理和中微子天文学提供观测约束。

Method: 利用KM3NeT/ARCA探测器前四个配置（6、8、19、21个活跃探测单元）累计640天有效观测时间的数据集，分别对全天区域和银河脊区域进行弥漫中微子通量搜索。全天分析在单幂律（single power-law）假设下拟合通量参数，并与最新测量结果进行对比。

Result: 全天分析在68%可信度下给出的单味天体物理中微子通量参数为$\phi_0^{1f}=3.0^{+2.1}_{-2.0}\times 10^{-18}$ GeV$^{-1}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$ sr$^{-1}$，谱指数（spectral index）$\gamma=3.00^{+0.30}_{-0.35}$。银河脊区域的拟合在当前灵敏度下无法对通量参数给出约束。两项分析均导出了上限并与前沿测量进行了比较。

Conclusion: 当前数据集未产生统计显著的探测结果，但所发展的分析方法为未来利用KM3NeT探测器进行的测量提供了可靠基础，并为后续更大数据量下的搜索确立了分析框架。

Abstract: We report on the search performed for a diffuse astrophysical neutrino flux from the full sky and from a specific region of the Galactic plane, the Galactic Ridge. The study uses the dataset collected with the first KM3NeT/ARCA configurations with 6, 8, 19, and 21 active detection units, corresponding to a total livetime of 640 days. For the all-sky analysis, the fitted single-flavour astrophysical neutrino flux parameters, under the single power-law assumption, are $φ_0^{1f} = 3.0^{+2.1}_{-2.0} \times 10^{-18}$ GeV$^{-1}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$ sr$^{-1}$ with spectral index $γ= 3.00^{+0.30}_{-0.35}$ at 68% credible level. The Galactic Ridge fit does not yield constraints for the flux parameters with the current sensitivity. For both analyses upper limits are derived and compared with state-of-the-art measurements. While the analysis of these datasets has not yielded statistically significant results, the developed methods provide a solid basis for future measurements with the KM3NeT detector.

</details>

### [66] [Summary statistic for pulsar timing arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05295)
*Gabriela Sato-Polito,Matias Zaldarriaga,Barak Zackay*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array, PTA）似然函数显式表示为地球项（Earth term）图与脉冲星项（pulsar term）方差函数的形式，并证明仅需很少的球谐系数即可捕获约95%的引力波信号信息。在NANOGrav 15年数据集的噪声特性和脉冲星位置假设下，Fisher矩阵分析显示地球项图的ℓ_max=2与脉冲星项方差的单极项即可达到这一信息保留比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA观测到的引力波计时残差由非相干（脉冲星项）和相干（地球项）两部分组成，信号本质上具有低维度特征（其角关联函数随多极矩快速衰减），因此有必要量化表征该信号实际所需的最少参数个数，以简化似然函数构造和参数估计。

Method: 作者给出一个显式依赖地球项图与脉冲星项方差的PTA似然函数表达式；随后在NANOGrav 15年数据集的脉冲星噪声属性与天空位置假设下，分别针对随机背景和确定性点源两种信号类型，计算信号振幅的Fisher矩阵（Fisher matrix），以量化不同截断阶数对信息保留比例的影响。

Result: 对于随机背景信号，地球项图的ℓ_max=2阶球谐系数加上脉冲星项方差的单极项即可保留约95%的信号信息；对于点源信号，仅靠这些项还不够，还需将脉冲星项方差的偶极项（dipole）纳入考虑才能达到相近的信息保留比例。

Conclusion: 引力波信号对PTA而言本质上是一种低维对象，少数几个球谐系数即可充分表征，这为构建高效且近似的PTA似然函数提供了理论依据，并指出了不同类型信号在信息压缩时的截断策略差异。

Abstract: The timing residuals produced by gravitational-wave signals can be described as an incoherent (pulsar term) contribution and a coherent (Earth term) map on the sky, which PTAs measure at the locations of the timed pulsars. The observed Earth term map and the variance induced by the pulsar term contain all of the information about any GW signal available to a PTA (assuming pulsar distances are unknown). Furthermore, any type of signal produces on average the same angular correlation function, the Hellings and Downs curve, which decays steeply with multipole as $C_\ell \propto 1/[(\ell+2)(\ell+1)\ell(\ell-1)]$. This suggests that the signal is inherently low-dimensional and therefore only a small number of parameters are needed to fully characterize it. We present an expression for the PTA likelihood that makes the dependence on the Earth term map and pulsar term variance explicit, and show that only a few spherical harmonic coefficients are needed to capture most of the information about the signal. To quantify this in a realistic setting, we compute the Fisher matrix of the amplitude of a stochastic background or a deterministic point source assuming the noise properties and sky locations of the pulsars in the NANOGrav 15yr dataset. We find that $\ell_{\rm max}=2$ of the Earth term map and the monopole of the pulsar term variance retain $\sim 95\%$ of the information about the signal. For a point source, including the dipole of the pulsar term variance is important to achieve a similar fraction.

</details>


### [67] [3-form dark energy and cosmic birefringence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05296)
*Kohei Kamada,Tucker Manton*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨使用3-形式（3-form）暗能量解释宇宙双折射（cosmic birefringence）的可能性。作者研究了两种基于有效场论（EFT）的3-形式与光子之间的耦合，并计算了双折射角β。结果显示，维度-6规范不变算符需要极大的耦合常数才能解释观测值β~0.3°，而维度-4算符可以匹配该值但会激发光子的纵模。文章还推导了β(z)的"普适"轮廓，并比较了3-形式暗能量与轴子类粒子（ALP）暗能量的双折射特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3-形式场在宇宙学背景中是有趣的研究对象，可解释暴胀和暗能量，且其张量结构允许规范标量场无法实现的独特耦合。近期宇宙微波背景辐射（CMB）观测暗示β~0.3°的双折射角，本文旨在探究3-形式暗能量能否解释这一观测现象。

Method: 作者考虑两种受EFT启发的3-形式与光子之间的耦合算符（维度-6规范不变算符和维度-4算符），计算相应的双折射角β。他们还推导β(z)的普适轮廓，并与数值积分得到的极轻质量3-形式场（ultralight massive 3-form）以及轴子类粒子（ALP）暗能量的双折射轮廓进行比较。

Result: 维度-6算符需要极大耦合常数才能解释β~0.3°；维度-4算符可以解释该值但以激发光子纵模为代价。自洽退耦极限（decoupling limit）要求光子质量位于H₀的几个数量级内。维度-4算符产生的β对3-形式势的形式不敏感但依赖于初值条件，而维度-6算符对势的形式高度敏感。3-形式暗能量的双折射可以模仿ALP的行为，也可能与之区分，取决于早期宇宙的场构型。

Conclusion: 3-形式暗能量有潜力解释宇宙双折射观测，但不同耦合算符的可行性差异显著。维度-4算符在实际解释中更具可行性，而维度-6算符则面临耦合常数过大的挑战。该模型的双折射特征依赖于早期宇宙场的初始条件与势的形式，这为区分不同暗能量模型提供了观测窗口。

Abstract: 3-forms are interesting fields to study in the cosmological context for numerous reasons, such as being candidates for explaining inflation and dark energy. The background evolution of a 3-form field is similar to but distinguishable from a scalar field in an expanding universe, and its tensorial structure allows for unique couplings that cannot be experienced by canonical scalars. In this work, we explore the possibility that 3-form dark energy can explain cosmic birefringence. We consider two EFT-inspired couplings between the 3-form and the photon, and compute the birefringence angle $β$. We find that a dimension-6 gauge-invariant operator necessitates an extremely large coupling to explain $β\sim0.3^\circ$, the value suggested by observations of recent cosmic microwave background radiation. Conversely, a dimension-4 operator can accommodate $β\sim0.3^\circ$ at the expense exciting the longitudinal mode of the photon. Interestingly, demanding a self-consistent decoupling limit implies the photon mass lies within a few orders of magnitude of $H_0$. We also derive `universal' profiles for $β(z)$, finding the $β$ from the dimension-4 operator is insensitive to the form of the 3-form potential but is sensitive to the initial conditions. Contrarily, the dimension-6 operator is highly sensitive to the form of the potential. We finally compare the universal profiles to that of axion-like particle (ALP) dark energy and an ultralight massive 3-form, the latter obtained from numerically integrated cosmological histories consistent with $Λ$CDM up to low redshift. Our results show that birefringence from 3-form dark energy can both mimic that from an ALP or be distinguishable, depending on the field configuration in the early universe.

</details>

### [68] [On the heat flow conjecture for random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05185)

Abstract: We derive the generalized time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau theory of a disordered noncentrosymmetric superconductor from the Keldysh nonlinear sigma model, using a two-dimensional electron gas with Rashba spin-orbit coupling and an in-plane Zeeman field as a minimal model. On the thermodynamic side we construct the Lifshitz invariants of the free energy, the linear and cubic gradient terms and the momentum-odd part of the quartic vertex, and trace their dependence on disorder. These couplings are governed by a single closed-form kernel controlled by the ratio of the Dyakonov-Perel spin-relaxation rate to temperature, interpolating between the weak-relaxation regime, where the invariants are suppressed, and the relaxation-dominated regime, where the helical modulation of the order parameter saturates at a universal, disorder-independent value. This crossover reconciles conflicting results for the magnetoelectric couplings of dirty Rashba superconductors. Because the theory is formulated on the Keldysh contour, it also determines the dissipative dynamics: the relaxation rate of a fluctuation with pair momentum $\mathbf{q}$, and hence, by the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, the Langevin noise power, acquires a term odd in $\mathbf{q}$ and odd in the magnetic field. The structure of this kinetic Lifshitz invariant is dictated by Onsager reciprocity: friction and noise renormalize in lockstep, so equal-time fluctuations remain Gibbsian while the dynamics are nonreciprocal. As applications we compute the superconducting diode efficiency near $T_c$, where the cubic invariant competes with the quartic vertex and with even higher-gradient terms rendered odd by the helical shift, reversing the sign of the diode coefficient, and the fluctuation-induced magnetochiral anisotropy above $T_c$, where the current-resolved nonreciprocal resistance forms a plateau across the Gaussian regime.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 预测了波函数呈环状分布的稳定零能马约拉纳模式，即环形马约拉纳模式。在单非线性狄拉克锥情形下，该模式源于有效手性f波超导性（chiral f-wave superconductivity）；在三线性狄拉克锥情形下，源于三个锥上有效手性p波超导性（chiral p-wave superconductivity）的干涉。两种情形中环形模式均由旋转对称性和表面态携带的绕数3共同稳定，并给出了候选材料。

Abstract: When the surface states of a topological insulator becomes superconducting, topological superconductivity can be obtained, and each vortex on the surface can host one single Majorana zero-energy mode which is usually a wave packet decaying exponentially off the vortex core. Here, we predict stable Majorana zero-energy mode whose wave function is ring-shape, dubbed as annular Majorana mode, in the superconducting vortex in topological insulators respecting $3$-fold or $6$-fold rotational symmetry. Such topological insulators are featured with a single nonlinear Dirac cone located at $\barΓ$ or three linear Dirac cones at $\bar{\text{M}}$ in the surface Brillouin zone. The annular Majorana mode originates from the effective chiral $f$-wave superconductivity on the nonlinear Dirac cone in the former case and the interference of the effective chiral $p$-wave superconductivity on the three linear Dirac cones in the latter. In both cases, the annular Majorana mode is stabilized by the rotational symmetry and the winding number $3$ carried by the surface states. Candidate materials supporting the annular Majorana mode are predicted. Our work provides new insights into the topological superconductivity in superconducting topological insulators.

Motivation: 传统自下而上的粗粒化（bottom-up coarse-graining）方法在模拟复杂生物分子动力学时存在信息损失，难以准确刻画多态现象。已有超粗粒化（UCG）方法依赖用户定义集体变量（CVs），并忽略了粗粒化分子内部及相互之间的内部状态关联，制约了模型表达能力。

Method: SC-UCG利用UCG相互作用直接分配内部状态，无需在粗粒化系综中设计集体变量；模拟中通过图消息传递（graph message passing）自洽确定内部状态概率。采用Bethe近似和基于AI的推理增强快速局域平衡（RLE）哈密顿量以表示UCG珠子间的显式关联。力场训练方面，提出多层内部状态一致性（MISC）机器学习方法，源于相对熵最小化，避免了中间力场的迭代采样。

Result: SC-UCG应用于具有二级对称性破缺相变的四聚体体系，该体系经历从超临界外消旋流体到亚临界D-rich与L-rich流体的转变。SC-UCG成功捕获了亚临界区域内部状态的集体开关行为，并在仅用单温度数据集训练的情况下，跨温度复现了该相变。

Conclusion: SC-UCG通过自一致性内部状态分配和显式关联建模，显著提升了UCG框架对多态现象与相变的表征能力，并以其单温度训练即可跨温度泛化验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Bottom-up coarse-graining expands the length and time scales accessible to molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, but information loss can hinder accurate representation of multistate phenomena in complex biomolecular dynamics. Ultra-Coarse-Graining (UCG) projects discrete "quantum-like" extended degrees of freedom, or "internal states," onto coarse-grained (CG) molecules, extending CG model expressiveness. The rapid-local-equilibrium (RLE) approximation in UCG depends on user-defined collective variables (CVs, e.g., local density) and neglects correlations between internal states within and between CG molecules. We present Self-Consistent UCG (SC-UCG), which uses the underlying UCG interactions directly to assign internal states without designing CVs in the CG ensemble. During simulation, internal state probabilities are determined self-consistently through graph message passing. We enhance the RLE Hamiltonian with the Bethe approximation and AI-based inference to represent explicit correlations between UCG beads. For force-field training, we develop Multilayer Internal State Consistency (MISC), a machine-learning method derived from relative entropy minimization that avoids iterative sampling of intermediate force fields. We apply SC-UCG to a tetramer exhibiting a second-order symmetry-breaking phase transition from a supercritical racemic fluid to subcritical D-rich and L-rich fluids. SC-UCG captures collective switching of internal states in the subcritical region and recapitulates the phase transition across temperatures, despite being trained on a single-temperature dataset.

</details>

Abstract: We construct exact transparent boundary conditions (TBCs) for a time-continuous, spatially discrete Schrödinger equation that models a one-dimensional quantum lattice. Using a recently developed exact solution for the discrete system, we derive the Dirichlet-to-Neumann maps analytically via Laplace transforms. This yields a convolution-type boundary condition governed by Bessel functions. We rigorously demonstrate the consistency of this discrete formulation with its continuous counterpart in the continuum limit. Additionally, we present an efficient time-discretization scheme based on the trapezoidal rule for practical implementation. Numerical experiments using a Crank-Nicolson solver verify that our proposed TBCs eliminate spurious backscattering entirely and preserve reflectionless propagation of a Gaussian wave packet exiting the computational domain

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a notion of completeness for two-dimensional second-gradient elastic continua and propose a microstructural route toward its synthesis. A continuum is said to be complete if the Hessian of the stored energy with respect to the second-gradient variable is locally positive definite, so that every nonzero admissible increment of the placement second gradient is quadratically controlled in the highest-order part of the energy about each configuration. The theory is then applied to several continua motivated by pantographic microstructures. Finally, we formulate a tri-pantographic continuum associated with a proposed three-family architecture and prove that it is complete. The examples illustrate how microstructural architecture influences both the completeness properties of an effective continuum and the pointwise form of its higher-order boundary interactions.

Abstract: We introduce a second-order mean-field description of the four-dimensional Euclidean $φ^4$ model within the Wilson--Polchinski renormalization-group framework. Abstract: Starting from the Standard Model Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}_{\mathrm{SM}} = \mathfrak{sl}_3 \oplus \mathfrak{sl}_2 \oplus \mathbb{C}$ sitting inside the complex Lie algebra $\mathfrak{e}_7$, we show how to decompose $\mathfrak{e}_7$ into the direct sum of a Lie subalgebra containing $\mathfrak{g}_{\mathrm{SM}}$ and three 32-dimensional subspaces, each of which forms the same representation of $\mathfrak{g}_{\mathrm{SM}}$ as one generation of fermions and their antiparticles. Abstract: The search for scalable, electrically controlled qubits remains a central challenge in quantum technology. We introduce gate-defined moiré quantum dots as a promising platform for valley-based qubits. Moiré engineering resolves the central conflict of valley physics: momentum-space separation protects the states, while the enlarged moiré length scale allows smooth gates to mix them controllably. Dot geometry and confinement strength program valley hybridization, while a displacement field controls detuning, providing two noncommuting electrostatic axes for qubit control.

</details>

Method: 作者证明电荷转移激子在实空间中的嵌入可以生成对称性相关的离位复合轨道流形（manifold），其耦合支持拓扑激子能带。横向电子-空穴分离将局域激子嵌入连接两个组分位点的键上而非任一单独位点。在蜂窝晶格中，三个键中心电荷转移激子轨道构成Kagome晶格，并通过求解Bethe-Salpeter方程来验证这一机制。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条通往局域复合束缚态拓扑能带以及非常规强关联玻色子相的一般性路径。

Abstract: Excitons possess internal structure absent from single-particle Bloch particles, allowing their band topology to emerge from the bound-state structure rather than being inherited from their constituents. This raises the question of how the internal structure of a bound state can provide a microscopic origin of exciton topology. Here we show that the real-space embedding of charge-transfer excitons can generate an intrinsic manifold of symmetry-related off-site composite orbitals whose coupling supports topological exciton bands. Lateral electron-hole separation embeds the localized exciton on the bond connecting its constituent sites rather than on either site. We demonstrate this mechanism in a honeycomb lattice, where three bond-centered charge-transfer exciton orbitals form a Kagome lattice. By solving the Bethe-Salpeter equation, we show that this emergent multi-orbital manifold supports a topological exciton flat band upon time-reversal symmetry breaking, even when the electron and hole bands are topologically trivial. The resulting band exhibits nearly uniformly distributed quantum geometry, favorable for interaction-driven bosonic states. Our results establish a general route toward topological bands of localized composite bound states and unconventional strongly correlated bosonic phases.

Method: 作者在Matsubara空间中工作，通过动态屏蔽的库仑相互作用（dynamically screened Coulomb interaction）和杂质梯图（impurity ladder），在扩散通道中计算交换贡献。系统能带由两个横向弱隧穿耦合的翘曲片（warped sheets）组成，SOC将各片分裂为具有固定自旋轴的螺旋性分支（helicity branches）。

Result: 计算结果显示DOS异常呈现由横向耦合尺度ε_c控制的维度交叉：当|ε−ε_F|<ε_c时，系统呈二维行为，具有对数型DOS凹陷，其幅度因本征SOC增强；当|ε−ε_F|>ε_c时，系统呈准一维行为，具有更尖锐的平方根奇异性，其幅度被SOC显著增强。此外，存在一个临界SOC强度，在该强度下自旋轨道效应精确抵消电子关联修正，完全恢复未扰动态密度；超过该临界点后异常符号完全反转，产生正的DOS修正。

Conclusion: 自旋轨道耦合不仅能增强DOS异常的幅度，还能在临界强度下完全抵消电子关联效应，并在超过临界点后反转修正符号，改变正修正的能量依赖趋势（在ε_F+ε_c以上随能量增大而衰减），这与标准负修正形成鲜明对比，提供了SOC调制关联效应的独特光谱特征。

Abstract: We study Altshuler-Aronov-type interaction corrections to the single-particle density of states (DOS) in a strongly anisotropic 2D conductor with an open Fermi surface (FS) and weak disorder, in the presence of coexisting Rashba and Dresselhaus spin-orbit couplings (SOCs) constrained to the longitudinal direction. The low-energy band consists of two warped sheets weakly tunnel-coupled transversely; SOC splits the sheets into helicity branches with a fixed spin axis. Working in a Matsubara space, we compute the exchange contribution in the diffusion channel with dynamically screened Coulomb interaction and an impurity ladder. The resulting DOS anomaly exhibits a dimensional crossover governed by the transverse coupling scale $\varepsilon_c$. Close to the Fermi level ($|\varepsilon-\varepsilon_F|<\varepsilon_c$), the system behaves two-dimensionally, featuring a logarithmic DOS dip whose magnitude is enhanced by intrinsic SOCs. Further from the Fermi level ($|\varepsilon-\varepsilon_F|\!>\!\varepsilon_c$), the system behaves quasi-one-dimensionally, featuring a sharper square-root singularity whose amplitude is remarkably enhanced by the SOCs. Notably, we identify a critical SOC strength at which these spin-orbit effects exactly cancel the electron-correlation correction, perfectly restoring the unperturbed density of states. Furthermore, increasing the SOC beyond this critical point inverts the sign of the anomaly entirely, yielding a positive DOS correction. This sign reversal fundamentally alters the energy dependence, such that at energies beyond $\varepsilon_F + \varepsilon_c$, the positive correction decays to smaller values as energy increases, opposite to the standard negative correction. This contrasting trend provides a distinct spectroscopic signature of SOC-modulated correlation effects.

</details>


### [83] [One-dimensional Dirac modes in the core of a pentagonal topological crystalline insulator nanowire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05908)
*Saeed Samadi,Rafał Rechciński,Marta A. Chabowska,Ryszard Buczko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五边形 IV-VI 族半导体纳米线包含五个径向 {111} 孪晶界（twin planes）汇聚于纳米线轴线，为研究拓扑电子结构提供了新的几何构型。作者旨在探明这类结构中体带反转与孪晶界化学性质如何影响其电子能带拓扑性质。

Method: 采用紧束缚计算方法研究纳米线电子带结构，系统比较体带反转与未反转、孪晶界为阳离子型或阴离子型等情况下的能谱特征。进一步对 Pb$_{0.4}$Sn$_{0.6}$Te 体系开展实际多轨道（multiorbital）计算，验证不同纳米线厚度下的模式演化。

Result: 当体带结构反转且孪晶界为阳离子型时，能谱在 $\overlineΓ$ 附近出现两个空间分离的螺旋狄拉克交叉，分别定域于纳米线核心和外表面；孪晶界为阴离子型时对应能谱保持有隙。该交叉源于五个与孪晶界边缘关联的螺旋通道（helical channels）的杂化，其奇数个数使得一个克拉默斯对（Kramers pair）留近轴线、另一个留于外边界。Pb$_{0.4}$Sn$_{0.6}$Te 的多轨道计算预言在约 50 nm 及以上厚度出现清晰的核心和表面模式。

Conclusion: 五边形 SnTe 类纳米线可被视为空间分离螺旋通道的实验可及实现，这些通道分别束缚于轴向缺陷（核心）和外边界（表面），为研究一维拓扑边界态的分离与操控提供了新平台。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic band topology of recently fabricated pentagonal IV-VI semiconductor nanowires, which contain five radial $\{111\}$ twin planes meeting at the nanowire axis. Tight-binding calculations show that, when the bulk band structure is inverted and the twin planes in the nanowire are cationic, the spectrum contains two spatially separated helical Dirac crossings near $\overlineΓ$: one localized at the core and the other at the outer surface. When the twin planes are anionic, the corresponding spectra remain gapped. The crossings originate from the hybridization of five helical channels associated with the twin-plane edges, whose odd number leaves one Kramers pair near the nanowire axis and the other at the outer boundary. Realistic multiorbital calculations for $\mathrm{Pb}_{0.4}\mathrm{Sn}_{0.6}\mathrm{Te}$ predict well-developed core and surface modes at nanowire thicknesses of approximately 50~nm and above. These results establish pentagonal SnTe-class nanowires as an experimentally accessible realization of spatially separated helical channels bound to the axial defect and the outer boundary.

### [84] [An Effective String Theory Toolbox for Quantum Hall Interfaces I: Worldsheet Kinematics and Constraint Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06097)

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对自由移动量子霍尔（quantum Hall）界面的理论框架，利用空间重参数化不变的世界面（worldsheet）描述，从双侧Chern–Simons响应推导出正常电荷输运与界面运动的关系，并结合相对面积构造和折叠K-矩阵流代数，识别出移动界面的普适霍尔运动学，同时将几何能量和中性动力学归因于微观界面物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由移动的量子霍尔界面与由外部约束势固定的普通边缘（edge）在物理上有本质差异：界面法向位移会改变相邻不可压缩相所占面积，因此界面几何与电荷动力学不能作为独立自由度处理。作者旨在为这类动力学界面建立系统化的低能有效理论框架。

Method: 论文采用空间重参数化不变的世界面描述方法，其中切向运动仅为界面的重新标记（relabeling），而法向运动才具有物理意义。作者从双侧Chern–Simons响应出发推导法向电荷输运与界面运动的联系，随后引入相对于材料参考曲线的相对面积构造，将速度关系转化为连接带电边界扇区与界面形状的等时约束，再结合折叠K-矩阵（folded K-matrix）流代数，完整识别移动界面的普适霍尔运动学。

Result: 该框架成功分离了界面动力学中的普适与微观依赖部分：霍尔运动学（Hall kinematics）部分对阿贝尔（Abelian）量子霍尔相界面具有普适性，而界面几何能量和中性动力学则依赖于微观界面物理的具体细节。

Conclusion: 该研究为动力学量子霍尔界面的有效理论提供了系统化的基础，使得界面形状自由度与电荷扇区之间的耦合能在统一的变分框架下得到自洽描述。

Abstract: A freely moving quantum Hall interface is fundamentally different from an ordinary edge fixed by an external confining potential. Since a normal displacement changes the areas occupied by the adjacent incompressible phases, the interface geometry and charge dynamics cannot be treated as independent degrees of freedom. We formulate this problem for interfaces between Abelian quantum Hall phases using a spatially reparametrization-invariant worldsheet description, in which tangential motion is a relabeling of the interface while normal motion is physical. Starting from the two-sided Chern--Simons response, we derive the relation between normal charge transport and interface motion. We then introduce a relative-area construction, defined with respect to a material reference curve, that converts this velocity relation into an equal-time constraint linking the charged boundary sector to the interface shape. Combined with the folded $K$-matrix current algebra, this identifies the universal Hall kinematics of the moving interface while leaving its geometric energy and neutral dynamics dependent on microscopic interface physics. The resulting framework provides a systematic basis for effective theories of dynamical quantum Hall interfaces.

</details>


### [85] [An Effective String Theory Toolbox for Quantum Hall Interfaces II: Majorana Fermions on Fluctuating Moore-Read Worldsheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06098)

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个空间重参数化不变（spatially reparametrization-invariant）的马约拉纳（Majorana）理论，用于描述自由运动界面上携带的手征马约拉纳模。变化的线元确定了普适的半密度输运定律，而马约拉纳应力张量（stress tensor）作为中性模式与几何动力学之间的中介，为动态非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）量子霍尔界面的有效理论提供了中性扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moore–Read界面在手征边界上携带马约拉纳模，但边界几何本身可发生涨落，固定边界理论无法确定该中性模式在界面弯曲和运动时如何输运，也无法确定其动力学与界面形状涨落的耦合方式。

Method: 作者在自由运动界面的非相对论性世界面（worldsheet）上构造了一个空间重参数化不变的马约拉纳理论。通过变化的线元（line element）确定普适的半密度（half-density）输运定律，并引入额外的曲率和速度依赖耦合，这些耦合由微观界面物理学控制。

Result: 该框架将马约拉纳应力张量识别为中性模式与几何动力学之间的中介体，提供了动态非阿贝尔量子霍尔界面有效理论所需的中性扇区（neutral sector）。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态非阿贝尔量子霍尔界面的有效理论补全了中性模式部分，建立了中性输运与界面几何涨落之间的理论框架。

Abstract: A Moore--Read interface carries a chiral Majorana mode on a boundary whose geometry may itself fluctuate. Fixed-edge theory does not determine how this neutral mode should be transported when the interface bends and moves, or how its dynamics couples to the fluctuating shape. Here we construct a spatially reparametrization-invariant Majorana theory on the nonrelativistic worldsheet of a freely moving interface. The changing line element fixes a universal half-density transport law, while additional curvature- and velocity-dependent couplings remain controlled by microscopic interface physics. The resulting framework identifies the Majorana stress tensor as the mediator between neutral and geometric dynamics and provides the neutral sector needed for effective theories of dynamical non-Abelian quantum Hall interfaces.

</details>


### [86] [An Effective String Theory Toolbox for Quantum Hall Interfaces III: Open Worldsheets, Endpoint Conditions, and Branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06100)
*Ken K. W. Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个"开世界面结"（open-worldsheet junction）框架，统一组织嵌入、材料电荷、反常流和拓扑边界条件，为自由运动的量子霍尔（quantum Hall, QH）界面在物理边界或拓扑边界上的终止一致性提供了系统性判据，并指出孤立的手性马约拉纳（chiral Majorana）模式无法在有限维端点自由度上终止。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由运动的量子霍尔界面可能在物理边缘或拓扑边界上终止，但固定边缘理论无法确定什么样的端点数据能使这种终止在物理上自洽。因此需要一个新的理论框架来刻画这种终止的一致性条件。

Method: 作者构建了一个"开世界面结框架"，将嵌入（embedding）、材料电荷（material charge）、反常流（anomaly flow）和拓扑边界条件（topological boundary condition）统一组织。端点由几何支撑（geometric support）和变分边界数据（variational boundary data）指定，同时包含可凝聚拓扑扇区（condensable topological sectors）以及吸收或延续世界面通量所需的外向通道（outgoing channels）。

Result: 该框架将电荷-形状关系（charge–shape relation）推广到区间情形，揭示了孤立的手性马约拉纳模式无法在有限维端点自由度上终止的原因，并提供了量子霍尔膜（QH brane）的操作性定义。

Conclusion: 这一框架为动态量子霍尔界面的端点和网络理论提供了系统性的基础，使得端点数据的自洽性可以得到统一的判据约束。

Abstract: A freely moving quantum Hall (QH) interface may end on a physical edge or topological boundary, but fixed-edge theory cannot determine what endpoint data make such a termination consistent. Here we formulate an open-worldsheet junction framework in which the embedding, material charge, anomaly flow, and topological boundary condition are organized together. The endpoint is specified by a geometric support and variational boundary data, together with condensable topological sectors and any outgoing channels required to absorb or continue the worldsheet flux. This construction extends the charge--shape relation to an interval and shows why a lone chiral Majorana cannot terminate on a finite-dimensional endpoint degree of freedom. It gives an operational definition of a QH brane and a systematic basis for endpoint and network theories of dynamical QH interfaces.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond Electrons: Radiative Thermal Computing and Neural Networks at the Near-Field Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06203)

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种可编程的近场辐射热网络（near-field radiative thermal network），通过热交换模拟卷积与循环操作，构建了热卷积神经网络（T-CNN）和热循环神经网络（T-RNN）。该系统利用VO2与GST等相变材料构成的辐射热二极管和晶体管，实现了无电荷载体、无电路的接触式深度学习，通过温度场作为智能载体，统一了热传输、记忆与自适应推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度学习依赖电荷载体的电学系统，能耗高且受电路物理限制。本文旨在探索一种全新的计算范式，利用近场光子隧穿（near-field photon tunneling）与相变材料的热交换机制，在不依赖电荷载体和电路的前提下实现卷积、记忆与反馈学习，从而建立温度场作为智能载体的新型非电子计算平台。

Method: 该研究提出了两种基本架构：T-CNN通过温度依赖的发射率调制实现对空间模式的识别，每个门作为可调的辐射滤波器以放大或抑制局域热流；T-RNN则通过嵌入辐射反馈和滞回相变在时间维度上实现记忆与序列推理。系统通过VO2/GST中门温度分布进行外部编程，利用滞回（hysteretic）转变提供状态依赖响应以支持存储功能，所有逻辑节点仅依赖纳米尺度表面间的近场光子隧穿运行，无需电荷载体或电学电路。

Result: 所构建的系统展现出三态逻辑行为（tri-state logic behavior）、辐射增益（radiative gain）以及非易失性热存储（non-volatile thermal storage），这些是物理学习（physical learning）的关键属性。系统实现了加权求和与非线性激活功能，并能够在接触式、节能的平台中直接实现卷积操作、记忆保持与反馈学习。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了第一个辐射形式的热深度学习（thermal deep learning）实现，揭示了以温度场作为智能载体的全新计算范式。该框架将热传输、记忆与自适应推理统一在单一的非电子系统中，为无电荷载体的深度学习计算提供了新的物理基础与实现路径。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a programmable near-field radiative thermal network that emulates convolutional and recurrent operations through heat exchange. By integrating radiative thermal diodes and transistors composed of phase-change materials such as VO2 and GST, we construct two fundamental architectures: the Thermal Convolutional Neural Network (T-CNN) and the Thermal Recurrent Neural Network (T-RNN). The T-CNN executes spatial pattern recognition via temperature-dependent emissivity modulation, where each gate acts as a tunable radiative filter that amplifies or suppresses localized heat flux. The system is externally programmable via gate temperature distributions in VO2/GST, enabling weighted summation and nonlinear activation; hysteretic transitions provide state dependent responses that can be harnessed for memory. The T-RNN extends this functionality temporally, embedding radiative feedback and hysteretic phase transitions to realize memory and sequential inference. Together, these systems exhibit tri-state logic behavior, radiative gain, and non-volatile thermal storage, key attributes of physical learning. Each logical node operates without charge carriers or electrical circuits, relying solely on near-field photon tunneling between nanoscale surfaces. The resulting framework enables direct implementation of convolution, memory retention, and feedback learning within a contactless, energy-conserving platform. This study establishes the first radiative realization of thermal deep learning, revealing a new computing paradigm where temperature fields function as carriers of intelligence, uniting heat transfer, memory, and adaptive inference in a single non-electronic system.

</details>


### [88] [Unconventional Scaling of Electric Hall Effect in Magnetic Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06217)
*Chaoxi Cui,Yilin Han,Run-Wu Zhang,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究了二维磁性外尔半金属（Weyl semimetal）中的电霍尔效应（Electric Hall Effect, EHE），发现该效应在零温下呈现拓扑性的 $E_F^{-1}$ 费米能量标度律，其前因子由节点全局拓扑荷决定而与局域参数无关；在有限温度下该效应演化为非传统的对数修正标度 $\sigma_{xy}\propto E_z\ln(1/|E_z|)$，且不被温度削弱，由此提出了一种无需磁场、可实际实现的弱电场探测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电霍尔效应是二维磁性系统中由面外电场产生霍尔电流的独特现象，但此前对其在磁性拓扑材料中的标度行为尚不清楚。作者试图揭示磁性（magnetism）与拓扑（topology）相互作用下电霍尔效应所呈现的非常规标度律及其应用潜力。

Method: 该研究聚焦于具有双重简并节点的二维磁性外尔半金属，通过理论分析分别考察零温度和有限温度两种情形下电霍尔效应的标度行为。零温下分析费米能量趋近外尔点时霍尔响应的标度律及其前因子与全局拓扑荷的关系；有限温度下考察弱电场极限中的修正标度及电场磁化率行为。

Result: 零温度下，电霍尔效应呈现拓扑性的 $E_F^{-1}$ 费米能量标度，其前因子仅由节点的全局拓扑荷决定，与系统局域参数无关，因此在费米能量接近外尔点时霍尔响应获得普遍且显著的增强。有限温度下这一增强响应不被削弱，弱电场下演化为 $\sigma_{xy}\propto E_z\ln(1/|E_z|)$ 的对数修正标度，电场磁化率仍发散。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了磁性-拓扑相互作用产生的独特标度律，并提出了一种标度增强、温度鲁棒的机制，可通过全电学（all-electric）途径实现弱电场的实际探测。

Abstract: Electric Hall Effect (EHE), a unique phenomenon in two-dimensional (2D) magnetic systems, refers to the generation of Hall current by an out-of-plane electric field $\Ez$. Here, we demonstrate that for 2D magnetic Weyl semimetals that host doubly degenerate nodal points, the EHE features multiple unconventional scaling laws. At zero temperature, the EHE exhibits a topological $E_F^{-1}$ Fermi-energy scaling. Remarkably, the prefactor of the scaling is determined by the global topological charge of the point without any dependence on the local parameters of the system, leading to a universal and significant enhancement of Hall response in any species of Weyl points as the Fermi energy approaches the Weyl point. This significant response enables a weak electric field to be directly converted into a measurable Hall signal. Surprisingly, this enhanced Hall response is not diminished by temperature, but evolves into an unconventional logarithmically corrected scaling at finite temperature $σ_{xy}\propto\Ez\ln(1/|\Ez|)$ for weak $\Ez$, still yielding a divergent electric-field susceptibility. Thus, our work not only unveils intriguing scaling laws resulting from the interaction between magnetism and topology, but also suggests a novel scaling-enhanced and temperature-robust mechanism that may enable weak electric-field sensing through a practical and all-electric route.

</details>


### [89] [Geometric closure of classical nucleation theory for magnetic-field-controlled nanoparticle size across magnetic classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06220)
*Yazeed Tawalbeh,Mauro Fernandes Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过引入球堆积（sphere-packing）表示，将经典成核理论（classical nucleation theory）重构为几何封闭的热力学框架，建立了磁场辅助纳米粒子成核的统一理论，能够定量复现磁铁矿和镍纳米粒子在磁场下平均尺寸减小和尺寸分布变窄的实验观察。

Method: 引入球堆积表示原子组装过程，将离散原子结构与外加磁场下的连续自由能贡献直接联系起来，由此将经典成核理论重构为几何封闭的热力学框架，推导出临界核尺寸随磁场演化的方程。

Abstract: Controlling nanoparticle size during synthesis remains a central challenge in nanoscience, particularly in systems where external magnetic fields are used as continuous control parameters. Existing descriptions of magnetic-field-assisted nucleation are typically material-specific or rely on computationally intensive atomistic methods. Here, we reformulate classical nucleation theory as a geometrically closed thermodynamic framework by introducing a sphere-packing representation of atomic assembly. This construction establishes a direct link between discrete atomic structure and continuum free-energy contributions under applied magnetic fields, yielding a field-driven evolution equation for the critical nucleus size. The resulting theory provides a unified description of nanoparticle nucleation across superparamagnetic, paramagnetic, and diamagnetic systems within a single formalism. It quantitatively reproduces previously unresolved experimental observations for magnetite and nickel nanoparticles, namely the systematic reduction of mean particle size and narrowing of size distributions with increasing magnetic field. In the diamagnetic limit, the framework recovers our earlier analytical susceptibility-based description of silver nanoparticles, in which the field-dependent critical radius is governed by the induced-magnetization contribution to the nucleation free energy. Beyond modeling the reduction of mean particle size with increasing magnetic field, the framework reveals that the narrowing of size distributions emerges naturally from the curvature of the field-modified free-energy landscape. These results establish magnetic-field-assisted nucleation as a geometrically constrained thermodynamic process, providing a computationally efficient route for controlling nanoparticle size across distinct magnetic material classes.

</details>

Abstract: Mobile impurities provide a powerful means of probing correlated and topological quantum matter, through their dressing by the surrounding medium and the practical probes granting access to the resulting composite object. Motivated by the recent observation of anyon-impurity composites in the solid state, as well as recent realizations of Laughlin-type states in engineered lattice systems, we investigate the formation of a bound state between a mobile impurity and a single pinned quasihole in the interacting Harper-Hofstadter model deep in the fractional Chern insulator regime. Combining analytical arguments with large-scale numerical simulations, we characterize the structure, energetics, and stability of hybrid anyon-impurity bound states, and show that their binding energy provides direct access to the fractional charge of the quasihole under conditions that we identify. We further demonstrate that the composite object can be coherently transported by externally steering the quasihole pinning potential. Our results establish a realistic pathway for controlled anyon-impurity manipulation in quantum-engineered platforms, enabling experimentally feasible protocols for braiding.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者首先证明热密度矩阵的微扰理论产生两个对偶的信息论联络（dual information-theoretic connections）和一个近复结构（almost complex structure）。引入经典信息论中Amari-Chentsov张量的复量子推广，即cQAC张量，用以刻画微扰密度矩阵的多态几何。基于此推导出绝缘体频域积分DC整流响应的零温求和规则，将其表示为基态三阶累积量（third cumulant）与复畸变张量（complex distortion tensor）之差。

Result: 求和规则将已知的移位电流和非线性霍尔电流的单粒子求和规则推广到多体系统及一般微扰情形。针对移位电流，作者将多带绝缘体的几何贡献分解为类粒子和类空穴项。在广义Kane-Mele模型中数值验证了求和规则，发现几何贡献可以主导积分响应。此外，尽管非零温度下求和规则的累积量-几何贡献分解不再成立，但绝缘体实测求和规则与零温形式之间的修正随能隙指数小，使得低温整流测量能够探测绝缘体的多态几何。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了无序和相互作用系统中多态几何与非线性响应之间的严格联系，为通过低温整流实验探测绝缘体多态几何提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The geometry of quantum states has emerged as a key ingredient in understanding the linear and nonlinear responses of quantum materials. To date, however, the connection between geometry and nonlinear response is best understood for clean, noninteracting systems at zero temperature. In this work, we develop a theory of multi-state geometry for density matrices and use it to derive sum rules for second-order rectification, making no assumptions about the strength of disorder or interactions. We first show that perturbation theory for thermal density matrices gives rise to two dual information-theoretic connections and an almost complex structure. We introduce a complex, quantum generalization of the Amari-Chentsov tensor of classical information theory, the cQAC tensor, which captures the multi-state geometry of the perturbed density matrix. We derive a zero-temperature sum rule for the frequency-integrated DC rectification response of an insulator as a difference between a ground state third cumulant and the complex distortion tensor, a multi-state geometric quantity built from the cQAC tensor. This generalizes known single-particle sum rules for the shift and nonlinear Hall currents to many-body systems and general perturbations. Specializing to the shift current, we resolve the geometric contribution for multiband insulators into particle-like and hole-like terms. We verify the sum rule numerically in a generalized Kane-Mele model, finding that the geometric contribution can dominate the integrated response. Finally, we show that although the splitting of the sum rule into cumulant and geometric contributions does not survive at nonzero temperature, the measured sum rule for insulators differs from its zero-temperature form by corrections exponentially small in the gap, allowing low-temperature rectification measurements to probe the multi-state geometry of insulators.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有拓扑力学系统大多关注线性边缘态，但缺乏对非线性拓扑边缘波（nonlinear topological edge waves）的系统研究，特别是在机械系统中如何将非线性与拓扑保护相结合以实现更丰富的信息处理功能尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建由具有在位三次非线性（on-site cubic nonlinearity）的摆锤通过线性弹簧连接而成的体晶格以实现量子自旋霍尔效应（quantum spin Hall effect）；在具有相反自旋陈数（spin Chern number）的两个体晶格界面上设计线性弹簧，使色散关系出现EGV点并获得有利的CNLS系数；采用双分量耦合非线性薛定谔方程描述两个边缘模间的非线性相互作用，并通过数值方法验证孤子解及其拓扑保护性。

Result: 在聚焦CNLS系数条件下实现了亮-亮（bright-bright）边缘孤子；在散焦条件下实现了暗-暗（dark-dark）边缘孤子、边缘畴壁和暗-亮（dark-bright）边缘孤子。这些解在位振幅上类似于亮暗呼吸子（breathers），且当两个载频均位于带隙内时具有拓扑保护性——通过将BB边缘孤子穿过界面上的紧致缺陷验证了这一点。此外，BB边缘孤子碰撞中的能量转移展示了基于碰撞计算（collision-based computing）的潜在应用。

Conclusion: 向量边缘孤子（vector edge solitons）为孤子碰撞提供了广阔的参数空间，赋予机械器件更大的信息处理和其他功能潜力，为机械拓扑系统中非线性信息处理技术的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: We report nonlinear edge waves in a 2D mechanical topological insulator. A bulk lattice consists of pendulums with on-site cubic nonlinearity connected by linear springs realizing quantum spin Hall effect. We show that the nonlinear interaction between two edge modes with equal group velocities (EGV) is described by a 1D two-component coupled nonlinear Schrödinger (CNLS) equation. On the interface separating two bulk lattices with opposite spin Chern numbers, we construct linear springs such that the dispersion relation exhibits EGV points with favorable CNLS coefficients. Thus, we realize nonlinear edge waves propagating along the interface, including bright-bright (BB) edge solitons for focusing CNLS coefficients, and dark-dark edge solitons, edge domain walls, and dark-bright edge solitons for defocusing CNLS coefficients. In terms of the site amplitudes, these solutions resemble bright and dark breathers. These solutions should be topologically protected when both carrier frequencies lie within a band gap, which we explicitly show by passing BB edge solitons through compact defects on the interface. We also show energy transfer in BB edge soliton collisions with potential application to collision-based computing. Generally, vector edge solitons exhibit a large parameter space for soliton collisions, which endows mechanical devices with greater potential for information processing and other functionalities.

</details>

Method: 采用无界次正规算子的模型论方法（model theory of unbounded subnormal operators），并对照复数情形下Szefraniec在文献[19]中的相关处理来建立四元数情形下的理论联系。

Abstract: In this note, we observe that the class of unbounded subnormal operators on quaternionic Hilbert spaces is a solution of quaternionic quantum harmonic q-oscillator whose complex case is addressed by Szefraniec in [19]. We also address the connection of unbounded subnormal operators on quaternionic Hilbert spaces, quaternionic quantum harmonic q-oscillator and quaternionic quantum economics using model theory of unbounded subnormal operators.

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用理论框架，描述三角格子量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）中的U(1)狄拉克自旋液体及其有隙的Z₂和手征（chiral）后代如何通过带电玻色子（chargon）的希格斯凝聚（Higgs condensation）转变为超导相和电荷有序相，并构建了涵盖d+id超导、电荷密度波、键密度波和配对密度波等丰富有序态的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角格子量子自旋液体绝缘体在压力或掺杂下会转变为超导态，并在中红外光驱动下表现出增强的太赫兹电导率，但缺乏统一的微观理论框架来解释这些转变。实验和数值研究已为多种自旋液体态提供证据，需要建立从自旋液体到常规有序态转变的一般理论。

Method: 作者提出一个一般性理论框架，从带有费米子自旋子（spinon）的U(1)狄拉克自旋液体出发，推导超导和电荷有序相的产生；利用投影对称群（projective symmetry group）唯一确定自旋子的对称性和色散关系，从而构建自旋子能带极小值附近的低能有效理论，并利用自旋子希格斯场的规范不变复合算符来表征各相的有序参数。

Result: 该框架导出的相图包含多种有序态，包括d+id超导性、电荷密度波（charge-density waves）、键密度波（bond-density waves）和配对密度波（pair-density waves）。自旋液体相拥有分数化狄拉克自旋子与涌现规范场，而超导和电荷有序相均为常规相，不包含分数化激发或涌现规范动力学。

Conclusion: 三角格子自旋液体向超导和电荷有序相的转变可由自旋液体中无自旋、携带电荷e的玻色子自旋子（即“双占据子”doublons和“空穴子”holons）的希格斯凝聚统一驱动，其对称性和色散关系由狄拉克自旋子的投影对称群唯一确定，为该类转变提供了普适的理论基础。

Abstract: Triangular-lattice quantum spin-liquid insulators are observed to undergo transitions to superconductivity under pressure or doping, and exhibit enhanced terahertz conductivity when driven by mid-infrared light. We present a general theoretical framework for the emergence of superconducting and charge-ordered phases from a U(1) Dirac spin liquid with fermionic spinons, as well as from its gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and chiral descendants. Numerical studies have provided substantial evidence for these spin-liquid states.
  The spin-liquid phase hosts fractionalized Dirac spinons coupled to an emergent gauge field, whereas the superconducting and charge-ordered phases are conventional, with neither fractionalized excitations nor emergent gauge dynamics. The transition between these phases is driven by the Higgs condensation of spinless charge-$e$ bosonic chargons (``doublons'' and ``holons''). We show that the projective symmetry group of the Dirac spinons uniquely determines the symmetry and dispersion of the chargons, allowing us to construct an effective low-energy theory near the chargon band minima. Gauge-invariant composites of the chargon Higgs fields provide the order parameters characterizing the phases. The resulting phase diagram contains a rich variety of ordered states, including $d+id$ superconductivity, charge-density waves, bond-density waves, and pair-density waves.

</details>


### [95] [Self-dual $S_3$ gauge theory in 2+1d: lattice model and topological phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05294)
*Da-Chuan Lu,Chong Wang,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个非阿贝尔推广的电磁自对偶格点模型：$S_3$量子双$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$的格点模型，其任意子置换对称性$\mathbb{Z}^{\mathrm{em}}_2$通过格点平移实现。该模型的锯齿边界无需微调即可实现由四临界伊辛共形场论（tetracritical Ising CFT）描述的无质量临界边态，且体态中三个独立扰动驱动系统进入不同的能隙相。三类独立方法（范畴论任意子凝聚、微观格点哈密顿量和Chern-Simons-Higgs理论）给出的结果一致，并提炼出最小凝聚原理。该构造可推广到无穷族自对偶二面体量子双$\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$，且模型无符号问题（sign-problem-free），便于大规模数值研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathbb{Z}_2$规范理论的电磁自对偶是精确格点实现的对偶对称性的典范示例，具有深远影响；本文旨在构造其首个非阿贝尔推广——即$S_3$量子双$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$的格点模型，在该模型中任意子置换对称性通过格点平移实现，以探索非阿贝尔对偶对称性的格点实现及其相变行为。

Method: 构造$S_3$量子双$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$的张量积希尔伯特空间格点模型，其中$\mathbb{Z}^{\mathrm{em}}_2$任意子置换对称性（交换非阿贝尔电荷子$C$和通量子$F$）通过格点平移实现；采用三种独立方法分析体态相变：范畴论中的任意子凝聚、微观格点哈密顿量以及Chern-Simons-Higgs理论；并将构造推广至无穷族二面体量子双$\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$。

Result: 发现锯齿边界终止无需微调即可实现由四临界伊辛CFT描述的无质量临界边态；体态中存在三个独立的保持$\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$的玻色型微扰，驱动$\mathcal{D}(S_3)$进入不同的能隙相，三种独立分析方法结果彼此一致；提出最小凝聚原理：增殖某个玻色任意子通常导致包含它的最小可凝聚代数凝聚，与此相关的凝聚态作为简并真空出现并自发破缺任意子置换对称性；所有模型均无符号问题。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非阿贝尔量子双电磁自对偶的格点实现，验证了最小凝聚原理的统一图像，并将构造推广到无穷族自对偶二面体量子双$\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$。由于模型无符号问题，为大规模数值探索非阿贝尔Chern-Simons-Higgs理论的相结构开辟了道路。

Abstract: Electric-magnetic self-duality of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, realized microscopically as a half-lattice-translation exchanging electric charge and magnetic flux, has been an influential example of a duality symmetry with an exact lattice realization. We construct the first non-Abelian generalization of this construction: a lattice model of the $S_3$ quantum double $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ on a tensor product Hilbert space in which the $\mathbb{Z}^{\mathrm{em}}_2$ anyon-permutation symmetry, exchanging the non-Abelian chargeon $C$ and fluxon $F$, is realized via lattice translation. Consequently we find that the zigzag boundary termination of the model realizes, without fine-tuning, a gapless critical edge state described by the tetracritical Ising CFT. The bulk admits three independent $\mathbb{Z}_2^{\mathrm{em}}$-preserving bosonic perturbations, driving $\mathcal{D}(S_3)$ into distinct gapped phases. We analyze these transitions by three independent methods: category-theoretic anyon condensation, microscopic lattice Hamiltonians, and Chern-Simons-Higgs theory, which all agree, yielding a unified picture. These examples motivate a minimal-condensation principle: proliferating a bosonic anyon generically drives condensation of a minimal condensable algebra containing it, with symmetry-related condensates appearing as degenerate vacua that spontaneously break the anyon-permutation symmetry. Our model construction extends to an infinite family of self-dual dihedral quantum doubles $\mathcal{D}(D_{2n})$.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 研究发现Hund耦合的自旋翻转和Ising型分量能够生成局域自旋激发（LSEs），这些LSEs在轨道间耦合电子，重整化准粒子寿命和束缚能，进而破坏OSMP中明确定义的准粒子。移除这两个分量后，准粒子相干行为得以恢复，两个能带的电荷动力学完全退耦。

Conclusion: 电子与局域自旋激发（LSEs）的耦合是驱动OSMP内准粒子失效的根本机制，这一发现超越了传统的局域化/巡游电子共存的静态图像，为理解关联电子体系中的准粒子破坏提供了微观层面的理论解释。

Abstract: The orbital-selective Mott phase (OSMP) is commonly described as a coexistence of localized and itinerant electrons within effectively decoupled orbitals, but emerging evidence for quasiparticle breakdown points to physics beyond this picture, whose microscopic origin remains unknown.
