These LSEs couple electrons between different orbitals, renormalize quasiparticle lifetimes and binding energies, and thereby destroy well-defined quasiparticles in the OSMP. Removing these two components of Hund's coupling restores coherent quasiparticle behavior and fully decouples the charge dynamics of the two bands. Our results therefore identify electronic coupling to LSEs as the fundamental mechanism driving quasiparticle breakdown within the OSMP.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: EuRhGe$_3$为螺旋磁体中完整的磁场诱导结构演化序列——从圆螺旋经孤子晶格到自旋翻转xyz-扇形（椭圆圆锥）最后到平面xy-扇形——提供了原型（prototypical）实验示例，与理论预言一致。

Abstract: The magnetic structure of EuRhGe$_3$, a noncentrosymmetric body-centered tetragonal magnet with the space group $I4mm$, has been investigated by resonant X-ray diffraction. Below $T_{\text{N}}=12$ K, EuRhGe$_3$ undergoes a helical magnetic ordering with an incommensurate propagation vector $q=(0, 0, 0.809)$, in which the magnetic moments lie in the $ab$ plane and rotate by a constant turn angle of $145.8^{\circ}$ between adjacent layers. When a magnetic field is applied along the $a$ axis at 2 K, a second-harmonic $2q$ peak develops, indicating that the circular helix is gradually distorted into a helimagnetic soliton-lattice state, which eventually undergoes a lock-in transition to the commensurate structure with $q=0.8$ at 3.8 T. Above the subsequent phase boundary at 5 T, the helicity is lost, and a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical) state is realized, in which the moments oscillate predominantly along the $b$ axis but are accompanied by a small $c$-axis component. At higher fields, the system enters a conventional planar $xy$-fan phase without a $c$-axis component. EuRhGe$_3$ provides a prototypical example of a helimagnet that exhibits a full sequence of field-induced structures, evolving from a circular helix to a spin-flop $xyz$-fan (elliptic conical), and finally to a planar $xy$-fan structure, which has been theoretically predicted.

</details>


### [99] [Correlated topological-polarization surface states in the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05887)
*Takahiro Iwagaki,Hideki Matsuoka,Ginta Hoshino,Kanata Watanabe,Shungo Aoyagi,Shunsuke Kitou,Yuiga Nakamura,Motoaki Hirayama,Takashi Koretsune,Naoya Kanazawa*

TL;DR: 本研究在窄带隙绝缘体FeSb2外延薄膜中实验证明了拓扑极化（topological polarization）起源的金属极性表面态，其受体相强关联电子调控，并展示了该关联拓扑系统中的体-边对应关系（bulk-edge correspondence），以及通过静电门控驱动表面态进入铁磁或可能的交变磁（altermagnetic）量子态的量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联（strong electron correlations）与能带拓扑（band topology）各自能产生丰富的量子相，但由于二者对元素要求相互冲突，通常难以在同一材料中共存。作者希望寻找一种途径将二者统一，从而将能带拓扑拓展到关联的3d过渡金属化合物中。

Method: 作者制备了窄带隙绝缘体FeSb2的外延薄膜（epitaxial thin films），对其表面电子态进行表征，确认其金属极性表面态源于拓扑极化机制。通过调控温度观察非互易表面输运（nonreciprocal surface transport）的出现与体相Fe 3d轨道占据关联驱动重构的onset温度之间的关系，并利用静电门控（electrostatic gating）驱动关联表面发生量子相变。

Result: FeSb2外延薄膜中存在拓扑极化起源的金属极性表面态，其受体相强关联调控。非互易表面输运仅在体相Fe 3d轨道占据发生关联驱动重构的onset温度以下出现，直接证实了关联拓扑系统中的体-边对应关系。静电门控可将该关联表面驱动跨越量子相变，进入铁磁（ferromagnetic）或可能的交变磁态。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了拓扑极化作为一类广泛的关联拓扑相（correlated topological phases）设计原则，为在更多材料中实现关联与拓扑的统一提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Strong electron correlations and band topology each generate rich quantum phases, but conflicting elemental requirements have largely kept them apart. Topological polarization offers a route to unite them, producing polar surface states from bonding charge without spin-orbit coupling and thereby extending band topology to correlated 3d transition-metal compounds. Here we demonstrate that epitaxial thin films of the narrow-gap insulator FeSb2 host metallic polar surface states of topological-polarization origin, governed by the strong correlations of the bulk. Nonreciprocal surface transport emerges only below the onset temperature of a correlation-driven reconstruction of the bulk Fe 3d orbital occupation, providing direct evidence of bulk-edge correspondence in a correlated topological system. Moreover, electrostatic gating drives this correlated surface across a quantum phase transition into a ferromagnetic or possibly altermagnetic state. Our results establish topological polarization as a design principle for correlated topological phases in a broad range of materials.

</details>


### [100] [Landscape of incompressible crystals of hard-core bosons on the square-kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05927)
*Pratyay Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用超越常规单胞描述的分层平均场理论（hierarchical mean-field theory），研究方卡戈米晶格（square-kagome lattice）上的硬核玻色子模型，发现除已有报道的 $5/6$ 和 $2/3$ 密度不可压缩相外，还存在由扩大变分团簇所揭示的额外不可压缩态，其中 $\rho=3/4$ 相最为稳健，对应自旋-$1/2$ XXZ 模型中的半磁化平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方卡戈米晶格的常规最小单胞描述无法完全刻画该晶格所支持的不可压缩态层级结构，仅能捕获两种基于紧致局域态（compact localized state）的相。作者旨在超越此局限，揭示更丰富的量子相结构，并为实验材料提供理论预测。

Method: 采用超越常规单胞描述的分层平均场理论，通过扩大变分团簇来探索额外的不可压缩态。利用松原-松田映射（Matsubara-Matsuda mapping）将玻色子模型映射至自旋-$1/2$ XXZ 模型以解释磁化平台。对两种实验相关的方卡戈米化合物使用基于第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）得到的交换参数进行具体应用。

Result: 该方法重现了已知的 $5/6$ 与 $2/3$ 密度相，并发现了由扩大变分团簇所揭示的额外不可压缩态。其中 $\rho=3/4$ 相特别稳健，经松原-松田映射后对应自旋-$1/2$ XXZ 模型的半磁化平台。对两种方卡戈米化合物的计算磁化过程与现有实验结果吻合良好，并预测了这些材料中的额外平台结构。

Conclusion: 超越常规单胞描述的分层方法对于完整刻画方卡戈米晶格上硬核玻色子模型的不可压缩态层级至关重要。$\rho=3/4$ 相的存在及其对应的半磁化平台为相关方卡戈米化合物提供了可验证的实验预言，理论计算与实验的一致性表明该方法对真实材料具有预测能力。

Abstract: We investigate a hard-core boson model on the square-kagome lattice using hierarchical mean-field theory beyond the conventional unit-cell description. We show that the conventional description of the square-kagome lattice based on its smallest unit cell does not fully capture the hierarchy of incompressible states supported by the lattice, but only captures the two compact-localized-state-based phases at densities $5/6$ and $2/3$. Within our approach, we reproduce these previously established phases and uncover additional incompressible states enabled by enlarging the variational cluster. Among these, the $ρ=3/4$ phase is found to be particularly robust, which, through the Matsubara-Matsuda mapping, corresponds to a half-magnetization plateau in the spin-$1/2$ XXZ model. We further apply our approach to two experimentally relevant square-kagome compounds using exchange parameters obtained from first-principles calculations. The calculated magnetization processes are in good agreement with available experimental results and predict additional plateau structures in these materials.

</details>


### [101] [Dissipation-induced bulk and boundary criticality in the Haldane chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05984)
*Zhenjiu Wang,Manuel Weber*

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）模拟研究耦合到耗散欧姆浴（dissipative ohmic bath）的 Haldane 链，识别出一个由相互作用不动点（interacting fixed point）控制、动力学指数 z≈2 的二级量子相变，进入自发破缺 SO(3) 对称性的反铁磁态。文章推导了广义弦序参量（generalized string order parameter），表明弱耗散下 Haldane 链的对称性保护拓扑基态稳定，其在临界点出现非平庸标度维数；拓扑边缘模显示出与平庸态等价转变不同的边界临界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索耗散对对称性保护拓扑（symmetry-protected topological, SPT）态的影响，尤其是 Haldane 链在耦合到欧姆浴时其拓扑相和临界行为的性质。

Method: 采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究相变行为；推导广义弦序参量表征 SPT 基态的稳定性与临界标度；对平庸态转变进行耗散 φ⁴ 理论的精确 ε 展开（ε expansion），并与 Haldane 链的边界临界性进行对比。

Result: 发现二级量子相变进入自发破缺 SO(3) 对称性的反铁磁态，由相互作用不动点控制且动力学指数 z≈2；弱耗散下 SPT 基态稳定，其序参量在临界点具有非平庸标度维数；Haldane 链的拓扑边缘模表现出与平庸态转变不同的非平庸边界临界性。

Conclusion: 该体系实现了临界但非共形（nonconformal）反铁磁体中的自旋杂质，为通过耗散连续调控边界临界性（boundary criticality）开辟了途径。

Abstract: We study the Haldane chain coupled to a dissipative ohmic bath. Using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we identify a second-order quantum phase transition into an antiferromagnetic state with spontaneously broken SO(3) symmetry that is governed by an interacting fixed point with dynamical exponent $z\approx 2$. We derive a generalized string order parameter which indicates that the symmetry-protected topological ground state of the Haldane chain is stable for weak dissipation and develops a nontrivial scaling dimension at criticality. In particular, its topological edge modes display nontrivial boundary criticality that is distinct from an equivalent transition out of a trivial state; for the latter, we perform an accurate $ε$ expansion of a dissipative $φ^4$ theory with ordinary boundary conditions. Our setup realizes a spin impurity in a critical yet nonconformal antiferromagnet and paves the way for continuously tunable boundary criticality controlled by dissipation.

</details>


### [102] [Resilient strange metal at an unconventional quantum critical point in $d=2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05988)
*Jérôme Leblanc,A. -M. S. Tremblay*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用非微扰改进的两粒子自洽（non-perturbative improved two-particle self-consistent）方法，预测二维哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）在量子临界点附近的临界指数和奇异金属行为。研究发现，即使相互作用强度低于莫特转变（Mott transition），在费米面上的Kohn异常仍可导致非常规临界指数，且电阻率随温度线性变化可以在自能沿费米面不呈线性依赖的情况下出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷原子实验和图解量子蒙特卡洛（diagrammatic quantum Monte Carlo）方法正在研究具有最近邻跳跃的二维哈伯德模型的性质，因此有必要对奇异金属行为及其与量子临界性质的关系做出理论预测，特别是针对费米液体与不相称自旋密度波序之间的量子临界点。

Method: 采用非微扰改进的两粒子自洽方法（improved two-particle self-consistent approach）进行理论预测，该方法不依赖于微扰展开，能够处理相互作用强度低于莫特转变区域的中间耦合情形。

Result: 当关联长度（correlation length）从约一个到一百个晶格间距时，费米面上的Kohn异常导致非常规临界指数；单粒子自能（self-energy）的温度依赖沿费米面呈现强烈动量依赖，且没有清晰的朗道准粒子（Landau quasiparticle）证据。尽管如此，观测到电阻率随温度线性标度（$T\rightarrow 0$）的奇异金属（strange-metal）行为。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，线性温度依赖的电阻率可以在自能沿费米面不具有线性温度依赖的情况下出现，这与通常的假设（即电阻率线性来源于自能线性）不同，为理解奇异金属行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Properties of the two-dimensional Hubbard model with nearest-neighbor hopping are under scrutiny in cold-atom experiments and diagrammatic quantum Monte Carlo. Given the controlled nature of these approaches, it is timely to make predictions about strange-metal behavior and its relation to quantum-critical properties. Even for interaction strengths below the Mott transition, several peculiarities occur at the quantum critical point separating Fermi liquid and incommensurate spin-density wave order. Here, we predict, using the non-perturbative improved two-particle self-consistent approach, that for correlation lengths ranging from about one to one hundred lattice spacings, Kohn anomalies on the underlying Fermi surface lead to unconventional critical exponents. The temperature dependence of the single-particle self-energy acquires strong momentum dependence along the Fermi surface with no clear evidence of Landau quasiparticles. Nevertheless, we observe strange-metal behavior, namely, resistivity that scales linearly with temperature as $T\rightarrow{0}$. Our work shows that a linear temperature dependence of resistivity arises without a linear-in-temperature self-energy along the Fermi surface, as is often assumed.

</details>


### [103] [Neural Flux Attachment: From Bose Condensates to Chiral Topological Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06168)
*Rudik Badalyan,Khachatur G. Nazaryan,Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出ChernFormer，一种结合费米子transformer与固定Chern-Simons因子（为每对粒子添加一个统计涡旋）的变分波函数架构。其乘积精确满足玻色子交换对称性，使得玻色子学习问题等价于费米子问题。实验表明它能在同一框架下同时描述玻色凝聚态和手性拓扑液体（如Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态及其手性边缘态）。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经波函数（neural wave function）方法通常分别处理常规玻色有序态和手性拓扑态，缺乏统一的变分语言。作者希望提出一种单一架构，同时刻画玻色凝聚体与手性拓扑液体两种截然不同的物理现象。

Method: ChernFormer由费米子transformer与一个固定的Chern-Simons相位组合而成，该相位为每一对粒子附加一个统计涡旋；两个因子在粒子交换下各自改变符号，乘积保持玻色子性。固定相位仅改变统计而不影响概率，因此玻色子学习问题可映射为等误差的费米子问题。网络具有足够容量时，可近似平面上任意固定粒子数的可归一化玻色子波函数。虽然有限光滑网络在粒子相遇处波函数仍为零，但该接触孔（contact hole）可收缩，使波函数与凝聚分数趋近无节点凝聚体的结果。

Result: 在Nazaryan等人提出的"大海捞针"目标重建基准上，ChernFormer成功重构了Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态及其前两个手性边缘态。重叠曲线在最大采样尺寸下仍接近1；在N=20时，独立的振幅与相位图同时恢复了局部Laughlin涡旋和集体边缘涡旋。相比之下，连续的、非零的相同粒子因子乘积无法捕捉这些基本边缘扇区。

Conclusion: ChernFormer提供了一种统一的变分语言，可同时描述常规玻色序与手性拓扑物质，填补了神经波函数方法在面对两种不同物相时的架构空白。

Abstract: Can one neural wave function describe both a Bose condensate and a chiral topological liquid? We introduce ChernFormer, which combines a fermionic transformer with a fixed Chern-Simons phase that attaches one statistical vortex to every particle pair. Each factor changes sign under exchange, so their product is exactly bosonic. The fixed phase changes statistics but not probability, making every bosonic learning problem equivalent to a fermionic one with the same approximation error and overlap. With enough capacity, ChernFormer can approximate any normalizable bosonic wave function on the plane at fixed particle number. A finite, smooth network still vanishes when particles meet, yet this contact hole can shrink while the wave function and condensate fraction approach those of a nodeless condensate. Following the needle-in-a-haystack target-reconstruction benchmark introduced in \cite{NazaryanGaggioliTengFu2025}, we test ChernFormer on the Kalmeyer--Laughlin ground state and its first two chiral edge states. The overlap curves stay close to unity through their largest sampled sizes, while independent amplitude and phase maps at $N=20$ for all three states recover both local Laughlin vortices and the collective edge vortex. By contrast, a continuous, nonzero product of identical particle-wise factors misses these elementary edge sectors. ChernFormer therefore provides one variational language for conventional bosonic order and chiral topological matter.

</details>


### [104] [Phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06357)
*Luis Walther,Alberto Nocera,Mona Berciu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）方法，计算了低浓度掺杂（x≤0.15）一维无自旋Holstein模型中声子谱函数的电子-声子耦合依赖关系。研究发现声子谱权重向裸声子能量Ω上下方显著转移，与Migdal极限下Kohn反常现象预期相反。该行为可由随机相位近似（Random Phase Approximation, RPA）定性捕捉，并由"修饰RPA"方案半定量复现，该方案中电子增广传播子通过低密度极化子的动量平均（Momentum Average, MA）近似重整化，而其最低阶顶点修正会产生非物理的负谱权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对Holstein模型声子谱的研究主要集中于单极化子极限（x→0），而低浓度有限掺杂区间（x≤0.15）的声子谱函数尚缺乏直接数值计算结果。该区间对应于弱掺杂绝缘体中的低密度极化子金属（polaron metal），具有重要的物理意义。

Method: 采用DMRG方法精确计算一维无自旋Holstein模型在低浓度掺杂下的声子谱函数。同时提出"修饰RPA"方案：利用MA近似重整化电子增广传播子，在此基础上构建RPA框架；并将该方案与含最低阶顶点修正的方案进行对比验证。

Result: 发现显著声子谱权重同时转移到ω=0以下和裸声子能量Ω以上，与Migdal极限下Kohn反常（kink at q=2k_F）的预期形成鲜明对比，且数值结果中未出现2k_F扭折信号。修饰RPA方案以极小额外计算成本半定量复现DMRG结果，而加入最低阶顶点修正后产生非物理负谱权重。

Conclusion: 修饰RPA为低密度极化子金属的声子谱函数提供了高效近似方案，适用于弱掺杂绝缘体相关物理体系。结果同时表明，当传播子经过非微扰重整化后，顶点修正与传播子修饰必须保持一致处理，否则会产生非物理结果。

Abstract: We use the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) to compute the phonon spectral function of a one-dimensional spinless Holstein model doped with a low but finite carrier concentration, $x \leq 0.15$, as a function of the electron-phonon coupling $λ$. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first such results in this regime, complementing extensive prior work at the single-polaron level ($x\to 0$). We find that significant phonon spectral weight is transferred both below, all the way down to $ω=0$, and above the bare phonon energy $Ω$, in stark contrast with the Kohn-anomaly phenomenology expected in the Migdal limit, where weight remains centered near $Ω$ with a kink at $q=2k_F$. No signature of this $2k_F$ kink appears in our results. This behavior is captured qualitatively by the Random Phase Approximation (RPA), and semi-quantitatively, at negligible extra computational cost, by a ``dressed RPA'' scheme in which the electron addition propagator is renormalized using the Momentum Average (MA) approximation for the low-density electron-polaron. By contrast, adding the lowest-order vertex correction to this dressed scheme produces unphysical negative spectral weight, signaling that vertex and propagator dressings must be treated consistently once the propagators are dressed nonperturbatively. Our results provide an efficient approximation for the phonon spectral functions of low-density polaron metals, a regime relevant to weakly doped insulators.

</details>

### [105] [Living on the Edge of Effective Field Theory: Near-Extremal Black Holes in Quadratic Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05268)
*Kelvin Ka-Ho Lam,Gary T. Horowitz,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究近极端黑洞（near-extremal black hole）中高阶导数修正对引力有效场论（effective field theory）微扰可控性的放大效应。作者在具有动力学标量自由度的二次引力中，利用赝谱方法（pseudospectral method）构造快速旋转外部解，并分析趋向极端极限时两种理论的不同行为：动力学Chern-Simons引力可到达具有增强对称性的近极端解，而标量Gauss-Bonnet引力则在视界处出现对数奇异性并存在边界层，最后通过曲率不变量和潮汐力区分有效场论破缺的不同概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近极端黑洞能放大引力理论中的高阶导数修正，因此是检验引力有效场论微扰控制性的灵敏探针。作者希望厘清在不同理论框架（动力学Chern-Simons引力与标量Gauss-Bonnet引力）中趋向极端极限时，有效场论展开在何种意义上失效，从而为耦合常数的约束提供理论依据。

Method: 作者在具有动力学标量自由度的二次引力（quadratic gravity）框架下，考虑了动力学Chern-Simons和标量Gauss-Bonnet两种具体理论。利用赝谱方法，在固定表面引力（surface gravity）和固定视界角速度（horizon angular velocity）的条件下构造快速旋转外部解，并将这一受控的族向极端（extremal）极限推进。随后采用曲率不变量（curvature invariants）和潮汐力（tidal forces）来诊断微扰有效场论展开的失效。

Result: 在动力学Chern-Simons引力中，解列趋向于渐近平坦的极端外部解，其近视界极限与变形后的近极端Kerr几何一致，并继承了增强的SO(2,1)×U(1)对称性。在标量Gauss-Bonnet引力中，标量在视界处不可避免地出现对数奇异性，外部度规在视界附近形成边界层，且变形后的近极端解无法连接至正则渐近平坦外部解。此外，曲率不变量在视界处可能保持有限，而内落观测者测得的潮汐力却随表面引力的逆幂次发散。

Conclusion: 本文区分了极端极限附近有效场论破缺的不同概念：尺度不变的曲率不变量有限并不意味着潮汐测量不失效。研究提供了可用的微扰有效性估计（perturbative validity estimate），可用于与当前对动力学Chern-Simons和标量Gauss-Bonnet耦合常数的约束进行比较。

Abstract: Near-extremal black holes can amplify higher-derivative corrections to general relativity, making them sharp probes of the perturbative control of gravitational effective field theory. We study this question in quadratic gravity with a dynamical scalar degree of freedom, focusing on dynamical Chern-Simons and scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity. Using pseudospectral methods, we construct rapidly rotating exterior solutions at fixed surface gravity and fixed horizon angular velocity, and follow this controlled family toward extremality. In dynamical Chern-Simons gravity, the sequence approaches an asymptotically flat extremal exterior whose near-horizon limit agrees with the dynamical-Chern-Simons-deformed near-horizon extremal Kerr geometry and inherits its enhanced $SO(2,1)\times U(1)$ symmetry. In scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity, the same limiting procedure behaves differently: the scalar develops an unavoidable logarithmic singularity at the horizon, the exterior metric develops a small boundary layer near the horizon, and the scalar-Gauss-Bonnet-deformed near-horizon extremal solution is not connected to a regular asymptotically flat exterior. We then use curvature invariants and tidal forces to diagnose the breakdown of the perturbative effective-field-theory expansion. We find that scalar curvature invariants can remain finite at the horizon even when tidal forces measured by infalling observers diverge as inverse powers of the surface gravity. These results separate distinct notions of effective-field-theory breakdown near extremality and provide a perturbative validity estimate that can be compared with current bounds on the dynamical Chern-Simons and scalar-Gauss-Bonnet couplings.

</details>


### [106] [The glow of eternal black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05270)
*Vladimir Strokov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过光线追踪（ray tracing）计算了极扩展（eternal）史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）周围环绕几何薄吸积盘（geometrically thin accretion disk）时的光学外观，并用白洞/过去类光面替代过去奇点，得到一个位于阴影中心的亮斑，其干涉可见度（visibility）为基线长度的纯指数函数，与盘和光子环的幂律包络形成对比。作者进一步从M87*和Sgr A*的230 GHz通量约束了该表面位置的参数积，并讨论了普朗克星（Planck star）场景的可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天文物理解释黑洞影（shadow）依赖于事件视界（event horizon）捕获光线，但极扩展黑洞解包含白洞（white hole）区域和过去奇点（past singularity），需要检验这类结构是否与观测到的黑洞阴影一致，从而区分黑洞是否为永恒（eternal）解。

Method: 采用Markov关于物质密度上限的假说，将过去奇点替换为类空“最后散射面”（surface of last scattering）$r=r_{\rm m}$，假设其发射温度$T$的黑体辐射；利用光线追踪计算穿过过去视界（past horizon）的光线在该面上的终止点，生成盘与亮斑的图像，并推导亮斑的闭合形式干涉特征（closed-form interferometric signature）。

Result: 阴影中心出现一个亮斑（bright spot），其径向分布由引力频移（gravitational frequency shift）决定；当散射面足够靠近奇点时，斑心发生蓝移（blueshifted）。亮斑的干涉可见度为基线长度（baseline length）的纯指数衰减函数，而盘与光子环的包络为幂律（power-law）形式。从M87*和Sgr A*的230 GHz观测通量可约束$r_{\rm m}T$；基于量纲分析确定$r_{\rm m}$的普朗克星场景则超出任何现有或计划中的设施可探测范围。

Conclusion: 观测到的阴影黑暗程度本身仍可视为对黑洞是否为永恒解的检验；当前观测尚未排除极扩展黑洞中亮斑的存在，但其在普朗克星极限下不可探测。

Abstract: We compute the optical appearance of a maximally extended (eternal) Schwarzschild black hole surrounded by a geometrically thin accretion disk. Unlike astrophysical black holes formed by gravitational collapse, the eternal solution contains a white hole (WH) region connected to a past singularity. Following Markov's hypothesis of a limiting density of matter, we replace the past singularity with a spacelike "surface of last scattering" at $r=r_{\rm m}$ and assume that it emits black-body radiation with temperature $T$. Past-directed rays that cross the past horizon terminate on this surface, resulting in a bright spot at the center of the shadow of an eternal black hole. The radial profile of the spot is set by the gravitational frequency shift, with a blueshifted center when the spacelike surface is sufficiently close to the singularity. We use ray tracing to generate an image of the disk and the spot, and we derive a closed-form interferometric signature of the spot. Its visibility is a pure exponential in the baseline length, in contrast to the power-law envelopes of the disk and the photon ring. We constrain the product $r_{\rm m} T$ from the observed 230 GHz fluxes of M87* and Sgr A*. We also consider a Planck-star scenario in which the value of $r_{\rm m}$ is determined on dimensional grounds, and show that it is out of reach of any current or planned facilities. Nevertheless, the darkness of the observed shadows remains a test of whether these black holes are eternal.

</details>


### [107] [Absorption spectrum and greybody factors of charged black holes in loop quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05292)
*Marco A. A. de Paula,Valdir B. Bezerra,Luiz C. S. Leite*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文数值研究了带电荷圈量子引力（Loop Quantum Gravity, LQG）黑洞对无质量标量场的吸收特性，计算了任意入射频率下的吸收截面（absorption cross section, ACS）。结果表明：量子参数增加时总ACS的峰值与谷值呈现相反变化趋势；电荷质量比增大时ACS减小（与量子参数的影响相反）；数值结果与经典和半经典近似在相应极限下高度吻合，并进一步研究了灰体因子（greybody factors）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圈量子引力框架下无奇点黑洞以其反弹半径替代经典奇点，成为探索LQG在黑洞物理中印记的潜在场景。虽然这类黑洞附近的标量微扰已有研究，但吸收谱尚未被详细分析，本文旨在填补这一空白，深入理解量子参数与电荷参数在黑洞时空吸收特性中所扮演的角色。

Method: 采用数值方法，对带电荷LQG黑洞时空中的无质量标量场任意入射频率下的吸收截面进行求解计算，并与Reissner-Nordström（RN）黑洞进行比较，同时在相应极限下与已知的经典和半经典近似进行对照验证。

Result: 发现量子参数增大时，总吸收截面的峰与谷呈现相反行为，即最高峰对应最深谷；电荷质量比增大时吸收截面下降，这与改变量子参数时吸收谱的行为形成鲜明对比；还识别出LQG与RN黑洞具有相同吸收性质的参数情形，并在极限范围内与经典及半经典近似结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 本研究系统刻画了带电荷LQG黑洞的标量场吸收特性，为理解LQG启发黑洞的吸收性质迈出了第一步，并揭示了量子参数与电荷参数对吸收谱的不同影响机制。

Abstract: In the last few decades, singularity-free black holes (BHs) obtained in the framework of Loop Quantum Gravity (LQG) have gained attention in the literature. These compact objects replace the classical singularity with a transition hypersurface called the bounce radius and stand out as potential scenarios for exploring the imprints of LQG in BH physics. Although scalar perturbations in the vicinity of LQG-based BHs are currently being studied, the absorption spectrum has not yet been analyzed in detail. In this work, we present an in-depth investigation of the absorption properties of massless test scalar fields by a charged LQG BH, aiming to better understand the role played by the quantum and charge parameters of the BH spacetime. Using a numerical approach, we compute the absorption cross section (ACS) of the massless scalar wave for arbitrary values of the frequency of the incident wave. We find that the behavior of the ACS as we increase the quantum parameter indicates that the peaks and troughs of the total ACS exhibit opposite behaviors, i.e., the curve related to the highest peak corresponds to the deepest troughs. Moreover, we show that the ACS decreases as we consider higher values of the BH charge-to-mass ratio. This is in stark contrast to the behavior of the absorption spectrum as we vary the quantum parameter. We also draw comparisons with the Reissner-Nordstrom (RN) BH, exploring the situations where LQG and RN BHs can have the same absorption properties. Furthermore, we find excellent agreement between our numerical results and the well-known classical and semiclassical approximations for the total ACS in their corresponding limits. For completeness, we also investigate the greybody factors. Our results can be viewed as a first step toward a better understanding of the absorption properties of LQG-inspired BHs.

</details>


### [108] [Charged Penrose Extraction in RN--AdS Black Holes with Dark Energy: Ergosphere Geometry and Rigorous Efficiency Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05641)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,M. Zeyauddin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了Reissner–Nordström反德西特时空中由Kiselev型各向异性暗能量包围的带电Penrose提取过程的定量分析框架，证明了外视界与负能轨迹转折点的唯一性，推导了逃逸能量上界和有限半径接收判据，并引入了绝热放电与吸积定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有暗能量背景的Reissner–Nordström AdS黑洞中，带电Penrose提取（charged Penrose extraction）的动力学受多种参数影响，缺乏系统性定量描述；现有研究未严格区分局部运动学约束与背景反作用、辐射损失及近极端稳定性效应。

Method: 定义广义静电能层（generalized electrostatic ergosphere）为红移与静电功平衡的边界；在lapse函数与静电势的单调性假设下证明外能层边界及负能轨迹转折点的唯一性；推导局部逃逸能量上界，结合AdS渐近形式给出有限半径接收判据；引入一阶绝热放电与吸积定律以追踪缓慢演化的视界与能层。

Result: 获得了关于质量、电荷、宇宙学常数、Kiselev归一化因子及状态参数的允许提取域映射；给出了受控的解析与数值诊断结果，并明确了局部运动学边界与背景反作用、辐射损失和近极端稳定性效应之间的区分。

Conclusion: 该框架为RN–AdS背景暗能量环境下的带电Penrose提取提供了系统性的定量工具，但需在后续工作中将辐射与稳定性效应纳入完整一致性处理。

Abstract: We develop a quantitative framework for charge assisted Penrose extraction in Reissner--Nordström Anti-de Sitter black holes surrounded by a Kiselev type anisotropic dark energy sector. The generalized electrostatic ergosphere is defined by the balance between redshift and electrostatic work. Under explicitly stated monotonicity assumptions on the lapse and electrostatic potential, we prove uniqueness of the exterior ergosphere boundary and of the turning point of negative energy trajectories. We derive a local upper bound on the escaping energy, formulate a finite radius reception criterion appropriate to AdS asymptotics, and map the allowed extraction domains in terms of the mass, charge, cosmological constant, Kiselev normalization, and state parameter. We also introduce first order adiabatic discharge and accretion laws for slowly varying mass and charge, clarifying the regime in which the evolving horizon and ergosphere can be tracked consistently. The results provide controlled analytic and numerical diagnostics for charged Penrose extraction in RN--AdS backgrounds with a dark energy environment, while clearly separating local kinematic bounds from backreaction, radiative losses, and near extremal stability effects.

</details>


### [109] [Kinematic Bounds on Charged Energy Extraction in the Purely Electric Branch of Euler--Heisenberg Type f(R,T) Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05644)
*Anirudh Pradhan,K. Ghaderi,M. Zeyauddin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导并检验了静止f(R,T)黑洞族（由欧拉-海森堡型非线性电磁场作为源）在纯电荷分支中的带电能量提取运动学上限，发现分支参考的视界势能控制局部上包络，而渐近结构决定外行轨迹能否到达无穷远、宇宙视界或仅到达有限外转折点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论框架下的黑洞能量提取研究中，f(R,T)引力与非线性电磁场耦合的静态带电黑洞缺乏系统的能量提取上界分析，尤其是考虑外部探测粒子具有非零角动量和径向速度的一般情形。

Method: 在固定背景上对外部最小耦合探测粒子进行运动学分析，推导保留非零角动量和径向速度的通用局部上界；采用随动分解（co-moving split）作为局部四动量守恒基准；利用双根条件F=F'=0对视界分类，区分渐近平坦、de Sitter和anti-de Sitter分支，并施加全局外向可达性判据。

Result: 零角动量转折点选择并非一般衰减配置，而是上包络；在de Sitter静态补丁中静电能量相对于宇宙视界参考，相关尺度为Q(1/r_+-1/r_c)而非Q/r_+；对比扫描表明分支参考视界势控制局部上包络，渐近结构决定外行轨迹的最终可达区域。

Conclusion: 能量提取上限由分支参考的视界势主导，而渐近时空结构（无穷远、宇宙视界或有限外转折点）决定提取粒子的最终去向，为f(R,T)引力框架下带电黑洞能量提取提供了统一运动学描述。

Abstract: We derive and test kinematic upper bounds on charged energy extraction in the purely electric branch of a static $f(R,T)$ black hole family sourced by an Euler--Heisenberg type nonlinear electromagnetic sector. The analysis is restricted to external, minimally coupled probe particles on the fixed background. In this electric solution the field remains Coulombic up to an additive gauge constant; the nonlinear electromagnetic and matter-coupling coefficients therefore affect extraction indirectly through the metric, the event-horizon position, and the global causal structure. We obtain a general local bound retaining nonzero angular momentum and radial velocity of the negative energy fragment, and identify the commonly used zero angular momentum turning-point choice as an upper envelope rather than a generic decay configuration. A co-moving split supplies a locally four momentum-conserving benchmark. We classify horizons with the double root conditions $\mathcal F=\mathcal F'=0$, distinguish asymptotically flat, de Sitter, and anti-de Sitter branches, and impose the corresponding global outward-accessibility criterion. In the de Sitter static patch the electrostatic energy is referenced to the cosmological horizon, so the relevant scale is $Q(1/r_+-1/r_c)$ rather than $Q/r_+$. Reproducible scans compare Reissner--Nordström, Einstein--Euler--Heisenberg type, $f(R,T)$--Maxwell, and full $f(R,T)$--Euler--Heisenberg type backgrounds. The results show that the branch-referenced horizon potential controls the local upper envelope, whereas the asymptotic structure determines whether an outward trajectory can reach infinity, a cosmological horizon, or only a finite outer turning point.

</details>


### [110] [Constructing the canonical harmonic coordinates of Kerr metric to the fourth post-Minkowskian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05713)
*Zizheng Xing,Xiaokai He,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在后闵可夫斯基（post-Minkowskian, PM）框架下构造了Kerr度规的第四阶后闵可夫斯基（4PM）规范调和坐标（canonical harmonic coordinates），利用Geroch-Hansen矩和Gürsel定理导出了无规范矩的精确规范MPM矩，并迭代计算了4PM阶的规范度规及完整度规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多极后闵可夫斯基（multipolar post-Minkowskian, MPM）框架中，Kerr度规的调和坐标表示通常包含规范矩（gauge moments），使得度规形式不够"纯净"。作者旨在构造一种完全无规范矩的"规范坐标"表示，以获得最纯粹的Kerr度规形式。

Method: 基于已知的Kerr度规Geroch-Hansen矩和Gürsel定理，推导精确的规范MPM矩M_L和S_L（不含任何规范矩）；利用这些矩迭代计算到4PM阶的gothic度规微扰h^{μν}_{can}及4PM规范度规g^{μν}_{can}。同时提取Jiang-Lin坐标下1PM源矩，与规范表示进行对比；Schwarzschild情形则计算到所有PM阶。

Result: 规范调和坐标下的度规空间分量和时间分量为自旋参数a的偶函数，混合分量为奇函数，这一奇偶性将规范坐标与其他调和坐标区分开来；对比发现Jiang-Lin表示从1PM阶起就存在非零规范矩，而规范表示在所有阶都无规范矩；Damgaard等人的动量空间递归独立构造在4PM阶给出等价结果（以a的幂级数表示），交叉验证了闭式4PM规范度规的准确性。

Conclusion: 规范坐标是MPM框架下Kerr度规最"规范纯净"（gauge-pure）的调和坐标表示；Schwarzschild情形的完整规范度规已计算到所有PM阶；将规范Kerr坐标与已知调和Kerr坐标联系起来的坐标变换仍是未解问题。

Abstract: In this paper we construct the canonical harmonic coordinates of the Kerr metric within the multipolar post-Minkowskian (MPM) formalism to the fourth post-Minkowskian (4PM) order. Based on the well known Geroch--Hansen moments of Kerr metric and Gürsel's theorem, we derive the exact canonical MPM moments $\mathrm{M}_L,\mathrm{S}_L$, which are free of any gauge moments. With these moments, we iteratively compute the gothic metric perturbation $h^{μν}_{\mathrm{can}}$ up to 4PM order and compute the 4PM canonical metric $g_{μν}^{\mathrm{can}}$. The resulting spatial and time components of these metrics are even functions of the spin parameter $a$ while the mixed components are odd. This parity property distinguishes the canonical coordinates from other harmonic coordinates. To contrast this minimal-gauge construction, we also extract the 1PM source moments of the Kerr metric in the Jiang--Lin coordinates. We find that the Jiang--Lin representation possesses non-vanishing gauge moments starting from the 1PM order, whereas in the canonical representation gauge moments vanish to all orders. This comparison highlights the canonical coordinates as the most gauge-pure representation of the Kerr metric in the MPM framework. The complete canonical metric for the Schwarzschild case is also computed to all PM orders. A recent independent construction by Damgaard et al. using momentum-space recursion yields 4PM equivalent results expressed as a power series in $a$, providing a cross-validation of our closed-form 4PM canonical metric. The coordinate transformation linking the canonical Kerr coordinates to previously known harmonic Kerr coordinates remains an open problem.

</details>


### [111] [How Loud Must a Neutron-Star Merger Be to Reveal Its Equation of State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05794)
*Sk Md Adil Imam*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯嵌套采样参数估计，对第三代引力波探测器网络（爱因斯坦望远镜加两台宇宙探测器）观测的模拟双中子星信号进行研究，发现正确与错误状态方程（EOS）恢复模型之间的证据差满足幂律标度 Δlog Z = A·SNR^n（n≈1.74–1.95），该标度可由奥卡姆因子（Occam factor）论证解释为与 (ΔΛ̃·SNR)² 成正比。基于三个配置标定该关系后，可在运行前以 0.3% 精度预测第四代探测器在区分状态方程所需信噪比，为评估第三代引力波探测器网络的状态方程判别能力提供了定量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合过程中的潮汐响应将致密物质状态方程（equation of state, EOS）编码在引力波信号中，因此量化第三代探测器需要多大信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR）才能区分相互竞争的状态方程模型至关重要。这一量化能力是规划下一代探测器科学目标的基础。

Method: 对爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）加两台宇宙探测器（Cosmic Explorer）组成的探测器网络观测到的模拟双中子星信号进行贝叶斯嵌套采样（Bayesian nested-sampling）参数估计，计算正确与错误状态方程恢复模型之间的证据差（evidence difference）。研究覆盖两个潮汐变形度（tidal deformability）对比度、真实与恢复状态方程互换以及两个双星质量点等多个配置，并推导了基于奥卡姆因子的标度论证。

Result: 在多种配置下发现了统一的幂律标度 Δlog Z = A·SNR^n，其中指数 n≈1.74–1.95，而状态方程对比度与双星性质仅决定前置因子（prefactor）A。该标度行为可由奥卡姆因子论证解释，得到 Δlog Z ∝ (ΔΛ̃·SNR)²。基于三个配置标定此关系后，可在运行前预测所需信噪比，误差在 0.3% 以内。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了定量框架，用于评估第三代引力波探测器网络的状态方程判别能力（EOS-discrimination reach），有望在观测运行之前通过少量标定配置即可预测达到决定性区分状态方程所需的信噪比。

Abstract: The tidal response of neutron stars during binary inspiral encodes the equation of state (EOS) of dense matter in the gravitational-wave signal. Quantifying the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) required to distinguish competing EOS models with third-generation detectors is therefore essential. We perform Bayesian nested-sampling parameter estimation on simulated binary neutron star signals observed by an Einstein Telescope plus two Cosmic Explorer detector network and compute the evidence difference between correct- and incorrect-EOS recovery models over a broad range of SNR. Across two tidal-deformability contrasts, a swap of the true and recovery EOS, and two binary mass points, we find a common scaling, $Δ\log Z = A\,\mathrm{SNR}^{n}$ with $n \simeq 1.74$--$1.95$, where the EOS contrast and binary properties determine only the prefactor $A$. This behavior follows from an Occam-factor argument, yielding $Δ\log Z \propto (Δ\tildeΛ\,\mathrm{SNR})^{2}$. Calibrating this relation on three configurations predicts, before the run, the SNR required for decisive EOS discrimination in the fourth to within $0.3\%$. These results establish a quantitative framework for assessing the EOS-discrimination reach of third-generation gravitational-wave detector networks.

</details>


### [112] [Asymmetric Quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05818)
*Michał Bobula,Tomasz Pawłowski*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在环量子宇宙学（loop quantum cosmology）框架下研究了均匀各向同性尘埃球的引力坍缩，发现尘埃球表面出现物理激波（physical shock），并证明了由此形成的时空具有测地完整性（geodesic completeness），首次在环量子宇宙学一致的框架中建立了完全动态的规则黑洞（regular black hole）形成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞模型中引力奇点（singularity）仍未得到完全解决，而环量子宇宙学提供了可能的非奇异坍缩框架。作者希望证明在该框架下不仅能够描述大爆炸（big bounce）反弹，还能实现真正动态的、无奇点的黑洞形成过程。

Method: 作者在Lemaitre-Tolman-Bondi时空中构建了环量子动力学（loop dynamics），该动力学可退化为非对称的环量子宇宙学反弹场景。对尘埃球施加强幂律收缩（power-law contraction）后跟随德西特型膨胀（de Sitter-like expansion），通过分析内部膨胀与外部收缩真空壳层的动力学失配，推导出真空区的两种类史瓦西线元（Schwarzschild-like line elements）。

Result: 发现尘埃球表面出现物理激波，其特征是诱导度量（induced metric）连续而外曲率（extrinsic curvature）不连续。推导出两种类史瓦西线元变体，并证明所得时空测地完备、因果结构（causal structure）丰富。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在环量子宇宙学相容的框架中建立了完全动态的规则黑洞形成过程，避免了经典奇点，并揭示了内部反弹与外部坍缩之间复杂的耦合机制。

Abstract: We study black hole formation resulting from the collapse of a homogeneous and isotropic dust ball within a framework built upon loop quantum cosmology. Using loop dynamics formulated for Lemaitre-Tolman-Bondi spacetimes---which reduce to the asymmetric loop quantum cosmological bounce scenario---we analyze the dust ball undergoing power-law contraction followed by a de Sitter-like expansion. We discover an emergence of a physical shock at the dust ball surface, characterized by a continuous induced metric and a discontinuous extrinsic curvature arising from a dynamical mismatch between the expanding interior and the contracting exterior vacuum shells. Furthermore, we explicitly derive two variants of Schwarzschild-like line elements for the vacuum sector, and we demonstrate that the resulting spacetime is geodesically complete featuring a rich causal structure. This establishes, for the first time, fully dynamical regular black hole formation within a framework consistent with loop quantum cosmology.

</details>


### [113] [A Novel Kerr-like Black Hole in a General Double Power Law Dark Matter Environment: Geometry, Spectroscopy, and Energy Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05861)
*Supakchai Ponglertsakul,David Senjaya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过 Newman–Janis 算法在一般双幂律暗物质背景下构造了 Kerr 型旋转黑洞解，并系统研究了暗物质对黑洞视界、极值自旋、曲率奇异性、标量拟束缚态谱、超辐射放大因子及旋转能量提取效率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多忽视暗物质环境对旋转黑洞结构的影响，缺乏一个能统一描述任意双幂律密度分布暗物质晕中旋转黑洞的统一时空框架。

Method: 运用 Newman–Janis 算法将 Schwarzschild-like 种子几何旋转化，构造嵌入一般双幂律暗物质环境的 Kerr 型黑洞解；随后利用低频解析匹配方法（low-frequency matching method）研究 Dehnen (1,4,γ) 晕中大规模标量扰动的拟束缚态谱、标量云形成条件和超辐射放大因子。

Result: 发现旋转-晕相互作用对 Dehnen 型 ρ∝r^{-γ}（γ≤2）轮廓可消除本质曲率奇异性，即使相应静态构型具奇异性；晕参数 ρ₀r₀³ 和 γ 在标量谱上留下特征印记：提高晕密度或增大尖峰（cusp）会增强拟束缚态束缚与衰减、移动标量云阈值、收窄超辐射频率窗口并降低放大因子峰值；旋转能量提取效率由热谱与超辐射不稳定的相互作用决定，低温和较缓的密度轮廓更有利于能量提取。

Conclusion: 该统一框架揭示了暗物质环境对旋转黑洞多方面性质（视界结构、极值自旋、曲率、谱学特征和能量提取）的系统性调制作用，为暗物质黑洞的观测判别提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We construct a novel Kerr-like black hole solution embedded in a general double power law dark matter environment by applying the Newman--Janis algorithm to a Schwarzschild-like seed geometry. This framework provides a unified rotating spacetime for arbitrary double power law density profiles and reveals how dark matter modifies the horizon structure, extremal spin, and curvature properties of rotating black holes. Remarkably, we find that the rotation--halo interplay can eliminate essential curvature singularities for Dehnen-type profiles with $γ\leq2$, despite the singular nature of the corresponding static configurations. We then investigate the spectroscopic signatures of the dark matter environment through massive scalar perturbations in the Dehnen $(1,4,γ)$ halo. Using an analytical low-frequency matching method, we derive the quasibound state spectrum, the onset condition for scalar cloud formation, and superradiant amplification factor, showing that the halo parameters $ρ_0 r_0^3$ and $γ$ leave characteristic imprints on the scalar spectrum. Increasing the halo density or the cusp strengthens the binding of quasibound states and enhances their decay, shifts the scalar cloud threshold, and suppresses superradiant amplification effectivity by narrowing the allowed frequency window and lowering the amplification factor peak. Finally, we analyze rotational energy extraction from thermal scalar fields and demonstrate that the efficiency is controlled by the interplay between the thermal spectrum and the superradiant instability, with lower temperatures and less cuspy density profiles yielding more efficient energy extraction.

</details>


### [114] [A Scale-Invariant Theory of the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05929)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于莱布尼茨充足理由原则（principle of sufficient reason），探讨消除绝对位置、方向、时间和尺度等不可观测结构后的关系性本体论（relational ontology），从而将引力N体问题重整为一个仅依赖无量纲比值的标度不变公式体系，并提出新的对称性和基于时空间关联的解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代物理学在取得巨大经验成功的同时，仍然保留了由牛顿引入的大量绝对且不可观测的结构，而对其必要性缺乏质疑。作者旨在探究在莱布尼茨充足理由原则指导下，这些绝对结构可以被消除到何种程度。

Method: 采用关系性本体论框架，系统性地逐一消除绝对位置、方向、时间和尺度，推导出引力N体问题的一种新表述。在该框架下，引入标度不变流形（scale-invariant variety）V作为核心量，用于度量结构、排序形状并产生引力时间箭头（gravitational arrow of time）。

Result: 该表述统一了此前被视为不同的牛顿解类别，发现了可能的新对称性，提出了一种基于无时间空间关联（timeless spatial correlations）而非时间演化的解释概念，并指向更经济（economical）的本体论。

Conclusion: 虽然该框架在牛顿引力语境下发展，但其所提出的原则可能为广义相对论和量子力学提供新的视角，暗示这一关系性方法具有更广泛的适用潜力。

Abstract: Modern physics has achieved extraordinary empirical success while retaining much of the absolute, unobservable structure introduced by Newton, largely without questioning its necessity. We investigate how far this structure can be eliminated by adopting a relational ontology guided by Leibniz's principle of sufficient reason. Removing absolute position, orientation, time and, finally, scale leads naturally to a formulation of the gravitational $N$-body problem where only dimensionless ratios are physically meaningful. Within this framework, the scale-invariant variety $V$ becomes a central quantity, providing a measure of structure, a natural ordering of shapes, and an emergent gravitational arrow of time. We argue that the resulting formulation unifies classes of Newtonian solutions previously regarded as distinct, uncovering a possible new symmetry, suggests a notion of explanation based on timeless spatial correlations rather than temporal evolution, and points towards a more economical ontology. Although developed in the context of Newtonian gravity, the principles proposed here may also offer a new perspective on general relativity and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [115] [Coherent End-to-End Search for Generic Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05974)
*Xiaobo Zou,Xingyu Zhong,Wen-Biao Han,Soumya D. Mohanty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对极端质量比旋近（EMRI，extreme-mass-ratio inspiral）信号在宽泛天文先验下的盲检测与参数恢复难题，利用次级极大值在高似然区域逐步集中于全局极大值附近的结构特性，提出基于降维剖面似然（profile likelihood）的相干分层搜索策略，首次实现了对通用EMRI系统端到端的相干参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI信号包含超过10^5个强场轨道周期，是空间引力波干涉仪的关键探测目标，但目前的Mock LISA、LISA及Taiji数据挑战（MLDCs、LDCs、TDCs）尚未能在天文宽先验下给出完整可靠的盲EMRI检测与参数恢复方案。核心障碍在于“大海捞针”式似然景观：六个相位演化参数跨度极大，导致全局极大值极窄且被众多次级极大值包围，传统方法难以直接搜索。

Method: 作者发现高似然值的次级极大值会逐步向全局极大值附近集中，从而可用于引导搜索体积的自适应收缩。基于此结构，论文采用降维剖面似然与相干分层搜索（coherent hierarchical search）策略，在完整14维参数空间中系统地搜索EMRI信号。

Result: 在平稳高斯LISA噪声下，该方法成功恢复了两条半年分析-锤造（analytical-kludge）信号，信噪比约为50；拟合因子（fitting factor）分别达到0.989和0.971，相位演化参数的分数误差为10^-3至10^-2，距离误差约为3%，天区定位误差小于0.1弧度。

Conclusion: 该工作将次级极大值转化为相干分层搜索的引导信息，首次实现了在天文宽先验下对通用EMRI系统的端到端相干参数估计，为未来空间引力波天文台的EMRI探测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) encode more than $10^5$ strong-field orbital cycles and are key targets for space-borne gravitational-wave interferometers, yet coherent recovery of generic systems over astrophysically broad priors remains unresolved. Successive Mock LISA, LISA, and Taiji Data Challenges (MLDCs, LDCs, and TDCs) have not yet produced a complete, generally reliable solution for blind EMRI detection and parameter recovery across such priors. The central obstacle is a needle-in-a-haystack likelihood: six phase-evolution parameters span a vast domain, producing an exceptionally narrow global maximum amid numerous secondary maxima. We show that higher-likelihood secondary maxima concentrate progressively around the global maximum and can therefore guide an adaptive contraction of the search volume. We exploit this structure through a reduced-dimensional profile likelihood and a coherent hierarchical strategy to search for EMRI signals across the full 14-dimensional parameter space. This enables the first end-to-end coherent parameter estimation for generic EMRIs with astrophysically broad priors. In stationary Gaussian LISA noise, the search recovers two half-year analytical-kludge signals with signal-to-noise ratios near 50, yielding fitting factors of 0.989 and 0.971, fractional errors of $10^{-3}$--$10^{-2}$ in the phase-evolution parameters and near $3\%$ in the distance, and error of less than $0.1$ radian in the sky location. The method turns secondary maxima into guides for a coherent hierarchical search.

</details>


### [116] [Radial spectra and dynamical signatures of excited boson stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06067)
*Chen-Hao Hao,Wen-Di Guo,Qin Tan,Jieci Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了球对称玻色子星（boson stars）沿固定径向节点数平衡分支上的最低径向模特征值，通过将脉动方程（pulsation equations）重整为在背景标量场零点处正则的加性变量，使本征值问题可以跨越激发态的节点直接积分。研究发现，所有分支中有约束基态径向本征值的第一个零点与ADM质量、诺特荷（Noether charge）和结合能的第一个临界点一致；并针对非线性球面演化中识别的阈值模型建立了其径向本征值与节点数和自相互作用强度之间的简单经验关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激发玻色子星（excited boson stars）的稳定性分析长期受限于脉动方程在背景标量场节点处发散的问题，缺乏一套规则的计算框架。作者旨在提供一种正则的微扰框架来系统评估激发态玻色子星的约束径向模，并厘清其与平衡临界点及非线性稳定性诊断之间的关系。

Method: 作者将脉动方程重新表述为加性变量（additive variables），使得方程在背景标量场零点处保持正则，从而本征值问题可直接跨越激发态节点积分。对象涵盖mini玻色子星与四次自相互作用（quartic self-interaction）模型，沿固定径向节点数的平衡分支计算最低径向模，并与ADM质量、诺特荷和结合能的临界点进行对照。

Result: 在所有考察的分支上，约束基态径向本征值的首个零点与ADM质量、诺特荷和结合能的首个临界点在数值精度内一致；对于非线性球面演化中识别的阈值模型，径向本征值与节点数和自相互作用强度之间存在简单的经验关联。

Conclusion: 本文为激发玻色子星提供了正则的微扰框架，明确了约束径向模、平衡临界点与非线性稳定性诊断之间的对应关系。这一结果有助于深化对激发态玻色子星稳定性判据的理解，并为其非线性动力学行为提供线性层面的理论支撑。

Abstract: We compute the lowest radial mode of spherically symmetric boson stars along equilibrium branches with a fixed number of radial nodes, considering both mini boson stars and quartically self-interacting models. By reformulating the pulsation equations in additive variables that remain regular at the zeros of the background scalar field, the eigenvalue problem can be integrated directly through the nodes of excited configurations. For all branches examined, the first zero of the constrained fundamental radial eigenvalue coincides, within numerical resolution, with the first simultaneous critical point of the Arnowitt--Deser--Misner (ADM) mass, Noether charge, and binding energy. We further evaluate the radial eigenvalue for the threshold models identified in nonlinear spherical evolutions of excited boson stars and find a simple empirical correlation with the node number and self-interaction strength. Our results provide a regular perturbative framework for excited boson stars and clarify the relation between constrained radial modes, equilibrium critical points, and nonlinear stability diagnostics.

</details>


### [117] [Quasinormal Modes of Gauss--Bonnet Black Holes via the Spectral Method: Scalar, Vector, and Tensor Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06083)
*Davide Batic,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对高维 Schwarzschild–Gauss–Bonnet 引力中标量、矢量和张量三类拟正规模（quasinormal modes）进行了统一研究，覆盖 D=5 至 D=26 的维度范围。通过高精度 Chebyshev 谱方法，发现了过阻尼纯虚模式、高泛音实部的非单调行为、维度放大效应、标量与矢量间的精确等谱性，以及六维张量扇中的不稳定性，并给出了 Darboux 因子化的解析证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如六阶 WKB 近似和特征积分技术）在高曲率修正和强耦合区域不再可靠，且高维 Gauss–Bonnet 修正下拟正规模的谱特征缺乏系统统一的数值基准。

Method: 采用高精度 Chebyshev 谱方法，在 D∈{5,6,7,8,10,11,12,26} 中系统计算标量、矢量和张量三个自旋扇区的拟正规模频谱，并对标量单极与矢量偶极的等谱性给出基于 Hamiltonian 的 Darboux 因子化解析证明。

Result: 发现在三类自旋扇区中存在过阻尼纯虚模式、高泛音实部非单调行为以及维度相关频率放大效应；在零 GB 耦合下标量单极（ℓ=0）与矢量偶极（ℓ=1）存在精确等谱性；六维张量扇首次数值确认长周期预言的不稳定性，其起始点由 Cohn–Calogero 界刻画，对应质量阈值 GM≤158.1α^(3/2)，七维及以上未发现类似不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究为 Einstein–Gauss–Bonnet 引力中的拟正规模提供了全面的基准数据，并表明高维放大的模式可能进入未来空间引力波探测器（如 DECIGO）的灵敏度范围，同时凸显了标准近似方案在强耦合和高泛音区域的局限。

Abstract: We present a unified study of scalar, vector, and tensor quasinormal modes (QNMs) of Schwarzschild black holes corrected by a Gauss--Bonnet (GB) term in higher dimensions. Using a high-precision Chebyshev spectral method, we map the QNM spectra across $D\in\{5,6,7,8,10,11,12,26\}$ well beyond the regime where sixth-order WKB and characteristic-integration techniques remain reliable. Across the three spin sectors, we find several robust signatures of higher-curvature dynamics: the appearance of overdamped purely imaginary modes, non-monotonic behaviour in the real parts of higher overtones, and a strong amplification of the dimensionless QNM frequencies in string-motivated dimensions. In the scalar and vector sectors, we uncover an exact isospectrality between the scalar monopole ($\ell=0$) and vector dipole ($\ell=1$) at vanishing GB coupling, and we provide an analytic proof based on a Darboux factorisation of the corresponding Hamiltonians. In the tensor sector, we obtain the first numerical confirmation of the long-predicted instability in six dimensions; its onset is sharply captured by the Cohn--Calogero bound and leads to the mass threshold $GM\leq 158.1\,α^{3/2}$. No analogous instability is found for $D\geq 7$, and no tensor isospectrality occurs. Converting the dimensionless frequencies to physical units suggests that the amplified modes in higher dimensions may enter the sensitivity window of future space-based detectors such as DECIGO. The merged analysis provides a comprehensive benchmark for QNMs in Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet gravity and highlights the limitations of standard approximation schemes in the strong-coupling and high-overtone regimes.

</details>


### [118] [Spinning Particle Dynamics and Observational Redshift around an Asymptotically Flat Symmergent Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06114)
*Beyhan Puliçe,Ali Övgün*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了渐进平对称引力（symmergent gravity）中可变标量曲率分支下的类时粒子动力学、碰撞能量学及光子频移。通过线性扰动分析，外部几何通过径向模式共形于史瓦西时空（Schwarzschild spacetime），γ>0分支给出汤川型（Yukawa）衰减形变，γ<0分支则产生振荡型逆半径形变，并分别推导了中性、带电及自旋粒子的轨道条件与可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索可变曲率扇区中对称引力对粒子动力学和光子观测量的修正效应，为弦模型依赖的备选引力理论提供可观测探测途径。该研究特别关注不同符号的对称参数γ如何导致截然不同的几何形变特征及其可观测后果。

Method: 在微扰框架下，作者保留了与边界条件相关的独立振幅，将外部几何视为史瓦西时空的共形变换，其中径向模式满足线性方程。粒子运动学分析涵盖中性粒子测地线、带电粒子的试探场近似（test-field approximation），以及自旋粒子基于Mathisson–Papapetrou–Dixon方程配合Tulczyjew条件的研究。文中还推导了圆轨道和边缘稳定条件、中性粒子碰撞质心能量，以及切向发射光子的红移/蓝移频移因子。

Result: 关键结果包括：红移与蓝移因子满足恒等式(1+z_{+})(1+z_{-})=1/A(r_e)，将乘积与发射点的lapse函数直接关联。γ>0分支产生平滑的短程史瓦西偏离，而γ<0分支可出现振荡径向带，允许圆轨道解存在但稳定性需独立检验。可观测量的定量解释依赖于形变振幅及振荡分支的相位。

Conclusion: 这些观测量为可变曲率扇区提供了互补的探测手段，表明对称引力中参数符号的选择决定了形变的物理表现特征。微扰域的限制要求定量解读必须同时考虑振幅和相位依赖，相关观测约束工作有待进一步开展。

Abstract: We investigate timelike particle dynamics, collision energetics, and photon frequency shifts in the perturbative variable-scalar curvature branch of asymptotically flat symmergent gravity. The low-energy vacuum action contains an $R^{2}$ correction whose coefficient is set by the boson--fermion imbalance of the underlying quantum field theory. At linear order, the exterior geometry is conformal to Schwarzschild spacetime through a radial mode satisfying a linear equation. We retain an independent boundary-condition-dependent amplitude and restrict the analysis to the perturbative domain. The two signs of the symmergent parameter $γ$ yield distinct profiles: $γ>0$ gives a Yukawa-suppressed deformation, whereas $γ<0$ produces an oscillatory inverse-radius deformation. We derive radial equations, effective potentials, circular-orbit and marginal-stability conditions for neutral, electrically charged, and spinning massive particles. Charged particles are treated in the test-field approximation, while spinning particles obey the Mathisson--Papapetrou--Dixon equations with the Tulczyjew condition. We also compute the center-of-mass energy of neutral-particle collisions and the frequency shifts of photons emitted tangentially by circular geodesic sources and detected by a static observer at infinity. The redshift and blueshift factors satisfy $(1+z_{+})(1+z_{-})=1/A(r_e)$, directly linking their product to the lapse function at emission. The $γ>0$ branch yields smooth, short-range deviations from Schwarzschild dynamics, whereas the $γ<0$ branch can generate oscillatory radial bands admitting circular-orbit solutions whose stability must be tested independently. These observables provide complementary probes of the variable-curvature sector, although their quantitative interpretation also depends on the deformation amplitude and, for the oscillatory branch, its phase.

</details>


### [119] [A Neutron Star Hidden Inside a Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06224)
*Chen Tan,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究暗物质（dark matter）混合中子星，其与各向异性暗物质晕（由Einasto密度分布描述）共存。通过求解修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程（采用BSk19和SLy4两种状态方程），发现暗物质晕显著改变中子星结构；在特定暗晕参数范围内，事件视界（event horizon）形成于恒星表面之外，中子星作为规则构型存在于黑洞内部，形成“黑洞中的中子星”这一新构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究暗物质与中子星共存时的结构效应，特别是Einasto密度剖面暗物质晕对中子星引力结构的影响，以及这种共存是否可能导致事件视界在恒星表面外形成，从而为“黑洞内部结构”这一长期未解问题提供具体可计算的实例。

Method: 求解修改的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程（modified TOV equations），将中子星与由Einasto密度剖面（Einasto density profile）描述的各向异性暗物质晕耦合在一起；采用两种不同的状态方程（equation of state）BSk19与SLy4进行对照验证。Einasto模型选自近期被证明可产生规则、无奇点（singularity-free）黑洞解的文献。

Result: 暗物质晕显著改变中子星结构。在特定暗晕参数范围内，度规分量$g_{rr}^{-1}$在恒星表面外改变符号，形成事件视界，而中子星作为规则构型存留于视界内部，即“黑洞中的中子星”。该构型在BSk19和SLy4两种状态方程下均出现，不依赖于具体状态方程的选择。

Conclusion: “黑洞中的中子星”这一构型的发现，为研究黑洞内部结构提供了新视角和具体可计算的实例，表明在某些暗物质晕参数下，黑洞事件视界内部可能存在规则、无奇点的物质构型。

Abstract: We investigate dark matter admixed neutron stars in which the neutron star coexists with an anisotropic dark matter halo described by the Einasto density profile, a model recently shown to produce regular, singularity-free black hole solutions~[Phys. Rev. D \textbf{113}, 043011 (2026)]. Solving the modified Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations with two different equations of state (BSk19 and SLy4), we find that the dark matter halo significantly alters the neutron star structure. Furthermore, for a specific range of halo parameters, $g_{rr}^{-1}$ changes sign outside the stellar surface, forming an event horizon with the neutron star persisting as a regular configuration inside it--- ``neutron stars in black holes''. This configuration appears in both equations of state and does not depend on a specific choice of the equation of state. The discovery of this configuration provides a new perspective and a concrete computable instance for the study of what lies inside a black hole.

</details>

### [120] [Time-periodic solutions of the Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann system with a general external force in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05637)
*Renjun Duan,Jinkai Ni*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究全空间R^3中Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann（VPB）系统在给定时间周期外力和非势场作用下的时间周期问题。作者在混合函数空间中证明了围绕全局Maxwellian的小解的全局存在性，并进一步证明了渐近稳定性以及同周期时间周期解的唯一性和稳定性，同时作为推论获得了定常解的存在性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPB系统在带时间周期外力的全空间中的时间周期解问题是动力学理论中具有挑战性的课题，尤其是当外力为非势场时，低频区域的非线性Vlasov力处理是主要困难。本文旨在通过结构消去和混合函数空间框架克服这一困难，建立全局适定性和稳定性理论。

Method: 作者构造混合函数空间，将低频Besov框架（用于受迫Boltzmann方程）与自洽电场的控制相结合。关键创新在于：对低频区域中的非线性Vlasov力，不将其视为一般源项，而是利用Poisson方程和宏观平衡律恢复VPB半群估计所需的结构消去（structural cancellation），并与高频能量估计和加权微观传播相结合，建立封闭的全局适定性理论。

Result: 证明了围绕全局Maxwellian的小解的全局存在性；证明了由同一外力驱动的小解的渐近稳定性。当外力为时间周期时，利用Serrin方法获得具有相同周期的唯一时间周期解及其稳定性；作为直接推论，当外力不依赖时间时，还给出了定常解的存在性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文为带非势时间周期外力的VPB系统建立了时间周期解的全局适定性（global well-posedness）与稳定性理论，并通过处理低频非线性Vlasov力的结构消去方法填补了该问题在数学分析上的空白。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the time-periodic problem for the Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann (VPB) system with a given time-periodic external force in the whole space $\mathbb{R}^3$. The force is allowed to be non-potential. Around the global Maxwellian, we prove the global existence of small solutions in a hybrid function space that combines the low-frequency Besov framework for the forced Boltzmann equation with a corresponding control of the self-consistent electric field. The main novelty lies in the treatment of the nonlinear Vlasov force $-\nabla_xφ\cdot \nabla_vf + \frac{1}{2}(v \cdot \nabla_xφ)f$ at low frequencies. Rather than treating it as a generic source term, we exploit the Poisson equation and macroscopic balance laws to recover the structural cancellation required for the VPB semi-group estimates, which combined with high-frequency energy estimates and weighted microscopic propagation, yields a closed global well-posedness theory. We further prove the asymptotic stability of small solutions driven by the same force. When the external force is time-periodic, Serrin's method yields a unique time-periodic solution with the same period, together with its stability. As a direct consequence, our result also gives the existence and stability of stationary solutions when the external force is time-independent.

</details>


### [121] [On generalised d'Alembert-type integral representations for damped wave equations on the quarter-plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06355)
*Andreas Chatziafratis,Claudio Giorgi,Alain Miranville,Federico Zullo*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展Fokas统一变换方法（unified transform method），首次将其应用于半无穷区间上的双曲-抛物（hyperbolic-parabolic）问题，为受迫Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte方程（即阻尼波方程/双曲热传导方程/有损传输线上的电报方程）在时空四分之一平面上的一般初边值问题构造了严格的新闭式解，并建立了关于正则性、边界与渐近性质以及适定性（well-posedness）的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受迫Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte方程在连续介质力学、数学物理、生物学等广泛领域具有重要建模意义，但其在半无穷区域上的解析解构造长期缺乏严格的系统性工具。Fokas统一变换方法此前仅适用于椭圆型和具有多项式色散关系的演化方程，需要扩展以处理双曲-抛物型问题在半无穷区间上的边值问题。

Method: 将Fokas现代复分析统一变换方法首次推广应用于半无穷区间上的双曲-抛物问题，在经典函数空间中处理一般初边值条件。对解公式中的振荡积分项进行精细的数学分析，并证明关于正则性、边界行为、渐近性质及适定性的定理，期间揭示了初值、边值与强迫项在原点处需要满足的相容性条件（compatibility conditions）。

Result: 严格构造并验证了受迫Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte方程在时空四分之一平面上的一般闭式解，建立了解的正则性、边界与渐近性质定理及边值问题的适定性定理。所得显式积分表示可用于数值基准测试以及与非线性对应问题的适定性研究。

Conclusion: 该工作为双曲-抛物型方程在半无穷区域上的解析研究提供了新的统一方法框架，所得显式解公式对数值benchmark、跨学科建模以及非线性问题的适定性研究均具有直接实用价值。

Abstract: We rigorously construct and verify a posteriori new closed-form solutions for the forced Maxwell-Cattaneo-Vernotte equation (also broadly known as the damped wave equation, hyperbolic heat, and telegrapher's equation on lossy transmission lines) posed on the spatiotemporal quarter-plane with general initial and boundary data in classical function spaces. For this purpose, the modern complex-analytic unified transform method of Fokas (originally developed for elliptic PDE and evolution equations with polynomial dispersion relations) is here, for the first time, extended for analysis of hyperbolic-parabolic problems on the semi-infinite interval. Importantly, we then establish theorems which pertain to regularity, boundary and asymptotic properties of the new analytical formulae as well as to well-posedness of the addressed boundary-value problems. Notably, the nature and generality of problems considered, combined with the semi-unboundedness of the domain, induce substantial analytic challenges which demand delicate treatment, both in appropriately interpreting oscillatory integral terms of the solution formulae and in proving the proposed results. In this process, crucially, certain compatibility conditions, between initial, boundary and forcing data at the origin, are revealed, which guarantee the existence of a smooth solution across the whole domain of interest. Our explicit integral representations are of direct utility for numercal benchmarking purposes, for exploring connections with modelling in continuum mechanics, mathematical physics, biology and the natural sciences, and for the investigation of well-posedness for nonlinear counterparts too.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [The QCD Axion and Neutrino Masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05150)
*Hridoy Debnath,Pavel Fileviez Perez,Grace Miller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索强CP问题（strong CP problem）与中微子质量来源由同一物理起源统一描述的可能性，即通过将Peccel-Quinn对称性破缺能标与中微子质量生成能标相关联。作者在DFSZ与KSVZ两种实现方案中，系统分析Type-I/II/III跷跷板、Zee辐射模型及有色跷跷板等中微子质量生成机制，并给出各方案中轴子（axion）与光子和中微子的耦合及其唯象学后果。所有框架的共同预言是轴子-中微子耦合的存在，该耦合显著影响轴子衰变，并在轴子构成暗物质的情况下产生大量单色中微子通量，为未来中微子实验提供互补的检验途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型之外的两大明确动机——强CP问题与中微子质量起源——长期以来被分别处理；文中尝试将二者统一归因于同一个能标，从而减少理论中的自由参数并建立现象学上的内在联系。

Method: 以Peccel-Quinn机制为核心框架，将其对称性破缺能标设定为中微子质量生成的能标；在DFSZ与KSVZ两种轴子实现方案下，逐一搭配六种中微子质量生成机制（Type-I/II/III跷跷板、Zee辐射模型、有色跷跷板），系统计算每种组合的轴子-光子及轴子-中微子耦合，并分析其热力学与衰变行为。

Result: 所有被考察的框架均稳定地预言轴子与中微子之间存在非零耦合；该耦合会显著改变轴子的衰变分支比与衰变动力学；若QCD轴子构成当前观测到的暗物质，其衰变将产生大幅度的单色中微子流，可被未来中微子实验探测到。

Conclusion: 统一的轴子-中微子起源框架在多种中微子质量生成机制下均自洽成立，轴子-中微子耦合是该类模型健壮的现象学信号；其产生的单色中微子通量提供了利用中微子实验独立检验轴子暗物质方案的新途径。

Abstract: The strong CP problem and the origin of neutrino masses are among the clearest motivations for physics beyond the Standard Model. The Peccei-Quinn mechanism offers an elegant dynamical solution to the strong CP problem, predicting the QCD axion, while neutrino masses point to new degrees of freedom or interactions beyond the Standard Model. We explore the possibility that these two phenomena arise from a common origin by identifying the Peccei-Quinn symmetry-breaking scale with the scale responsible for neutrino mass generation. We perform a systematic and unified analysis of this connection in both DFSZ and KSVZ realizations of the Peccei-Quinn mechanism, considering the Type-I, Type-II, and Type-III seesaw mechanisms, the Zee radiative model, and the colored seesaw. For each scenario, we determine the axion couplings to photons and neutrinos and examine their phenomenological consequences. A robust prediction of all the frameworks considered is the existence of axion-neutrino couplings. We show that these interactions can significantly affect the axion decays. If the QCD axion makes up the observed dark matter, these couplings also lead to a large monochromatic neutrino flux from axion decays, opening a complementary path to test these theories in future neutrino experiments.

</details>


### [123] [First-principles upper bounds on dark matter-electron scattering rates from condensed matter sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05282)
*Bradford A. Barker,Jay Epstein,Luke James,Yonatan Kahn,Elizabeth A. Peterson,Anirudh Prabhu,Tanner Trickle,Samuel L. Watkins*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理求和规则（first-principles sum rules）推导出暗物质-电子散射率的上界，该上界仅依赖于少数宏观材料性质（等离子体频率、目标质量密度和有限动量转移下的静态介电函数），从而为任何探测电子密度耦合的暗物质直接探测实验设立了基本灵敏度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种凝聚态系统被用作（或提议用作）搜索暗物质-电子散射的探测器，但散射率通常依赖于对这些系统电子性质的详细认知。作者希望找到一种无需精确电子结构细节、仅依赖少数宏观参数即可约束散射率的普适方法。

Method: 利用第一性原理求和规则（其积分仅依赖于少量宏观目标性质）推导暗物质-电子散射率的上界。该上界仅依赖三个材料参数：等离子体频率ω_p、目标质量密度ρ_T以及有限动量转移下的静态（纵向）介电函数ε(q,0)；并通过解析与数值例子验证了ε(q,0)在较大q范围内的通用标度律。

Result: 推导出的上界几乎与具体材料无关（material-agnostic），因为体材料性质ω_p和ρ_T在多种材料中变化范围有限，且ε(q,0)的大q依赖可由仅取决于ω_p的简单标度律近似描述，该标度律已通过解析和数值例子验证。

Conclusion: 这些上界为任何探测电子密度耦合的暗物质-电子直接探测实验灵敏度设立了基本限制，具有普适性和基础性意义。

Abstract: A wide variety of condensed matter systems are used or proposed as detectors to search for dark matter-electron scattering. In general, the scattering rate depends on detailed knowledge of the electronic properties of these systems. However, when dark matter couples to electron density, the dark matter-electron scattering rate can be related to the electron energy loss function, whose integrals are bounded by first-principles sum rules that rely on only a few macroscopic target properties. In this paper, we use these first-principles sum rules to derive upper bounds on the dark matter-electron scattering rate depending on only a few material properties: the plasma frequency $ω_\text{p}$, the target mass density $ρ_T$, and the static (longitudinal) dielectric function at finite momentum transfer, $\varepsilon(q, 0)$. The bulk material properties $ω_\text{p}$ and $ρ_T$ vary only over a limited range across a wide variety of materials, and to a good approximation, the generic large-$q$ dependence of $\varepsilon(q, 0)$ can be understood from a simple scaling law depending only on $ω_\text{p}$ which we verify with analytic and numerical examples. Thus, our upper bounds are largely material-agnostic, and place a fundamental limit on the sensitivity of any dark matter-electron direct detection experiment probing the coupling to electron density.

</details>


### [124] [Photon-nucleon entanglement in Compton scattering at low and high energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05330)
*Yoshitaka Hatta,Víctor Martínez-Fernández*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了康普顿散射（Compton scattering）终态光子-核子系统中的自旋-自旋纠缠，涵盖低能（介子阈值以下）和高能（微扰QCD到次领头阶）两个区域。作者首先建立了一个否定性定理（no-go theorem），证明对于自旋1/2靶粒子，在散射振幅为实数时，非极化康普顿散射无法产生纠缠；随后研究了电子、质子和中子靶上的极化康普顿散射，在低能区发现多种最大纠缠贝尔态（Bell states），并揭示了质子和中子靶的不同纠缠模式，其中中子的电极化率和磁极化率对纠缠模式有显著影响，表明纠缠可以作为研究核子电磁性质的崭新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）这一概念引入粒子物理散射过程，探索康普顿散射终态的自旋纠缠能否作为研究核子电磁结构的新工具；同时填补此前对散射过程自旋纠缠认识的空白，特别是在低能和高能两个能量区域内的系统研究。

Method: 在低能区（低于π介子阈值），基于介子脉络（pion-ful）的有效场论框架分析散射振幅；在高能区，使用微扰量子色动力学（perturbative QCD）计算到次领头阶（next-to-leading order）。研究者先通过否定性定理分析实数振幅条件下非极化散射的纠缠不可生成性，再针对极化散射计算终态光子-核子系统的自旋纠缠度量和贝尔态结构。

Result: 建立了否定性定理：自旋1/2靶在实振幅条件下，非极化康普顿散射不能产生自旋纠缠。在极化散射中，低能区发现了跨运动学平面不同区域的一系列最大纠缠贝尔态及其幺正等价形式。质子与中子靶表现出不同的纠缠模式，在中子情形下，电极化率与磁极化率对纠缠模式甚至纠缠的存在性产生显著影响。

Conclusion: 康普顿散射终态的自旋纠缠为探测核子电磁性质提供了一种全新手段，尤其对中子而言，极化率与纠缠之间的敏感关联使纠缠有望成为研究核子内部电磁响应结构的高灵敏度探针。

Abstract: We study spin-spin entanglement in the final state photon-nucleon system in Compton scattering, both at low energy below the pion threshold and at high energy in perturbative QCD to next-to-leading order. We first establish a no-go theorem showing that, for any spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ target, entanglement cannot be generated in unpolarized Compton scattering if the scattering amplitudes are real. We then consider polarized Compton scattering off the electron, the proton and the neutron. At low energy, we uncover a rich variety of maximally entangled Bell states and their unitary equivalents realized across different regions of the kinematic plane. Interestingly, the proton and neutron targets exhibit distinct patterns of entanglement. In the neutron case, the electric and magnetic polarizabilities dramatically influence the pattern and even the existence of entanglement. This suggests that entanglement can serve as a novel tool for investigating the detailed electromagnetic properties of the nucleons.

</details>


### [125] [Coherent and Stochastic Axion Dark Matter from Thermal Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05538)
*Shahid Hussain Gurmani,Arzu Cilli,Phongpichit Channuie,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Farruh Atamurotov,Ertan Güdekli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子（axion）在膨胀等离子体中向热力学最小值趋近时的动力学行为，推导了场均值衰减与随机动量模产生的耦合关系。通过引入与动量相关的光学深度（optical depth），将标准错位机制（misalignment）、部分弛豫和随机产生统一在一个动力学框架中，并证明热弛豫决定了轴子暗物质的组成、动量分布及输运结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为被驱动趋向热力学最小值的轴子会保持相干凝聚态（coherent condensate），但作者质疑这一假设，旨在探究轴子在膨胀等离子体中的真实演化行为及其对暗物质（dark matter）性质的影响。

Method: 在膨胀等离子体背景中，推导场均值的耦合衰减以及随机动量模（stochastic momentum modes）的产生方程；引入单一动量依赖的光学深度，将每个模划分为相干功率与玻色占据（Bose occupation）两部分，考虑有限空间与时间响应效应，建立统一的动力学关系。

Result: 有限时空响应会抑制高动量模的产生，使得随机遗迹（stochastic relic）的平均动量更低、自由流长度（free streaming length）短于固定质量与浴温度下的平衡分布。弱耦合实现可产生暗物质丰度，减少继承的相干等曲率扰动（isocurvature），并将产生关联限制在无碰撞传播去除的尺度上。

Conclusion: 热弛豫（thermal relaxation）决定轴子暗物质的组成、动量分布与输运结构；被驱动向热力学最小值的轴子不一定保持相干凝聚态，其随机组分对暗物质性质具有实质性影响。

Abstract: An axion driven toward a thermal minimum need not remain a coherent condensate. We derive the coupled attenuation of the field mean and production of stochastic momentum modes in an expanding plasma. A single momentum dependent optical depth partitions every mode between coherent power and Bose occupation, placing standard misalignment, partial relaxation, and stochastic production in one dynamical relation. Finite spatial and temporal response suppresses high momentum production, so the stochastic relic has lower mean momentum and a shorter free streaming length than an equilibrium population at fixed mass and bath temperature. A weakly coupled realization produces the dark matter abundance, reduces inherited coherent isocurvature, and confines production correlations to scales removed by collisionless propagation. Thermal relaxation therefore determines the composition, momentum distribution, and transported structure of axion dark matter.

</details>


### [126] [GOOFy-compatible 3HDMs and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05304)
*I. de Medeiros Varzielas,A. Kunčinas*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入并系统分析了超越常规Higgs族与一般CP变换的"GOOFy"非标准变换，发展了一种符号轨道（sign-orbit）技术来确定与这些变换相容的双线性（bilinear）结构。应用于三Higgs双峰模型并推广至更大多Higgs扇区后，发现非零的GOOFy相容二次扇区仅当两个独立条件同时满足时存在：底层群具有非平凡符号特征（sign character），且该特征在标量表示与其共轭的张量积中被实现。关键结论是，重整化群（renormalization group）稳定性在$r_0 \to -r_0$流形上并非多Higgs势的普遍特征，而是$SU(2)$特殊代数性质的直接结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对潜在对称性的研究集中于常规Higgs族变换和一般CP变换，而更广泛的非标准"GOOFy"变换虽不构成完整拉格朗日量的对称性，却能稳定标量势，其二次扇区的影响尚未被系统分类，因此需要建立系统的分类方法。

Method: 论文发展了一种"符号轨道"（sign-orbit）技术，用于确定与GOOFy变换相容的双线性结构。该方法在三Higgs双峰模型（three-Higgs-doublet models）上实施，并设计为可推广到更大的多Higgs扇区。

Result: 方法表明，非零GOOFy相容二次扇区仅当两个独立条件同时满足才存在：底层群承认非平凡符号特征，且该特征在标量的表示与其共轭的张量积中被实现。作为关键推论，$r_0 \to -r_0$流形的重整化群稳定性并非多Higgs势的普遍性质，而是$SU(2)$特殊代数性质的直接后果。

Conclusion: 工作提供了GOOFY二次结构及其不变双线性子空间的完整分类，揭示了阻碍其重整化群稳定性的代数障碍，从而深化了对多Higgs势中非标准变换结构及其动力学后果的理解。

Abstract: Beyond conventional Higgs-family and general CP transformations, one may also consider a broader class of non-standard "GOOFy" transformations, in which the scalar fields and their conjugates are assigned related but inequivalent transformations. Although these transformations are not symmetries of a full Lagrangian in the conventional sense, some nevertheless stabilise the scalar potential. Their impact on the quadratic sector has not yet been systematically classified. We develop a sign-orbit technique to determine the bilinear structures compatible with these transformations. Applied to three-Higgs-doublet models, and formulated so as to extend to larger multi-Higgs sectors, the method shows that a non-vanishing GOOFy-compatible quadratic sector exists only when two independent conditions are met: the underlying group admits a non-trivial sign character, and that character is realised within the tensor product of the scalar representation and its conjugate. As a key consequence, we demonstrate that the renormalisation-group stability of the $r_0 \to -r_0$ manifold in isolation is not a generic feature of multi-Higgs potentials, but rather a direct consequence of special algebraic properties of $SU(2)$. These results provide a classification of the admissible GOOFy quadratic structures and their invariant bilinear subspaces, highlighting the algebraic obstructions to their renormalisation-group stability.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Borel Windows: A Systematic Optimization Framework for QCD Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05322)
*Raphael M. Albuquerque*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的优化框架，用于确定QCD Laplace求和规则（Laplace sum rules）中的求和窗口。该框架结合OPE熵判据（OPE entropy criterion）、局部质量平稳性（local mass stationarity）以及对Laplace参数τ、连续谱阈值tc和插值流混合角θ的相关分析，取代了传统依赖固定收敛百分比和目视平台选择的方法。应用于最轻的全粲四夸克态（fully charmed tetraquark state）J^PC=2^++，预测质量M=(6.32±0.11) GeV，与ATLAS和CMS实验观测结果相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的QCD求和规则中求和窗口的确定主要依赖固定的收敛百分比和目视平台选择，缺乏系统性、可重复性。作者旨在提出一种客观、可复现的优化框架，以减少辅助参数选择的主观性并量化剩余不确定度。

Method: 引入归一化OPE熵来量化微扰与非微扰QCD贡献之间的重新分配，划定初始工作区域；随后通过最小化质量估计量的残差变化并要求对连续谱阈值和流组成具有弱敏感性来进一步精修该区域。使用混合二夸克-反二夸克（diquark-antidiquark）和介子-介子（meson-meson）插值流研究J^PC=2^++全粲四夸克态。

Result: 优化选择θ=17.2°±0.4°，√tc=7.08±0.08 GeV，τ=0.410±0.010 GeV^-2，得到质量预言M=(6.32±0.11) GeV。该态位于二-J/ψ谱的阈值附近，与ATLAS报告的最低共振分量及CMS发布中参数化为BW0的增强结构在不确定度范围内相容。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可复现的方式来确定辅助参数敏感度降低的有限域，并将残余依赖性纳入最终不确定度，为QCD求和规则的系统化应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a systematic optimization framework for determining the sum rule window in QCD Laplace sum rules. Instead of relying primarily on fixed convergence percentages and visual plateau selection, the procedure combines an OPE entropy criterion, local mass stationarity, and a correlated analysis of the Laplace parameter $τ$, the continuum threshold $t_c$, and the interpolating-current mixing angle $θ$. The normalized OPE entropy is introduced to quantify the redistribution of the QCD contributions between the perturbative and nonperturbative sectors and to delimit an initial Working Region. This domain is subsequently refined by minimizing the residual variation of the mass estimator and requiring weak sensitivity to the continuum threshold and to the current composition. As an application, we study the lowest fully charmed tetraquark state with quantum numbers $J^{PC}=2^{++}$ using a mixed diquark--antidiquark and meson--meson interpolating current. The optimization selects $θ=17.2^\circ\pm0.4^\circ$, $\sqrt{t_c}=7.08\pm0.08~\mathrm{GeV}$, and $τ=0.410\pm0.010~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$, leading to the mass prediction $M_{c\bar c c\bar c}^{2^{++}}=(6.32\pm0.11)~\mathrm{GeV}$. The predicted state lies in the near-threshold region of the di-$J/ψ$ spectrum and is compatible, within uncertainties, with the lowest resonant component reported by ATLAS and with the enhancement parametrized as $BW_0$ in the recent CMS publication. The proposed framework provides a reproducible way of identifying finite domains of reduced auxiliary-parameter sensitivity and of incorporating the remaining dependence into the final uncertainty.

</details>

### [128] [Integrable curl-force Hamiltonians: bi-Hamiltonian structure, separability, and periodic orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05952)
*Alexander Felski,Andreas Fring*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有不定动能（indefinite kinetic energy）的哈密顿旋度力（Hamiltonian curl-force）系统，重新审视了Berry多项式模型并证明其未通过Painlevé检验，同时构造了一个四参数可积旋度力族，并给出了利uoville/Painlevé可积性的显式结构（相容Poisson张量、分离变量与Lax表示）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berry多项式旋度力哈密顿模型中数值观察到的闭合轨道曾引发可积性猜想，作者希望系统性地检验该模型的可积性，并探索更广泛的旋度力系统在何种参数条件下可积。

Method: 采用Painlevé分析（Painlevé analysis）检验Berry模型的可积性；随后引入拥有四个参数的一般化旋度力族，通过构造第二哈密顿量与相容Poisson张量、分离复特征变量以及Lax表示（Lax representation）来确立可积性；并利用零旋度约化（zero-curl invariant reduction）分析周期解。

Result: Berry模型展现出非主共振谱（non-principal resonance spectrum），Laurent级数无法容纳一般解所需的任意常数数量，因此未通过标准Painlevé检验。作者在四参数族上找到可积参数位点，并构造了任意次数的多项式可积旋度力哈密顿量；同时发现该构造允许一种高次时间导数势化（potentialisation），其自由极限是退化的Pais–Uhlenbeck振子。此外，在不可积区域外还发现了一个孤立的周期轨道。

Conclusion: 闭合轨道本身不足以作为可积性的判据——Berry模型虽表现出闭合轨迹，却既非Liouville可积也非Painlevé可积。本研究系统性地揭示了旋度力系统可积结构的来源，并推广到任意次数的多项式可积哈密顿量。

Abstract: We investigate Hamiltonian curl-force systems with indefinite kinetic energy. We first reconsider Berry's polynomial Hamiltonian curl-force model, whose numerically observed closed trajectories motivated an integrability conjecture. A Painlevé analysis yields a non-principal resonance spectrum, so that the corresponding Laurent series cannot accommodate the required number of arbitrary constants of the general solution. The model therefore fails the standard Painlevé test. We then introduce a four-parameter curl-force family and identify the parameter locus on which this system is integrable. We construct a second Hamiltonian, compatible Poisson tensors, separated complex characteristic variables, and a Lax representation. More generally, the separated form yields polynomial integrable curl-force Hamiltonians of arbitrary degree. We also show that the same construction admits a higher time-derivative potentialisation whose free limit is the degenerate Pais-Uhlenbeck oscillator. Finally, we analyse zero-curl invariant reductions and elliptic periodic solutions, and exhibit an isolated periodic orbit outside the integrable regime. This illustrates that closed trajectories alone do not imply Liouville or Painlevé integrability.

</details>

Zorn,M. Zurek,J. de Melo,D. de Souza Lemos,A. Accardi,I. Borsa,M. Cerutti,C. Cocuzza,J. Jiménez-López,K. Kumericki,R. Martinez,L. Rossi,P. Sznajder,A. Vladimirov,K. Wichmann,S. Yadav,P. Zurita*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本报告来自ePIC合作组，概述了电子离子对撞机（EIC）在达到设计亮度和能量范围之前早期运行阶段可实现的物理研究计划，涵盖从全包含到半包含、独占、衍射、标记、喷注及重味等多类测量，表明即使未达全设计性能，也能为量子色动力学（QCD）带来世界领先的新见解，并为后续完整物理计划奠定方法论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在EIC尚未达到完整设计亮度和能量范围之前，评估其早期运行阶段能够实现何种科学目标，并为EIC核心科学支柱提供早期验证与前期准备。

Method: 基于现实的早期运行束流配置，采用详细的Geant4 ePIC探测器模拟、击中数字化和数据重建进行分析；研究内容来自ePIC各物理工作组，涵盖电子-质子与电子-离子碰撞中的全包含、半包含、独占、衍射、标记、喷注和重味测量。

Result: 早期测量可约束核子与原子核中的部分子分布函数（PDF），获取横向动量依赖与自旋相关观测量，探测原子核中的胶子动力学，并启动夸克与胶子成像计划；每项测量均直接关联2018年美国国家科学院报告确定的三大EIC科学支柱。

Conclusion: EIC早期运行配合ePIC探测器将提供新颖的世界领先的QCD见解，同时建立的测量与分析方法论可为后续完整EIC物理计划铺平道路。

Abstract: This Early Science Report from the ePIC Collaboration outlines the compelling physics program achievable during the first years of operation of the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC), prior to the establishment of the full design luminosity and energy range. The analyses are based on realistic early-running beam configurations and detailed Geant4 ePIC detector simulations, hit digitization and data reconstruction. The projected studies from the physics working groups of ePIC span inclusive, semi-inclusive, exclusive, diffractive and tagging, as well as jet and heavy flavor measurements in both electron-proton and electron-ion collisions. Even before the collider reaches its full design performance, these measurements will constrain parton distribution functions in nucleons and nuclei, access transverse-momentum-dependent and spin-dependent observables, probe gluon dynamics in nuclei, and initiate a program of imaging of quarks and gluons. Each measurement is directly connected to the core science pillars of the EIC, identified in the 2018 report by the National Academy of Sciences: understanding the origin of the nucleon mass, unraveling the spin structure of the nucleon, and exploring the emergent properties of dense gluonic matter. The results presented here provide examples that demonstrate that the early years of EIC running with ePIC will deliver novel world-leading insights into Quantum Chromodynamics. In addition, the early science program will establish measurement and analysis methodologies that will pave the way to the subsequent full EIC physics program.

</details>


### [130] [Three-baryon femtoscopy as an effective 3$\rightarrow$3 scattering experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05708)
*ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用ALICE探测器在LHC上测量的pp碰撞（√s=13.6 TeV）中三质子关联函数，提出了探测三体系统中强子间相互作用的创新途径。通过新的分析策略分离ppp贡献并与三体连续谱理论计算直接比较，首次直接获取同位旋3/2三体系统的信息，并首次实验观测到三质子连续系统中等效长程吸引分量。研究揭示三强子契伦科夫成像（femtoscopy）可等效为3→3散射实验，并为奇异数领域的未来精密研究开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射实验长期以来是约束强子-强子相互作用的标准方法，但对三体动力学——特别是非束缚系统及涉及短寿命强子的系统——的实验约束仍然有限。作者旨在通过测量三质子关联函数为三体强子相互作用提供直接实验入口。

Method: 在LHC的ALICE实验中，于√s=13.6 TeV的pp碰撞中测量三质子（p-p-p）关联函数；采用新分析策略校正背景道与实验效应，从而将ppp贡献从关联函数中分离，并与最先进的三体连续谱理论计算进行直接比较。

Result: 提取的关联函数首次提供了对同位旋（isospin）3/2三体系统的直接探测。观测到的物理量对核子-核子相互作用的分波（partial-wave）结构敏感，表明核相互作用即使在核子的高角动量、高宇称（parity）态下依然起作用，并首次在实验上于三质子连续系统中揭示了等效长程吸引分量。

Conclusion: 三强子契伦科夫成像（three-hadron femtoscopy）可被视为一种有效的3→3散射实验，其初末态均为三个非束缚强子。鉴于现代高能对撞机上超子的大量产生，该方法可推广至奇异数（strangeness）领域，为未来三体动力学的精密研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Scattering experiments have long been the gold standard for constraining hadron$-$hadron interactions, providing direct information on the angular momentum and spin dependence over a wide range of kinematic configurations. However, experimental constraints on three-body dynamics remain limited, specifically for unbound systems and systems involving short-lived hadrons. In this work, the three-proton correlation function is measured in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV with ALICE at the LHC and presented as a novel approach to access hadronic interactions in three-body systems. A new analysis strategy is employed to isolate the p$-$p$-$p contribution to the correlation function by correcting for background channels and experimental effects, and enabling a direct comparison with state-of-the-art three-body continuum calculations. The extracted correlation function provides the first direct access to the isospin $3/2$ three-body system. The measured observable is found to be sensitive to the partial-wave structure of the nucleon$-$nucleon interaction and indicates that the nuclear interaction acts even at high angular momentum and parity states of the three-body system, revealing an effective long-range attractive component, observed experimentally for the first time in a three-proton continuum system. Hence, three-hadron femtoscopy emerges as an effective 3$\rightarrow$3 scattering experiment with three unbound hadrons in initial and final states. The copious production of hyperons at the modern high-energy colliders ensures the possibility of extending such measurements beyond nucleons, opening a new avenue for future precision studies of three-body dynamics in the strangeness sector.

</details>
