# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Observing the emergence of a velocity hierarchy in matter waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05241)
*Xudong Yu,Wenhan Chen,Igor Zhuravlev,Yi Zeng,Sudipta Dhar,Milena Horvath,Thierry Giamarchi,Laurent Sanchez-Palencia,Manuele Landini,Hanns-Christoph Nägerl,Yanliang Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该实验在强相互作用的晶格气体中首次观察到经典色散介质中相位速度与群速度分离类似的速度层级现象。利用淬灭光谱学（quench spectroscopy）与时间分辨关联测量，作者独立测定了超流-莫特绝缘体转变附近的声速、群速度和相位速度，发现这些速度在转变点附近几乎简并，但在莫特能隙开启后逐渐分离，并满足类相对论的不变关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典波在色散介质中自然具有不同的相位速度和群速度，但在强多体相互作用下物质波（matter waves）是否会出现类似的速度分离，此前缺乏实验探索。该工作旨在回答这一基础问题。

Method: 作者在一个强相互作用的晶格气体（lattice gas）中，结合淬灭光谱学与时间分辨的关联测量，在超流-莫特绝缘体（superfluid-to-Mott-insulator）转变及其邻域独立测定了声速、群速度和相位速度三种传播速度。

Result: 在超流-莫特转变附近三种速度几乎简并，但随着莫特能隙（Mott gap）打开、准粒子色散呈现大量相对论式（massive relativistic-like）形式，三种速度逐渐分离。相位相干前沿的传播速度超过Lieb-Robinson速度尺度，但仍与局域性一致；绝缘区测得的速度满足类相对论的不变关系。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了传播速度层级作为强关联量子动力学涌现特征的地位，为多体物理中的速度层次现象提供了实验基础。

Abstract: Classical waves in dispersive media naturally exhibit distinct phase and group velocities. Whether an analogous separation of velocities can emerge in matter waves under strong many-body interactions has remained experimentally unexplored. Here, we demonstrate the emergence of a velocity hierarchy in a strongly interacting lattice gas. Using quench spectroscopy together with time-resolved correlation measurements, we independently determine the sound, group, and phase velocities across the superfluid-to-Mott-insulator transition. These velocities are nearly degenerate close to the transition, but progressively separate as the Mott gap opens and the quasiparticle dispersion acquires a massive relativistic-like form. Strikingly, phase-coherence fronts propagate faster than the Lieb-Robinson velocity scale while remaining fully consistent with locality. The measured velocities satisfy a relativistic-like invariance relation in the insulating regime. Our results establish propagation-velocity hierarchies as emergent signatures of strongly correlated quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [132] [Exact statistical transmutation of quantum mixtures on a ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05290)
*Wayne J. Chetcuti,Nathan Goldman,Patrizia Vignolo,Anna Minguzzi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过精确求解一维强排斥量子混合物，证明了在单杂质极限下，人工规范场（artificial gauge field）环绕的环上Fermi-Fermi和Bose-Bose混合物的波函数在杂质与多数粒子交换时展现精确的任意子统计（anyonic statistics）。分数交换相位由所施加磁通（flux）选择的角动量扇区决定，杂质动量分布与任意子一致，并提出了可逆动态任意化（dynamical anyonization）的淬火协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维强排斥量子混合物中，轨道与自旋自由度的相互作用可产生非平凡的交换统计（exchange statistics），理解并操控这种统计对于实现普适量子计算和拓扑物态具有重要意义。现有方法难以直接实现和验证任意子统计，亟需可行的实验路径。

Method: 利用一维强排斥量子混合物的精确解（exact solution），在单杂质极限下对人工规范场包围的环上Fermi-Fermi和Bose-Bose混合物进行分析；通过施加磁通选择角动量扇区来确定分数交换相位；并设计了一个淬火（quench）协议以实现可逆的动态任意化。

Result: 在单杂质极限下，波函数在杂质与多数粒子交换时表现出精确的任意子统计，分数交换相位由磁通所选择的角动量扇区唯一确定。杂质的动量分布与任意子动量分布精确一致，与混合物的玻色或费米性质无关，且大动量尾（large-momentum tail）直接编码了任意子统计角的信息。

Conclusion: 该结果为实现和操控任意子化状态提供了基于超冷原子（ultracold atoms）的可行路径，为实验探测任意子统计在凝聚态物理中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: One-dimensional strongly repulsive quantum mixtures exhibit non-trivial exchange statistics arising from the interplay between orbital and spin degrees of freedom. Using an exact solution, we demonstrate that in the single-impurity limit the wavefunction of Fermi-Fermi and Bose-Bose mixtures on a ring threaded by an artificial gauge field display exact anyonic statistics under exchange of the impurity with the majority particles. The resulting fractional exchange phase is fixed by the angular momentum sector selected through the applied flux. We find that the impurity momentum distribution coincide exactly with the anyonic one, independently of the bosonic or fermionic nature of the mixture, with the large-momentum tails encoding a direct signature of the anyonic statistical angle. Finally, we devise a quench protocol for reversible dynamical anyonization. Our results provide a path for realizing and manipulating anyonized states with ultracold atoms.

</details>


### [133] [Criteria for Feasible Monte Carlo Stochastic Simulations of Bosonic Markovian Open Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06056)
*Toma Yoneya,Kazuya Fujimoto,Yuki Kawaguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文从路径积分表述出发，系统推导了基于准概率分布函数（quasiprobability distribution function）的随机蒙特卡洛（stochastic Monte Carlo）模拟在玻色开放量子多体动力学中可行的充分条件，即Fokker--Planck方程的扩散矩阵为正半定的条件，并分析了这些模拟在何种情形下是必要的，以及高阶量子涨落项何时可精确消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于Glauber--Sudarshan P、Wigner和Husimi Q等准概率分布函数的随机蒙特卡洛模拟为研究GKSL方程描述的玻色开放量子多体动力学提供了超越平均场近似的强大框架，但此类模拟仅在对应Fokker--Planck方程的扩散矩阵为正半定时才可行，而这一般条件此前尚不清楚。

Method: 从路径积分表述出发，推导了扩散矩阵为正半定的充分条件（适用于任意哈密顿量、跳跃算符及准概率分布函数的选择），并解析地导出了相应的随机微分方程（SDEs）。同时研究了热力学极限下GKSL方程的动力学，并推导了高阶量子涨落项精确消失的充分条件。

Result: 结果表明，在热力学极限下，取决于跳跃算符的形式，平均场近似可能无法准确描述动力学，使得随机蒙特卡洛模拟变得不可或缺；在某些充分条件下（即使跳跃算符包含二次项），高阶量子涨落项会恒等于零，此时只要SDEs可导出，随机蒙特卡洛模拟即可精确复现GKSL方程的动力学。

Conclusion: 这些结果阐明了随机蒙特卡洛模拟在相空间中准确描述GKSL方程动力学时既可行又必要的条件，为玻色开放量子多体系统的数值模拟提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The Monte Carlo sampling of the stochastic differential equations (SDEs) based on the quasiprobability distribution function, such as the Glauber--Sudarshan P, Wigner, and Husimi Q functions provides a powerful framework for investigating bosonic open quantum many-body dynamics described by the Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad (GKSL) equation, while considering the effects of quantum fluctuations beyond the mean-field approximation. However, the stochastic Monte Carlo simulation is possible only when the corresponding Fokker--Planck equation has a positive-semidefinite diffusion matrix, and the general conditions for the diffusion matrix to be positive semidefinite have remained unclear. In this work, starting from the path integral formulation, we first derive the sufficient conditions under which the diffusion matrix is positive semidefinite for an arbitrary Hamiltonian, jump operators, and choice of quasiprobability distribution functions. We also analytically derive the corresponding SDEs to be solved. We then investigate the dynamics of the GKSL equation in the thermodynamic limit and show that, depending on the form of the jump operators, the mean-field approximation may fail to describe the dynamics accurately, making stochastic Monte Carlo simulations indispensable. Furthermore, we derive the sufficient conditions under which the higher-order quantum fluctuation terms beyond the Fokker--Planck description vanish identically, even when the jump operators contain quadratic terms. Under these conditions, whenever the corresponding SDEs can be derived, the stochastic Monte Carlo simulation reproduces the exact dynamics. These results clarify the conditions under which the stochastic Monte Carlo simulations are both feasible and necessary for accurately describing the dynamics governed by the GKSL equation in phase space.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum transport in a non-Hermitian 1D synthetic lattice: from quantum Zeno reflection to near-perfect absorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06189)
*Emmanuel. D. Mercado-Gutiérrez,W. Xu,E. Gvozdiovas,A. M. Piñeiro,Javier Argüello-Luengo,W. D. Phillips,Q. Zhou,I. B. Spielman*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本论文通过在一维非厄米（non-Hermitian）合成晶格中实验研究波包对耗散区域的吸收行为，利用$^{87}$Rb玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）电子基态超精细流形态作为格点，观测到从弹道传播、近完美吸收到量子芝诺反射（quantum Zeno reflection）的演化过程，并确认最优吸收发生于隧穿与耗散相匹配时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨可控耗散如何成为调控量子动力学的一种资源，并将其应用于量子输运中的波包吸收问题，以拓展耗散工程在量子模拟中的潜力。

Method: 构建一维非厄米合成晶格，该晶格包含耗散子链与非耗散子链之间的突变界面，以$^{87}$Rb BEC的电子基态超精细流形态为格点，通过调节耗散率并辅以数值模拟来研究波包的传播与吸收行为。

Result: 观测到随耗散率变化的三阶段转变：弹道传播、近完美吸收和量子芝诺反射；数值模拟表明最优吸收出现在隧穿与耗散匹配时，且实验结果与理想化半无限模型在所有耗散区间定性一致。

Conclusion: 合成晶格可作为耗散工程化量子输运的多功能量子模拟平台，同时证明受控耗散可以作为一种资源来定制量子动力学的演化行为。

Abstract: We experimentally explore the absorption of a propagating wavepacket impinging upon a dissipative region in an engineered quantum system. We employ a 1D non-Hermitian synthetic lattice with an abrupt interface between dissipative and non-dissipative subchains, using the states of the electronic ground-state hyperfine manifold in a $^{87}$Rb Bose-Einstein condensate as sites. By tuning the dissipation rate, we observe a progression from ballistic propagation, to near-perfect absorption, to quantum Zeno reflection. Guided by numerical simulations, we identify that optimal absorption occurs when tunneling and dissipation are properly matched, and find qualitative agreement with an idealized semi-infinite model across all dissipation regimes. Our results establish synthetic lattices as a versatile Quantum simulation platform for dissipation-engineered quantum transport and highlight controlled dissipation as a resource for tailoring quantum dynamics.

</details>

### [135] [Equivalence of mapping gravitational wave background anisotropy across bases at equal information content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05200)
*Deepali Agarwal*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文证明，在引力波背景（GWB）各向异性重建中，不同基（如像素基和球谐基）生成的主映射（principal maps）尽管显式形式不同，只要它们保留相同的信息量（即张成相同的主子空间），重建的各向异性图是等价的，但在计算成本上可能存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing array, PTA）中重建引力波背景各向异性时，选择像素基或球谐基等不同基会引入截断参数（如像素数或最大多极矩l_max），而基于朴素计数论证设置的截断可能导致重建伪影，且增加截断参数会使逆问题变得病态并需要正则化。因此需要对不同基的重建等价性进行系统分析。

Method: 本文采用主映射方法，即由探测器响应（Fisher）矩阵的优势本征模（dominant eigenmodes）定义的良测子空间来限制重建。通过分析不同基（像素化和球谐展开）生成的主映射在信息内容相同（张成相同主子空间）时的等价性，并选取PTA配置的玩具模型及多种GWB各向异性形态（点源、具有确定性各向异性的扩展源、统计各向同性背景及其角功率谱汇总统计量）作为示例进行验证。

Result: 研究结果表明，不同基生成的重建各向异性图在保留相同信息内容（张成相同主子空间）的条件下彼此等价。尽管重建结果等价，但不同基的计算成本可能存在差异。

Conclusion: 基于不同基的重建在信息内容相当时是等价的，但需要根据计算成本选择合适的基础。文中还简要讨论了地面干涉仪主映射的构建问题。

Abstract: Mapping anisotropies in the gravitational-wave background (GWB) requires choosing a basis to represent the sky intensity, such as pixel and spherical harmonic bases. Each introduces a truncation---e.g., a number of pixels or a maximum multipole l_max---often set by a naive counting argument that relate the number of measurable modes to the number of independent cross-correlations, N_pair, in a pulsar timing array (PTA). However, such truncations can lead to reconstruction artefacts, as they do not reflect the true information content of the PTA response. Increasing the value of truncation parameters spans the observable space better but reveals poorly constrained modes, making the inverse problem ill-conditioned and requiring regularization. A natural approach is to restrict the reconstruction to a well-measured subspace via principal maps, defined by the dominant eigenmodes of the detector response (or Fisher) matrix. However, these maps are not a fundamental parameterization of the sky, but rather, are derived from an underlying representation---such as a pixelization or a spherical harmonic expansion. While their explicit form depends on basis choice, they can span the \textit{same subspace} when the underlying representation is sufficiently complete. Here, we show that reconstructed anisotropy maps via different bases are equivalent, provided they retain the same information content, i.e., span the same principal subspace. As illustrative cases, we consider a toy model for PTA configuration and several GWB anisotropy shapes: point source, an extended source with deterministic anisotropy, and a statistical isotropic background, along with its summary statistic---the angular power spectrum. Although the reconstructions are equivalent, their computational costs can differ. We conclude with brief comments on the construction of principal maps for ground-based interferometers.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Nucleation beyond Equilibrium: Fronts Control Invasion in Bistable Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05251)
*Victor Lequin,Giulio Biroli,Camille Scalliet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文为具有非守恒矢量序参量的反应-扩散系统推导出一种通用的成核理论，证明经典成核理论（CNT）的数学结构在非平衡条件下依然成立，只需将能量量替换为动力学量（前沿速度替代体自由能差、扩散系数替代表面张力）。将该理论应用于两物种Lotka-Volterra模型后发现，强种间竞争会在前沿内产生显著的耗损区，使入侵在固定竞争优势下随竞争增强而呈指数级变难，该预测可在微生物系统中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态动力学普遍违反细致平衡条件（detailed balance），系统由多个耦合的非守恒丰度（即矢量序参量）描述，而经典成核理论基于平衡态的能量景观，不适用于此类系统，目前尚缺少针对矢量序参量的一般性成核理论。

Method: 作者为反应-扩散系统中具有非守恒矢量序参量的情形推导出新的成核理论，该理论同时适用于靠近双节线（binodal）的前沿传播入侵区和靠近旋节线（spinodal）的亚稳态失稳区，并通过大量拟势能（quasipotential）数值计算在两种情形下验证理论。

Result: 理论表明经典成核理论的数学结构在非平衡条件下依然成立，需将能量量替换为前沿的动力学特性：前沿速度扮演体自由能差的角色，扩散系数扮演表面张力的角色。应用于两物种Lotka-Volterra模型时，强种间竞争导致前沿内出现总丰度远低于承载力的耗损区，且该矢量结构使入侵在竞争增强时呈指数级变难。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了非平衡矢量序参量系统缺乏一般性成核理论的空白，并揭示出标量描述无法捕捉的矢量结构对生态入侵动力学的重要影响，且提出了可在微生物系统中检验的定量预测。

Abstract: Bistability, the existence of two alternative stable states with distinct basins of attraction, is common across ecology and many other biological, chemical, and physical systems. In spatially extended systems, the invasion of one state by the other proceeds through nucleation, the fluctuation-driven growth of a droplet beyond a critical size. Classical Nucleation Theory (CNT) quantifies this process using the energy landscape, but this description relies on detailed balance, the condition of microscopic reversibility that holds at equilibrium. Ecological dynamics generally violate detailed balance and are described not by a single scalar field but by several coupled, non-conserved abundances--a vector order parameter--for which no general nucleation theory exists. Here we derive such a theory for reaction-diffusion systems with a non-conserved vector order parameter, valid both close to the binodal, where invasion proceeds through propagating fronts, and close to the spinodal, where the metastable state loses stability. Extensive numerical computations of the quasipotential confirm the theory in both regimes. We show that the mathematical structure of CNT survives out of equilibrium, once energetic quantities are replaced by dynamical properties of fronts: the front speed plays the role of the bulk free-energy difference between phases, and the diffusivity that of the surface tension. Applied to the two-species Lotka-Volterra model, an archetypal system of bistable ecological antagonism, our theory shows that strong interspecific competition generates a pronounced depletion region within fronts, where the total abundance falls well below carrying capacity. This vectorial structure, invisible to a scalar description based on species frequency alone, makes invasion exponentially harder as competition strengthens at fixed competitive advantage--a prediction testable in microbial systems.

</details>


### [137] [Theory of Measurement-Altered Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05289)
*Kabir Khanna,Sara Murciano,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了弱监测Tomonaga-Luttinger液体的理论框架，通过量子场论（quantum field theory）和数值方法揭示：当测量扰动相关（relevant）时，Born平均的关联函数在长距离呈现普适幂律衰减并伴随对数修正，且测量后态系综中表现出宽泛的非高斯多分形（multifractal）涨落，表明物理测量对临界量子态的影响超出了强制测量和传统临界标度的范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部测量会改变无能隙量子物质中的长程关联，但此前对测量如何影响临界量子态的系统性理解尚属空白。作者旨在建立一个理论框架，刻画弱监测下的Tomonaga-Luttinger液体这类一维量子临界态，以阐明测量诱导随机性（measurement-induced randomness）如何重构临界性（criticality）。

Method: 作者发展了replica instanton计算来处理Born平均可观测量，从而应对测量记录的固有随机性；同时采用矩阵乘积态（matrix product state, MPS）数值计算来验证解析结果。核心思想是将测量视为对量子态的一种扰动，在replica形式下计算关联函数的各阶矩，并提取其多分形谱。

Result: 当测量扰动相关时，密度和相位涨落的平均关联函数在长距离呈现普适幂律衰减并伴随对数修正，作者论证这是测量诱导随机性所特有的特征。关联函数各阶矩的完整多分形谱被刻画，揭示出测量后态系综中宽泛且强非高斯的涨落。MPS数值计算与解析结果一致。

Conclusion: 物理测量对临界量子态的影响是一种新型的"测量改变临界性"（measurement-altered criticality），它既不同于强制测量（forced measurements）的效应，也超越了传统临界标度行为。该理论为一维共形场论（conformal field theory）描述的基态提供了测量效应的普适图像，表明测量诱导随机性可以在量子临界系统中产生独特的普适类。

Abstract: Local measurements can alter long-range correlations in gapless quantum matter. We propose a theory of weakly-monitored Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids, a broad class of quantum critical states in one dimension. In order to address the intrinsic randomness of the measurement record, we develop a replica instanton calculation to study Born-averaged observables. We find that when measurements are relevant, average correlators of density and phase fluctuations decay at long distances as universal power laws with logarithmic corrections, a feature we argue is peculiar to measurement-induced randomness. We characterize the full multifractal spectrum of moments of correlations functions, revealing broad, strongly non-gaussian fluctuations across the ensemble of post-measurement states. We support these analytic results with matrix-product-state calculations, and provide a general picture of measurement-altered criticality for ground states described by 1+1d conformal field theories. Our results establish that physical measurements alter critical quantum states in a manner that lies beyond both forced measurements and conventional critical scaling.

</details>


### [138] [Superfluid helium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05354)
*Charles Radin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对超流氦-4（superfluid helium-4）提出一个数学上可求解的玩具模型（toy model），用以描述正常液态与超流态之间的相变（phase transition），并展示如何为超流相获取一个序参量（order parameter）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流氦-4的相变是一个重要的物理问题，但直接建模复杂；作者希望构建一个简化的可解模型，以便理解超流相变的基本机制，并澄清序参量的定义方式。

Method: 作者构造了一个数学上可精确求解的玩具模型，模拟正常液体与超流体之间发生的相变，并在此基础上推导序参量的获取途径。

Result: 该玩具模型能够描述正常液与超流相之间的相变，并成功展示如何从模型中导出超流相的序参量。

Conclusion: 通过可解的玩具模型，作者为超流相变提供了概念上清晰的建模框架，并为如何在超流体系中定义和提取序参量提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We are interested in modelling superfluid helium-4, the common isotope of helium, of atomic number 4. We present a mathematically solvable toy model of the phase transition between the normal liquid and superfluid phases and use it to show how an order parameter might be obtained for the superfluid.

</details>


### [139] [Morphology of frozen labyrinths from irreversible threshold dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05496)
*Daniel Richard Levy*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种不可逆的多数阈动力学（majority threshold dynamics）：每个代理在格点上通过高斯核相互作用，最多只能翻转一次到其局部加权少数状态。研究发现，这种一翻规则使系统冻结在平衡的非共识迷宫中，其形态是受阻粗化（arrested coarsening），且壁位置由确定性动力学决定，更新顺序仅起微调作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数阈动力学在可逆形式下已被精确求解（Merriman-Bence-Osher型阈值动力学），界面沿平均曲率运动。作者希望探究当该规则被改为不可逆时，系统的行为将如何改变，从而揭示不可逆性对模式形成的根本影响。

Method: 在格点上的高斯核（Gaussian kernel）交互设定下定义一翻规则（one-flip rule）：每个代理最多翻转一次出局部加权少数状态。对随机初始条件进行数值模拟，并通过固定初始条件、改变更新顺序的干预实验来区分确定性动力学与随机过程的各自贡献。

Result: 一翻规则使系统冻结在平衡的非共识迷宫图案中，而可逆松弛会使这些图案消失。图案化窗口由初始自旋组成的标准化偏差（standardized bias）控制，其阈值在四倍尺度范围内与尺度无关。冻结形态为受阻粗化，特征宽度随尺度亚线性增长。粗粒度域布局具有确定性，更新顺序仅在确定的壁位置上进行次级的首达（first-passage）微调，壁处标记着挫败（frustration）特征。

Conclusion: 不可逆性的引入使系统从可逆的均值曲率运动转变为冻结的非平衡图案状态。图案的粗粒度结构由确定性动力学决定，挫败特征在可逆弛豫中完全缺失，且几乎不携带图案的大尺度形状信息，揭示了不可逆性与可逆模式形成之间的本质差异。

Abstract: Majority threshold dynamics, in which each agent adopts the dominant state in a weighted neighborhood, relaxes a binary field toward consensus or stripes. We study what happens when this rule is made irreversible: each agent, interacting through a Gaussian kernel on a lattice, may flip out of its local weighted minority at most once. The reversible form is threshold dynamics of Merriman-Bence-Osher type, an exactly solvable calibration in which interfaces move by mean curvature with closed-form lattice pinning and mobility. Irreversibility changes the outcome. From random initial conditions, the one-flip rule freezes balanced non-consensus labyrinths that reversible relaxation drives away. The patterned regime is a window of initial spin compositions around equal balance, narrowing as the interaction range grows, controlled by a standardized bias whose onset is independent of scale over a fourfold range. The frozen morphology is arrested coarsening: bicontinuous at balance, with a feature width that grows sublinearly and falls below the interaction range at large scales. We determine the mechanism by intervention. Fixing the initial condition while varying the update order shows that the coarse domain layout is deterministic, while the update order enters only in a secondary first-passage race that fine-tunes the wall positions the deterministic dynamics has already set. Those walls are marked by frustration: agents frozen against their own local field. This signature is identically absent from any reversible relaxation, is overwhelmingly interfacial, and carries almost none of the pattern's large-scale shape.

</details>


### [140] [Entanglement Scaling and Full Counting Statistics in Excited States of Two-Dimensional Rotating Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05722)
*Priyangshu Goswami,Abhishek Dhar,Satya N. Majumdar,Anupam Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了囚禁在二维简谐势阱中并以角频率Ω旋转的N粒子费米子激发态纠缠熵。作者解析计算了半径为r的圆盘内Rényi熵和粒子数涨落累积量，证明面积律在激发态中仍成立，验证了纠缠熵与累积量的级数展开，并进一步将结果推广到环形区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子多体物理中，纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）是理解量子相变和拓扑有序的关键量，但以往研究主要集中在基态（ground state）；激发态（excited state）的纠缠结构尚未得到充分理解，本研究旨在探究旋转费米子系统激发态的纠缠熵行为。

Method: 作者选取填充特定N个单粒子能级的激发态，利用解析方法计算了圆形区域内q阶Rényi熵（Rényi entropy）及对应的粒子数涨落累积量（cumulant）；进一步推导了居中累积量生成函数（centered cumulant generating function），并将结果从圆盘区域推广至环形（annular）区域，检验其拆分性质。

Result: 研究发现面积律（area law）标度对激发态仍然成立；纠缠熵关于粒子数累积量的级数展开在非相互作用费米子情形下得到验证。所得概率分布函数与基态结果具有相同的标度性质（仅相差变量平移）。环形区域的Rényi熵和粒子数累积量均可分解为两个包围圆盘对应量之和，该加和关系在环宽足够大时成立。

Conclusion: 本研究将纠缠熵与粒子数涨落的分析从基态拓展到一类激发态，揭示出该激发态与基态在标度性质上的统一性，并为环形几何下的纠缠结构提供了圆盘之和的简洁分解关系。

Abstract: We investigate the entanglement entropy of a class of $N$-particle excited state of fermions confined in a two-dimensional harmonic trap rotating at an angular frequency $Ω$. The excited state is constructed by filling a particular set of $N$ single-particle energy levels. We analytically compute the Rényi entropies of order $q$ in a disc of radius $r$ around the centre of the trap, and the cumulants corresponding to number fluctuations of fermions within the disc. We found that the area law scaling of entanglement entropy holds even for a class of excited states. We also verified the well-known series expansion of entanglement entropy in terms of the particle number cumulants for non-interacting fermions. We further derive the centered cumulant generating function, demonstrating that the associated probability distribution function in the disc has identical scaling properties, up to a variable shift, to the known ground-state result. Finally, we extend our result to an annular region, showing that both the Rényi entropy and particle number cumulants of the annulus decompose into sums of the corresponding quantities of the two bounding discs. These additive relations hold as long as the width of the annulus is sufficiently large.

</details>


### [141] [Computing Shear Viscosities from Molecular Dynamics Simulation: Comparing the OrthoBoXY Approach with the Green-Kubo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05845)
*Marcel Brandt,Ralf Ludwig,Dietmar Paschek*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用OrthoBoXY方法对15种分子液体的剪切黏度进行了平衡分子动力学（equilibrium molecular dynamics, MD）模拟计算，并与Green-Kubo方法的结果进行了交叉验证。研究发现分子液体的黏度在低至250分子的体系下不受有限尺寸效应影响，且标准误差几乎与体系大小无关。文章进一步提出了OrthoBoXY模拟中块长度（block length）参数τ_block的缩放优化方案，可使高黏度体系将该参数缩小至1/8，中等黏度体系缩小至1/4，在250分子的小体系下可实现高达24倍的计算成本缩减而不损失数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子液体黏度的精确计算对工业应用和基础研究均具有重要意义，而OrthoBoXY方法作为一种新的黏度计算途径，缺乏系统的误差分析与参数优化指导。作者旨在验证OrthoBoXY方法的可靠性，揭示有限尺寸效应的影响规律，并提供最优的计算参数配置以降低计算成本。

Method: 通过平衡分子动力学模拟，采用OrthoBoXY方法计算15种纯净分子液体的剪切黏度，并与Green-Kubo方法的结果进行对比验证。通过改变模拟体系大小（最小至250分子）和运行时长，系统考察有限尺寸效应、统计误差以及块长度参数τ_block的影响，并据此提出参数优化方案。

Result: OrthoBoXY与Green-Kubo方法计算结果高度一致；在低至250分子的体系中黏度不受有限尺寸效应影响，标准误差也几乎与体系大小无关，这源于自扩散系数精度随体系增大而提升与OrthoBoXY方程中体系大小相关权重之间的补偿效应。基于此，建议采用更小体系配合更长模拟时间。对于高黏度体系，τ_block可取原推荐的1/8；对低黏度体系可取1/4；而对高流动性体系不宜缩减。在250分子体系中，这些优化可带来最高24倍的计算成本降低。

Conclusion: OrthoBoXY方法是一种可靠且高效的分子液体黏度计算工具，其计算结果与Green-Kubo方法一致。有限尺寸效应在极小体系下即可忽略，结合τ_block参数的分体系类型缩放策略，可在保持数值精度的前提下大幅降低计算资源需求。

Abstract: We calculated shear viscosities of 15 neat molecular liquids from equilibrium molecular dynamics (MD) simulations using the OrthoBoXY approach and compare them to viscosities calculated via the Green-Kubo method. Data from both methods agree very well. Here, we show how to avoid pitfalls while computing the OrthoBoXY-data to obtain optimal results. From simulations of multiple system sizes, we could verify that the viscosity of molecular liquids is not influenced by finite size effects down to systems as small as 250 molecules. Moreover, we demonstrate that also the standard error of the viscosity is nearly independent of the system size. This is shown to be a consequence of a compensation effect of an increasing accuracy of the self-diffusion coefficients with increasing systems-size and the system-size dependent weighting according to the OrthoBoXY-equation. As a consequence, we suggest that it is preferable to run simulations of smaller systems with longer simulation times rather than larger systems with shorter simulation runs. In addition, we discuss a refinement of the recently introduced "recipe" for OrthoBoXY simulations block-lengths $τ_\mathrm{block}$. Based on data from simulations with varying run-lengths, we suggest the following modification: for highly viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/8$, significantly reducing the computational resources needed. For less viscous systems, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ might safely be scaled by a factor of $1/4$. For systems with high fluidity, the value of $τ_\mathrm{block}$ should not be scaled down in order to achieve reliable results. When using a smaller system size of 250 molecules, these refinements are leading up to a 24-fold reduction in computational cost compared to the previous recommended set-up without sacrificing numerical accuracy.

</details>


### [142] [Measurement-induced entanglement Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06006)
*Viktor Eisler,Erik Tonni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一条无限跳跃链（infinite hopping chain）在占据数基（occupation basis）下进行部分投影测量后，其基态纠缠哈密顿量（entanglement Hamiltonian）的结构。研究发现，对于被两个测量区域与链其余部分隔开的一段区间，其约化密度矩阵可通过共形映射（conformal mapping）和规范变换（gauge transformation）与巨正则系综态联系起来，纠缠哈密顿量由局域逆温度描述，该温度在端点处以平方根方式消失且与测量结果无关；而局域化学势则与段内感应电荷密度相关，表明后选择态的纠缠哈密顿量包含比相应熵更多的测量结果信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解投影测量后量子系统基态的纠缠结构，特别是纠缠哈密顿量如何编码测量结果的信息，以及它与传统熵的关系。

Method: 采用底层场论描述，通过共形映射和规范变换将后测量态的约化密度矩阵与巨正则系综态建立联系，并分析纠缠哈密顿量中逆温度和化学势的空间分布。

Result: 纠缠哈密顿量由局域逆温度描述，该温度在测量区域端点处以平方根规律趋于零，且与具体测量结果无关；相反，局域化学势与段内诱导电荷密度相关联，从而依赖于测量结果。

Conclusion: 后选择态的纠缠哈密顿量比熵携带更多关于测量结果的信息，这表明仅用熵无法完全刻画后测量量子态的纠缠特性，局域化学势提供了一个更细致的刻画手段。

Abstract: We study the entanglement Hamiltonian of an infinite hopping chain in its ground state, after partial projective measurements in the occupation basis. For a segment separated by two measurement regions from the rest of the chain, we show that the reduced density matrix can be related to a grand-canonical state via a conformal mapping and a gauge transformation in the underlying field-theory description. The entanglement Hamiltonian is then described by a local inverse temperature that vanishes as a square root around the endpoints and is independent of the particular measurement outcome. In sharp contrast, the local chemical potential is shown to be related to the induced charge density in the segment. Hence the entanglement Hamiltonian of a post-selected state contains much more information on the measurement outcome than the respective entropy.

</details>


### [143] [Dynamic scaling behavior in the presence of a periodic magnetic driving across Ising continuous transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05936)
*Andrea Pelissetto,Ettore Vicari*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究由周期性均匀外源驱动的经典铁磁系统在临界点附近的临界动力学行为。以二维伊辛模型为范例，在临界温度下施加周期性磁场 h(t)=-A cos(2πt/P)，发现周期性驱动导致由时间 t、振幅 A 与周期 P 间非平凡相互作用产生的独特动态标度行为。关键标度变量为 τ=t/P 和 σ=A P^κ（κ=y_h/z≈0.865），磁化强度与键能密度的动态标度行为围绕平滑曲线振荡，并趋于大 τ 下的稳态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解经典铁磁系统在周期性驱动下跨越连续相变时产生的临界动力学行为，并揭示时间、振幅与周期之间的非平凡标度关系。

Method: 以二维（2D）伊辛模型（Ising model）为范例，在周期性磁场 h(t)=-A cos(2πt/P) 驱动下，于临界温度采用纯弛豫动力学演化。引入标度变量 τ=t/P 与 σ=A P^κ，其中 κ=y_h/z，y_h=(d+2-η)/2 为磁场临界维度，z 为临界弛豫动力学动态指数（二维伊辛模型下 κ≈0.865），由此分析磁化强度与键能密度的动态标度行为。

Result: 在热力学极限下，周期性驱动导致独特的动态标度行为，磁化强度与键能密度的动态标度行为表现为围绕平滑曲线的振荡，该平滑曲线随 τ 增大趋于稳态行为。此外，零磁场下周期性时变温度驱动伊辛系统跨越临界点的动态行为亦被简要讨论。

Conclusion: 周期性磁场驱动下的临界动力学展现出与参数 t、A、P 之间非平凡耦合相关的独特标度规律，且动态标度行为在长时间极限下收敛于稳态，为该类非平衡临界现象提供了理论描述框架。

Abstract: We study the critical dynamics arising from a time-dependent periodic homogenous source coupled to the order-parameter field, which drives a classical ferromagnetic system across a continuous transition. For this purpose, we consider the paradigmatic two-dimensional (2D) Ising model in the presence of a periodic magnetic field $h(t)=-A\, \cos (2πt/P)$, evolving under a purely relaxational dynamics at the critical temperature. We show that the periodic driving gives rise to a peculiar dynamic scaling behavior in the thermodynamic limit, arising from a nontrivial interplay among the time $t$, the amplitude $A$ and period $P$ of $h(t)$. The relevant scaling variables are $τ=t/P$ and $σ=A P^κ$, with $κ= y_h/z$, where $y_h=(d+2-η)/2$ is the critical dimension of the magnetic field, and $z$ is dynamic exponent for the critical relaxational dynamics ($κ\approx 0.865$ for the 2D Ising model). The dynamic scaling behaviors of the magnetization and bond-energy density show an oscillatory behavior around a smooth curve which approaches a large-$τ$ stationary behavior. We also briefly discuss the dynamic behavior of an Ising system driven across the critical point by a periodic time-varying temperature at zero magnetic field.

</details>


### [144] [Thermodynamic statistics of given names in USA and France](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06048)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 利用美国和法国的官方政府数据，对超过100年时间尺度的名字出现/频率/流行度分布进行分析，发现这些分布可通过洛伦兹曲线和帕累托曲线描述，基尼系数稳定在0.85–0.95范围内。作者指出名字分布与财富不平等类似，可用瑞利-琼斯（Rayleigh-Jeans）热化与凝聚现象来描述，且名字间的时间相关性在20世纪中期前保持稳定，此后发生显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨名字流行度分布的统计规律，并将其与财富不平等分析中广泛使用的洛伦兹和帕累托曲线框架相联系，以期揭示社会文化现象背后的普适物理规律。

Method: 基于美国和法国的官方政府数据集，对超过100年时间尺度上的名字出现频率/流行度分布进行统计分析；采用洛伦兹曲线和帕累托曲线刻画分布特征，计算基尼系数；类比物理系统中的能量状态，将名字流行度对应为能量态，引入瑞利-琼斯热化与凝聚现象进行建模；同时分析名字之间的时间相关性。

Result: 名字流行度分布在考察时间期内保持稳定，基尼系数维持在0.85–0.95的窄范围内；名字分布与财富不平等类似，可由瑞利-琼斯热化与凝聚现象良好描述；名字间的时间相关性在20世纪中期之前保持稳定，此后发生显著变化。

Conclusion: 名字流行度分布遵循与财富不平等相似的统计规律，可由物理系统中的瑞利-琼斯热化与凝聚机制解释，反映出社会文化现象背后存在与物理系统相通的普适统计规律；时间相关性的阶段性变化表明20世纪中叶后社会文化动态发生了显著转变。

Abstract: Using official government data sets of USA and France we analyze the occurrence/frequency/popularity distributions of given names on a time scale of more than 100 years. These distributions are characterized through the Lorenz and Pareto curves broadly used in the analysis of wealth inequality in the world. These curves remain stable during the considered time period with the Gini coefficient remaining in the narrow range 0.85-0.95. As for the case of wealth inequality, we show that the distributions of names are well described by the Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) thermalization and condensation phenomenon well studied in various physical systems. The RJ thermalization results from two integrals of motion being analogous to energy and probability norm conservation in physical systems with energy states corresponding to popularity levels of names. Time correlations between names are also determined showing their stability until the middle of the twentieth century and a significant change after that.

</details>


### [145] [Collective search-and-capture under competing assignment policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06084)
*Néstor Sepúlveda*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一个活性搜索-捕获（active search-and-capture）的最小格点模型，其中持久随机游走者通过有限范围、互斥的分配规则定位并不可逆地捕获静止目标。研究发现集体完成时间$T_c$随重取向率$\alpha$呈非单调变化，且受极端晚时尾支配；同时表明分配策略（尤其是最大基数匹配）对捕获时间的控制力强于游走者的持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集体搜索-捕获问题中，目标捕获的完成时间如何受到游走者运动属性和目标分配规则的共同调控，尚未得到系统理解。论文旨在厘清捕获时间是由游走者的持久性（persistence）主导，还是由分配策略主导。

Method: 作者构建了一个最小格点模型，其中持久随机游走者（persistent random walkers）通过有限范围（finite-range）、互斥（mutually exclusive）的分配规则不可逆地捕获静止目标。系统测量了集体完成时间$T_c$随重取向率$\alpha$和搜索半径$R$的变化；并将基线单轮分配规则与级联再分配（cascading reassignment）和候选图上的最大基数匹配（maximum-cardinality matching）三种策略进行比较。

Result: $T_c(\alpha)$呈非单调依赖，在中等持久性处存在最小值，且该最小值深度随$R$增大而减小。捕获动力学显示$T_c$由捕获过程的极端晚时尾（extreme late-time tail）控制，该尾主要受自由探索阶段支配而非最终定向接近阶段。两种贪心策略在$R$极小时一致，而最大基数匹配在中等$R$时即产生显著加速：在$R$大时$T_c$降低数倍，在中$R$时降低超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 在该集体、耗竭耦合（depletion-coupled）的搜索问题中，分配策略对捕获时间的控制作用强于游走者的持久性。这意味着优化目标分配规则比调节运动参数更能有效降低集体搜索-捕获任务的完成时间。

Abstract: We study a minimal lattice model of active search-and-capture in which persistent random walkers locate and irreversibly capture immobile targets through a finite-range, mutually exclusive assignment rule. We measure the collective completion time $T_c$ as a function of the walkers' reorientation rate $α$ and the search radius $R$. The dependence $T_c(α)$ is non-monotonic, with a minimum at intermediate persistence whose depth decreases as $R$ grows. Capture kinetics show that $T_c$ is not a typical capture time but is governed by the extreme, late-time tail of the capture process, while the bulk of targets are captured much earlier; this tail is controlled mainly by the free-exploration phase rather than by the final directed approach. We then compare the baseline single-round assignment rule with cascading reassignment and with maximum-cardinality matching on a candidate graph. The two greedy policies (single-round and cascading) agree at very small $R$, whereas maximum-cardinality matching already produces a strong speedup at moderate $R$: improved matching reduces $T_c$ by factors of several at large $R$, and by more than an order of magnitude at moderate $R$. Thus, in this collective, depletion-coupled search problem, the assignment policy can control the capture time more strongly than the walkers' persistence.

</details>


### [146] [Transport coefficients in hard-sphere fluids: thermodynamic versus kinetic descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06143)
*Miguel Hoyuelos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文从Onsager矩阵的一般表达式出发，建立了硬球流体输运系数的热力学理论。理论预测输运系数与稀薄气体值之比σ/σ_id仅取决于平衡热力学性质，可从状态方程直接计算。对热导率、黏度和自扩散系数导出的紧凑表达式在几乎整个流体范围内定量再现模拟数据，其中自扩散系数的预测显著优于Enskog动力学理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上输运系数的计算通常依赖于分子动力学层面的微观理论（如Enskog动力学理论），此类方法在某些情形下精度有限。该论文旨在探索能否仅通过平衡热力学框架纯热力学地准确描述输运系数，从而建立输运性质与状态方程之间的直接联系。

Method: 从Onsager矩阵（Onsager matrix）的一般表达式出发，发展硬球流体的输运系数热力学理论。理论推导了输运系数σ与其稀薄气体值σ_id之比，表明该比值仅依赖平衡热力学性质，因此可直接由状态方程（equation of state）计算。针对热导率、黏度和自扩散系数分别导出了紧凑表达式。

Result: 所推导的热导率、黏度和自扩散系数表达式在几乎整个流体范围内定量地再现了模拟数据；其中自扩散系数的结果显著改进了Enskog动力学理论（Enskog kinetic theory）的预测精度。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，输运系数可以在纯热力学框架内得到准确描述，输运性质与平衡态热力学性质之间存在内在联系，为输运系数的计算提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: A thermodynamic theory for transport coefficients in hard-sphere fluids is developed from a general expression for the Onsager matrix. The theory predicts the ratio $σ/σ_{\rm id}$, where $σ$ is a transport coefficient and $σ_{\rm id}$ denotes its dilute-gas value. This ratio depends exclusively on equilibrium thermodynamic properties and can therefore be computed directly from the equation of state. Compact expressions are obtained for the thermal conductivity, viscosity, and self-diffusion coefficient. These expressions quantitatively reproduce simulation data over almost the entire fluid range and, in the case of self-diffusion, significantly improve upon the predictions of Enskog kinetic theory. The results demonstrate that transport coefficients can be accurately described within a purely thermodynamic framework.

</details>


### [147] [A Universal Control Budget for First-Passage Kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06368)
*Shiling Liang,Ruicheng Bao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现任意有限马尔可夫链（finite Markov chain）的平均首通时间（mean first-passage time）对任意速率的对数敏感性绝对值不超过1，且所有敏感性之和为-1，两者共同构成一个守恒的"控制预算"（conserved control budget），约束了反应完成时间对速率变化的响应上限，并进而限制了动力学校对（kinetic proofreading）的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首通时间是反应完成（reaction completion）的自然观测量，但其平均值如何响应速率变化一直缺乏一般性约束。

Method: 推导并证明了有限马尔可夫链中平均首通时间对任意速率的对数敏感性满足两个普适定律：单速率的敏感性绝对值以1为界，以及所有敏感性之和等于-1。

Result: 两条定律共同构成一个守恒的控制预算：加快部分跃迁所缩短的时间必须由其他跃迁的减缓来买单；协同改变多个速率时，完成时间的移动幅度不会超过单一速率所能达到的最大效果。此外，提高活化势垒或改变势阱深度虽同时移动多个速率，也无法使完成时间偏移超过单一速率的效应。

Conclusion: 该预算将动力学校对的判别能力限制在校验点数量（checkpoint count）之内，并以其对底物浓度（substrate concentration）的敏感性来为这种判别定价。这些约束为反应动力学中完成时间的调控提供了普适的守恒法则。

Abstract: The first-passage time is the natural observable of reaction completion, yet how its mean responds to a rate change has lacked a general constraint. We show that the logarithmic sensitivity of the mean first-passage time of any finite Markov chain to any rate is bounded by one in magnitude, and that these sensitivities sum to -1. Together the two laws form a conserved control budget: speeding completion through some transitions must be paid for by others, and a coordinated change shifts the completion time only as far as the budget allows. Raising an activation barrier or shifting the depth of a well moves many rates at once, yet neither can shift the completion time further than a single rate could. The budget caps kinetic-proofreading discrimination at the checkpoint count, and prices it in sensitivity to substrate concentration.

</details>

### [148] [A floor and a ceiling for the advancing contact angle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05515)
*Yong Sung Park*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过自由表面围绕移动接触线的铰链运动（hinged motion）机制，解释了前进接触角（advancing contact angle）最大值在87°至147°之间分散的现象。研究发现存在一个接触角带（band），介于90°（无壁面剪切）和θ_h=128.73°（共振点）之间，瞬态前进角无法离开此带。对68个液-固体系的汇编数据验证了这一带的边界与两条出口路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观测到前进接触角的最大值在同一系统研究中散布于87°至147°之间，且静态角低至5°的液体动态最大角接近90°，现有理论无法解释这一大范围分散现象，需要建立统一机制说明。

Method: 利用楔形绕接触线旋转的局部Stokes解（local Stokes solution），分析自由表面铰链运动在移动接触线附近的奇异角度结构。通过解析两个奇异角（90°与θ_h=128.73°，满足tan 2α=2α）之间的接触角带，并结合来自9个来源、5种配置的68个液-固体系的实验数据汇编验证理论预测。

Result: 理论预测存在一个接触角带，瞬态前进角无法离开；90°处铰链运动不产生壁面剪切，旋转自由；θ_h=128.73°处与r²本征解发生共振，通过r² ln r项阻止旋转。实验汇编确认：不受装置限制、θ_s≤40°的系统无论化学性质如何均达到87°至119°；超过θ_h的情况仅在静角位于带内之上或流动离开准稳态时出现。垂直壁上水线记录在单一非定常实验中展示了该带及90°跨界时的接触角安静化现象。

Conclusion: 前进接触角最大值的大范围分散源于移动接触线附近自由表面的铰链运动所固有的奇异角度结构，而非各体系的化学差异。该机制给出了接触角带的两个出口条件，为理解动态润湿（dynamic wetting）行为提供了统一的解析框架。

Abstract: Reported maxima of the advancing contact angle scatter from $87^\circ$ to $147^\circ$ in a single systematic study, and liquids on surfaces with static angles as low as $5^\circ$ reach dynamic maxima near $90^\circ$. We show that both observations follow from the hinged motion of the free surface near a moving contact line. The local Stokes solution for a wedge rotating about its contact line has two singular angles of opposite character: at $90^\circ$ the hinged motion generates no wall shear and the rotation is free, and at $θ_h = 128.73^\circ$, where $\tan 2α= 2α$, a resonance with the $r^2$ eigensolution arrests the rotation through an $r^2 \ln r$ term. Between the two angles lies a band that a transient advancing angle cannot leave while the interface near the contact line remains a quasi-steady wedge. A compilation of 68 liquid-solid systems from nine sources and five configurations confirms the band and its two exits: systems not limited by the apparatus, with $θ_s \le 40^\circ$, reach $87^\circ$ to $119^\circ$ regardless of chemistry, and every exceedance of $θ_h$ occurs either where chemistry places the static angle above the band or where the flow leaves the quasi-steady regime. The record of a waterline on a vertical wall exhibits the band within a single unsteady experiment, together with a distinct quieting of the local contact angle at the crossing of $90^\circ$.

</details>


### [149] [Near-field Hydrodynamics Disentangles Angular Correlations in Confined Active Suspensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05756)
*Changle Liao,Haruki Hayano,Yuto Uesugi,Akira Furukawa,Daiki Nishiguchi,Kazumasa A. Takeuchi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验与流体动力学模拟相结合，研究了准二维莱茵衣藻（Chlamydomonas reinhardtii）悬浮液中活性物质的粒子间空间关联，揭示了偶极模式与卷入模式两种解耦的关联模式，并证明其源于奇异流体动力学与润滑引起的卷入效应之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然受限活性物质（confined active matter）中的集体有序已被广泛表征，但几何约束如何重塑近场流动及由此产生的粒子间关联仍鲜有探索，这是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 作者结合实验与流体动力学模拟（hydrodynamic simulations）考察准二维莱茵衣藻悬浮液中的粒子间关联，并综合单细胞流场分析、流体动力学模拟以及活性-被动混合体系来关联观察到的两种模式与相应的流体力学机制。

Result: 研究揭示细胞对中存在两种解耦模式：偶极模式（dipolar mode）和卷入模式（entrainment mode），二者表现出密度依赖和距离依赖的竞争关系；并证明这两种模式分别与奇异流体动力学（singular hydrodynamics）和润滑引起的卷入（lubrication-induced entrainment）相关联。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，受限活性物质中的时空关联（spatiotemporal correlations）从根本上源于这两种流体动力学机制的相互作用。

Abstract: Spatial confinement profoundly impacts the transport and self-organization of active matter across diverse biological systems. While the collective orders in confined active matter have been extensively characterized, how geometric constraints reshape near-field flows and the resulting inter-particle correlations remains largely unexplored. In this study, we combine experiments and hydrodynamic simulations to investigate inter-particle correlations within quasi-two-dimensional Chlamydomonas reinhardtii suspensions. We reveal two disentangled modes characterizing cell pairs: a dipolar mode and an entrainment mode, which exhibit a density- and distance-dependent competition. Combining single-cell flow field analysis, hydrodynamic simulations, and active-passive mixtures, we link these two modes to singular hydrodynamics and lubrication-induced entrainment. Our results demonstrate that spatiotemporal correlations in confined active matter are fundamentally rooted in the interplay of these two hydrodynamic mechanisms.

</details>


### [150] [Large Spin-Wave Fluctuations Suppress Activity in Malthusian Flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05805)
*Emir Sezik,Gunnar Pruessner*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究在二维马尔萨斯（Malthusian）群聚（flocking）模型中识别出一种此前未被注意的相态，其动力学与平衡态的XY模型等价。通过对称性分析推导出Goldstone模式的有效运动方程，并利用微扰重整化群（perturbative RG）方法确定了一个新型临界点及其普适标度行为。该相变源于活性（activity）与自旋波（spin-wave）的相互作用，而非平衡态中的涡旋（vortex）自由度，但其RG流与BKT（Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless）相变相似，且在强噪声下活性变得无关紧要，系统交叉到平衡XY普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对马尔萨斯群聚模型的研究主要集中于星彩（aster）增殖之前中间区域的标度指数，其他可能的相态尚未被探究。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统地识别该模型可能展示的其他相及相变行为。

Method: 作者首先通过识别模型的对称性来推导Goldstone模式的有效运动方程，并分析自旋波（spin-wave）涨落。随后利用微扰重整化群（perturbative RG）方法，确定新发现的临界点附近的RG流，并计算临界点处的普适标度行为及其对数修正。

Result: 文章识别出一个此前未被发现的相态，其动力学等价于平衡态XY模型，并确定了连接两个不同相态的新型临界点。在临界点处计算了普适标度行为及对数修正。该相变来源于活性与自旋波的相互作用，而非平衡XY模型中的涡旋自由度，但其RG流与BKT相变的RG流类似；在足够强的噪声下，活性变为无关量，系统交叉到平衡XY普适类。

Conclusion: 马尔萨斯群聚模型除了已知的中间标度区之外，还存在与平衡XY模型等价的相态以及一个新型临界点。该相变机制与平衡BKT相变不同（活性-自旋波相互作用 vs. 涡旋），但二者在重整化群意义上属于相似的普适类行为。

Abstract: Novel phases, beyond long-range order in two dimensions, have continued to be discovered within flocking models, establishing flocking as one of the pivotal paradigms in active matter. However, much of the discussion around ``Malthusian'' (constant density) flocks, an analytically more tractable alternative to the Vicsek model, has centred around the scaling exponents governing the intermediate regime prior to the proliferation of asters, leaving open the question of what other phases the model might display. Here, we study the two-dimensional dynamics of Malthusian flocks and identify a previously unnoticed phase, where the dynamics is that of the equilibrium XY Model. By identifying the symmetries of the model, we derive the effective equations of motion for the Goldstone modes and analyse the spin-wave fluctuations. We identify a novel critical point separating two distinct phases and, using a perturbative RG procedure, determine the RG flows in its vicinity. This allows us to calculate the universal scaling behaviour at the critical point, along with its logarithmic corrections. The novel phase transition here is due to the interaction of activity and spin-waves, unlike the equilibrium counterpart, which undergoes a phase transition in effective degrees of freedom, namely vortices. Nevertheless, the RG flows are similar to those of the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transition, and we show that for sufficiently strong noise, the activity becomes irrelevant and the system crosses over to the equilibrium XY universality class.

</details>


### [151] [True and Quasi Long-Range Order in Malthusian Flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05851)
*Patrick Jentsch,Anna Erzberger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用非微扰重整化群（nonperturbative renormalization group）方法研究马尔萨斯流（Malthusian flocks）的流体动力学理论，明确获得了控制真正长程有序（true long-range order）的强耦合不动点（strong-coupling fixed point），发现了准长程有序相（quasi long-range ordered phase），并在相变点识别出一个类似于但不完全等同于Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless普适类的临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命物质的持续更替（turnover）使其动力学行为复杂，马尔萨斯流的水动力学理论虽能描述具有更替的极性活性物质（polar active matter），但其相图和非线性标度行为尚不清楚，这正是本研究的出发点。

Method: 采用非微扰重整化群方法，在导数展开至第二阶且保持旋转不变性（rotationally invariant）的条件下进行研究，同时不考虑缺陷（defects）的影响。

Result: 研究明确获得了决定真正长程有序的强耦合不动点，揭示了准长程有序相的存在，并在相变处确定了与Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless普适类相似但彼此相异的临界点。

Conclusion: 马尔萨斯流系统在无需缺陷的条件下即可展现丰富的相图，包含真正长程有序、准长程有序以及一个独特的临界点，后者与经典的BKT普适类存在本质区别，拓展了对活性物质更替体系相变行为的理解。

Abstract: Living matter undergoes continuous turnover. The hydrodynamic theory of Malthusian flocks describes polar active matter with turnover, but its phase diagram and nonlinear scaling behavior is not well understood. Using a nonperturbative renormalization group approach, rotationally invariant to second order in derivatives, and without defects, we explicitly obtain the strong-coupling fixed point governing true long-range order, uncover a quasi long-range ordered phase, and identify a critical point similar to, but distinct from the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless universality class at the transition.

</details>

### [152] [Nonproportional Response of a GAGG Scintillator to Beta and Gamma Radiation over a Wide Energy Range in PIKACHU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05601)
*Takumi Omori,Takashi Iida,Yosuke Suga,Masao Yoshino,Ken-Ichi Fushimi,Azusa Gando,Nobuo Hinohara,Keishi Hosokawa,Motonao Ishigami,Kei Kamada,Tadafumi Kishimoto,Keita Mizukoshi,Takuya Sako,Yasuhiro Shoji,Shota Sugi*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本研究针对GAGG闪烁体中子能量阈值以下辐射的光产额非比例响应进行了精确测量，发现γ射线的非比例响应强于β射线，并将测量结果纳入背景模型后显著提高了PIKACHU实验背景谱的重现精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非比例响应（nonproportional response）是无机闪烁体在亚MeV辐射下的已知特性，而在PIKACHU实验中，精确理解GAGG闪烁体的非比例响应对背景建模至关重要，但此前缺乏针对β和γ射线的系统评估。

Method: 采用锗探测器的康普顿符合技术（Compton coincidence technique）测量β射线的响应，并使用多种单能γ射线源评估γ射线响应，在50–2614 keV能量范围内对两种射线进行了系统比较。

Result: 测量结果表明β与γ射线表现出不同的非比例响应，其中γ射线的非比例响应强于β射线；将实测非比例响应纳入背景模型后，背景谱的重现精度较未修正模型明显提高。

Conclusion: 精确评估GAGG闪烁体的非比例响应十分重要，不仅对PIKACHU实验的背景建模至关重要，也对基于闪烁体的实验进行精确能量刻度（energy calibration）具有普遍意义。

Abstract: Nonproportional response of scintillation light yield for sub-MeV radiations is a well-known characteristic of inorganic scintillators. In the PIKACHU experiment, a precise understanding of the nonproportional response of the GAGG ($\mathrm{Ce:Gd_3Al_2Ga_3O_{12}}$) scintillator is essential for background modeling. In this study, the nonproportional response of the GAGG scintillator was evaluated for both $β$ and $γ$ rays. The response for $β$ rays was measured using the Compton coincidence technique with a germanium detector, while that for $γ$ rays was evaluated using several monoenergetic $γ$-ray sources. The measurement results showed different responses for $β$ and $γ$ rays, with a stronger nonproportional response for $γ$ rays than for $β$ rays over the energy range of 50--2614~keV. The background model incorporating the measured nonproportional responses reproduced the measured background spectra more accurately compared with the model without the nonproportional response correction. These results demonstrate the importance of precise evaluation of the nonproportional response of the GAGG scintillator which is essential not only for background modeling in the PIKACHU experiment but also for accurate energy calibration in scintillator-based experiments.

</details>

### [153] [A priori error estimator for reduced-order models based on the higher-order Craig-Bampton method in dynamic substructuring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05625)
*Jaemin Kim,Seung-Hwan Boo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对Craig-Bampton (CB)方法及其高阶版本(Higher-order Craig-Bampton, HCB)提出了基于嵌套Ritz子空间结构的分层误差估计框架，在不求解全阶特征值问题的前提下预测降阶模型的固有频率误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降阶模型的精度评估通常需要求解全阶问题，但这违背了模型降阶的初衷；HCB方法虽已被证明优于CB方法，但尚缺乏无需求解全阶问题的先验误差估计器（a priori error estimator）。

Method: 利用HCB方法中嵌套Ritz子空间的结构，建立分层估计框架：将更高阶的解作为相邻低阶解的参考基准。具体包括两大部分：(i)基于Rayleigh商扰动分析（Rayleigh quotient perturbation analysis）推导广义CB误差估计器；(ii)以HCB-2特征解为参考，提出新的HCB-1误差估计器。

Result: 在几何复杂度不同的多个数值模型上，两种误差估计器均得到验证，证明了该分层框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了HCB方法缺乏先验误差估计的空白，提供了一种无需求解全阶问题即可评估降阶模型精度的方法，推动了动态子结构降阶技术在工程实践中的可可靠性应用。

Abstract: The Craig-Bampton (CB) method is a widely used dynamic substructuring technique based on component mode synthesis (CMS). The higher-order Craig-Bampton (HCB) method augments the CB basis with residual modes from a Neumann series expansion of the residual flexibility matrix, where HCB-n retains terms up to the n-th order and is reduced back to the CB size via the System Equivalent Reduction Expansion Process (SEREP), achieving improved accuracy at the same model dimension. However, assessing the accuracy of a reduced model without solving the full-order problem remains a fundamental challenge: if the full-order solution is required to evaluate the error, the purpose of model reduction is defeated. Despite the demonstrated superiority of the HCB method, no a priori error estimator (one that predicts eigenvalue errors without solving the full-order eigenvalue problem) has been proposed for it. The present work addresses this gap with a hierarchical estimation framework that exploits the nested Ritz subspace structure of the HCB method, where each higher-order solution serves as a reference for estimating the error of the preceding order. The framework provides (i) a generalized CB error estimator derived from a Rayleigh quotient perturbation analysis, and (ii) a novel HCB-1 error estimator using the HCB-2 eigensolution as a reference. Numerical examples across models of varying geometric complexity validate both estimators.

</details>


<div id='physics.atom-ph'></div>

# physics.atom-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Large anomalous shifts of potassium-39 Feshbach resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05512)
*Ali Zaheer,Mohammed Bouras,Krzysztof Giergiel,Rudolf Grimm,Andrei Sidorov,Peter Hannaford*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了钾-39原子在1063.9 nm光学偶极阱（optical dipole trap, ODT）中观测到的两个Feshbach共振（Feshbach resonance）位置发生高达+7.5 G的反常频移，其根源在于Feshbach分子具有异常大的动态极化率（dynamic polarizability），约为两个入射原子极化率之和的四到七倍。该大极化率归因于ODT激光频率与分子从最低三重态a3Σ+u势的最后一个振动能级到激发态b3Σ+g势某一振动能级的跃迁频率近乎重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feshbach共振在超冷原子物理中具有核心地位，通常用于调节原子间相互作用强度。然而，光学束缚势会导致共振位置发生频移，理解并控制这类频移对精密操控超冷原子系统至关重要。本研究旨在揭示ODT中Feshbach共振大频移的物理起源。

Method: 实验上，作者在1063.9 nm波长、阱深约136 μK的ODT中束缚钾-39原子，通过射频或磁场扫描方法测量33.6 G和39.9 G两个Feshbach共振的位置，并系统地改变ODT阱深以降温，考察共振频移随阱深的变化规律。从频移数据中提取Feshbach分子的动态极化率，并与两个入射原子极化率之和进行比较。

Result: 观测到33.6 G和39.9 G两个Feshbach共振位置发生最大达+7.5 G的反常频移；频移随ODT阱深线性减小，在零阱深处趋于零。提取得到的Feshbach分子动态极化率约为两个入射钾-39原子极化率之和的四到七倍，远超通常弱束缚Feshbach分子的极化率大小。其他已研究的钾-39 Feshbach共振则表现出零或极小的频移。

Conclusion: 大频移源于Feshbach分子与入射原子对之间显著的交流斯塔克频移（ac Stark shift）差异，其物理机制是1063.9 nm ODT激光频率与a3Σ+u到b3Σ+g分子跃迁频率存在近巧合共振。该发现表明，在选择ODT激光波长以操控Feshbach共振时，需考虑激光频率与分子跃迁频率的耦合效应。

Abstract: We report the observation of large anomalous shifts, up to +7.5 G, of the positions of the 33.6 G and 39.9 G Feshbach resonances in potassium-39 atoms confined in a 1063.9 nm optical dipole trap (ODT) at temperatures up to around 35 μK and trap depths up to about 136 μK. When the atom cloud is cooled to lower temperatures, by reducing the trap depth of the ODT, the shifts decrease proportionally with trap depth and approach zero at zero depth. We show that the large observed shifts originate from a large differential ac Stark shift between the incoming pair of potassium-39 atoms and the weakly bound Feshbach molecule, which in turn originates from an unexpectedly large dynamic polarizability of the Feshbach molecule. The polarizabilities of the Feshbach molecules extracted from the measured shifts of the 33.6 G and 39.9 G resonances are about four to seven times the sum of the polarizabilities of the two incoming potassium-39 atoms, that is, about four to seven times larger than the usual polarizability of weakly bound Feshbach molecules. The large polarizabilities of the Feshbach molecules are attributed to a near-coincidence between the frequency of the 1063.9 nm ODT laser and the frequency of a molecular transition from the last vibrational level of the lowest triplet a3Σ+u potential to a vibrational level of the excited b3Σ+g potential. Other potassium-39 Feshbach resonances we have studied exhibit a zero or very small shift, corresponding to molecular polarizabilities close to the sum of the polarizabilities of the two incoming potassium-39 atoms.
